# Tasks

Milestones in order (spec "Milestones and cut list", plus nate's 2026-11-16 anime additions). Each one is
code-complete when lint is clean, tests pass and the place builds; the "done when" checks that need people,
phones or Studio go on the Studio test list below.

| # | Milestone | Scope | Done when (spec) | State |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M1 | Move and fight slice | Leap, Glide, Flight with touch, PC and gamepad controls; Volt basic + 2 skills; clash hit feel; Spark Drone; greybox Downtown; 30-bot load test; Comic and Anime effect styles | 5 first-timers stop the first machine in 30 s with no help; 4 of 5 say flight and hits feel good on a phone; 30+ fps on a mid-range phone | Code-complete 2026-11-16 |
| M2 | Classes, abilities, enemies | 4 classes with passives, 5 sets (Volt, Frost, Titan, Pulse, Blade) with 40 abilities in both styles, the super slot, Awakening, ranks, mods, power budget, 6 enemies, data tables | Every class solos a contract and has a clear role in a 4-player test; no loadout exceeds the budget | Code-complete 2026-11-16 |
| M3 | Gear, loot, saves | 5 tiers, 8 slots, stats, drops and pity, salvage, upgrade, style collection, profile and migrations, levels and Renown | Drops match the table; a tester wears any look over any gear; rejoin restores everything | Code-complete 2026-11-16 |
| M4 | World generator, 30-player gate | Hub districts, seeded outskirts (2 zone types), weekly seed, streaming, presets, landmarks, safehouses, beacons, age questionnaire | Same seed gives the same hash on two servers; 30+ fps flying across the map on a phone; 30 bots hold the server frame | Code-complete 2026-11-16 (age questionnaire is nate's, on the Launch Checklist) |
| M5 | Destruction v1 | Chunker, 220 hub buildings with 3 states, debris, craters, vehicle flips, repair drones, citizens | 10 players wreck a block for 10 minutes with no server spike and 30+ fps on a phone; everything repairs | Code-complete 2026-11-16 |
| M6 | Incidents, contracts, events, boss | Incident spawner with 10 templates, 6 contract types, 6 random events, world boss with 2 bosses, contribution rewards | First boss within 8 minutes; boss rewards by tier; no idle wait over 5 s | Code-complete 2026-11-16 |
| M7 | Opening, Act 1, missions | Cinematic, playable disaster, choice, 6 missions in sky pockets, 3 choices, 8 side quests, journal | 5 new testers finish the opening and suit up with no help; both routes reach the finale | Code-complete 2026-11-16 |
| M8 | PvP, death, anti-exploit | Flag, Crater, Downed and Defeated, Fame and Infamy, rules table, strikes | The 30-minute exploit test finds nothing; PvP rules checklist passes | Code-complete 2026-11-16 |
| M9 | Creator, HQ, shop, passes | Creator with the 116 spec pieces plus the anime pieces and the Comic/Anime pick, 2 HQs, Gizmo's shop, Robux shop, passes, slots, Legacy | A tester makes a hero in under 3 minutes; test purchases grant once; looks show to others | Code-complete 2026-11-16 (economy sim gate met for the median level; see Decisions) |

| M11 | Juice, audio, wait fillers | Juice table, sounds, music, offline drones, downed polish, friend boost, Photo Mode | No idle wait over 5 s in a 30-minute session with 8 testers; every action has a sound and visual | Code-complete 2026-11-16 (the 8-tester gate and real sound ids are on the Studio list and Launch Checklist) |

## Studio list: Spider-Man swinging (2026-11-19, pt-swing)
Pick the Swing mobility (creator or a safehouse). Preview of the maths over a real Downtown street: art/preview/swing_path.png.
1. Downtown street, PC: jump, hold F looking down the street: a white energy cord (two strands twisting, crawling toward the anchor; texture since 2026-11-20) shoots out (a quick whip) from the right hand to a building face or a roof corner ahead and above, with a thwip. You drop into the arc, speed builds at the bottom, you rise up the far side; the swing runs along the street and never drags you across into the side buildings.
2. Let go of F on the way up: you fling forward and up with a whoosh (louder on the rising half). Hold F again as you start to fall: the next line catches with no loss of speed. Chain 6+ lines down a long street at about 60 studs/s with no MoveCorrect pull-back (Dev panel shows none).
3. Hold F the whole time: at the top of each arc it lets go by itself and the next line fires as you fall (hands-free chaining).
4. Look at open sky (the harbour, over the plaza) and press F: the line flicks out and drops away with a dull click, no swing, nothing hangs from the sky. Robots, players, vehicles and ghost signs never hold a line.
5. While swinging press Space (Leap): you zip up the line; on a roof edge you pop up onto the roof, on a wall you pop up its face; the pop flings you on. On the ground, F still zips to the spot aimed at.
6. Camera: the FOV widens and the camera eases back a few studs at speed; wind lines and the wind sound come in past half speed. Settings Camera motion Reduced: half; Off: no FOV or pull-back from the swing. The camera never pulls back through a wall.
7. Swing into a wall: you push off it, keeping the speed along it, never stuck. A building piece the line hangs from breaks off: the line drops and you fling on.
8. Gamepad: hold Y to swing, A to zip; phone: hold FLY (SWING / LET GO), LEAP shows ZIP while swinging; both chain the same way.
9. Second client: you see the other hero's line from their hand to the anchor (shoot-out too); on a phone (low preset) at most 3 other lines, 6 on medium, 8 on high, within 350 studs.
10. Low preset phone: FrameProf SwingFx under 0.2 ms, MoveController unchanged from before.

## Store art picks (2026-11-17)
Rendered with our Blender pipeline from notes/Store Page.md (scripts art/store/*.py; regenerate all with
`bash art/store/make.sh`, or one with `bash art/store/make.sh <clash|smash|build|boss|twist>`). nate's picks
(2026-11-17): the flying icon and thumbnail were dropped as too basic; the hero keeps his colours.
- Icons (512 x 512, no text): `icon_clash.png` (Anime hero with a gold aura against a Spark Trooper, shockwave
  ring), `icon_smash.png` (Comic hero punching a Spark Trooper through a cracking tower wall).
- Thumbnails (1920 x 1080): `thumb_build.png` ("HERO OR VILLAIN?": Bruiser, Blaster, Speedster and Controller,
  two Comic and two Anime, with Titan, Volt, Pulse plus a Blade, and Frost; the Controller in the horned villain
  look), `thumb_boss.png` ("GIANT WORLD BOSS": ten heroes around Rustjaw Prime with the boss bar and fireworks),
  `thumb_twist.png` ("TRUST NO HERO": the Comic hero's punch landing on Radiant, the golden famous fake, its face
  cracked half open to the metal and a red eye; white flash and sparks; Downtown by day with a corner of the
  Crane Industries billboard, its words drawn as bars).
- Files: art/export/store/*.png (also copied to the project files, capeline/art/store/); contact sheet
  art/preview/store_picks.png (project files: capeline/art/store/picks.png) shows each icon at 150 and 50 px as
  a phone shows it.

What nate does:
1. Look at art/preview/store_picks.png and the full-size files (or ask for changes).
2. Run the Playtest Kit check #42 (name and look) on the picks.
3. Upload them himself in the Creator Dashboard (Places > Configure: icon and thumbnails). Nothing was uploaded.
4. A/B test them with the Dashboard's icon and thumbnail tests and the launch ads; keep the best click-through.
5. Re-render with the final art before the ads: thumb_boss once the bosses' lore look is final
   (`bash art/store/make.sh boss`), thumb_twist once Radiant's cracked face decal and look are final in game
   (`bash art/store/make.sh twist`).

## Store art refresh (2026-11-18)
A second set with the newer villains and bosses (notes/Store Page.md "Store art refresh"; scripts art/store/store2.py,
cast.py, post2.py; regenerate with `bash art/store/make2.sh [name]`, renders under nice). Icons `icon_maw.png`
(recommended), `icon_fake.png`, `icon_titan.png`; thumbnails `thumb_maw.png`, `thumb_deep.png`, `thumb_fakes.png`,
`thumb_giant.png` in art/export/store2; previews art/export/store2/preview_sheet.png and preview_small.png
(also art/preview/store2_sheet.png).

What nate does:
1. Look at art/export/store2/preview_sheet.png and preview_small.png (or ask for changes).
2. Import art/export/finales/*.fbx and art/export/Bosses.fbx first if not done, so the images match the game; if
   the bosses' or finales' looks change, re-render (`bash art/store/make2.sh`).
3. Run the Playtest Kit check #42 (name and look) on the ones you pick.
4. Upload them yourself (Creator Dashboard); pick 2 or 3 icons or thumbnails per test and keep the best CTR
   (aim 2.5%). Nothing was uploaded.

## Studio test list (needs nate's PC)

### Living world: meshes over the plain shapes and the ambient life (2026-11-19, living-world)
Lune proves the looks, picks, shop and vending layout, lanes, lights, brakes, corners, manholes, flags, reactions and budgets (ONLY="Living world"; art/preview/life.png, life_street.png, life_textures.png, lorebooks.png, city_audit.txt). These need Studio:
1. Outskirts: trees have real crowns (round, tall, pine; some autumn and blossom), trunks, crop and corn rows, round hay bales, silo bodies and domes, panelled house doors with knobs, all facing right (no backwards pieces) and colliding like before (walk into a tree trunk, stand on a hay bale). Break a farm building: its door and silo meshes go with it.
2. Picket fences: white pickets with see-through gaps on both sides of every fence, not stretched; knock one loose: the flung pieces keep the pickets.
3. Fields: the tilled soil texture tiles, not stretched.
4. Downtown and Uptown: a shop front under every awning and on some bare faces, the sign word readable from the street (not mirrored), glass see-through, the back lit warm after dusk (ClockTime 18 to 6.3). Vending machines on the sidewalks; smash one: cans spill and bounce, then fade.
5. Traffic (medium preset): cars drive on the right, stop at red lights and for a hero standing in the lane, honk now and then, never through buildings or the tram lines; a big hit near them flings them and they fade. Low preset: 4 cars at most. Check the phone frame time (FrameProf "CityLife").
6. Pigeons on sidewalk corners peck and scatter up and away when you land or fly by fast, then come back.
7. Steam from manholes; a hit near one blows the cover off, it comes back. Roof vents steam.
8. Roof flags wave on some roofs (district flags, not mirrored, segments joined); a broken building's flag goes.
9. News drones orbit near a fight on medium and high, none on low.
10. Hydrants knocked loose spray a geyser; bins and news boxes scatter paper.
11. Citizens near you point when you fly or run by, hold up a phone (a white flash) when you stand near, cheer just after a big hit.
12. Lore pages: the open book wears the sculpted cover, pages, spine, clasp, ribbon and the faceted star (no part boxes left but the text lines and the halo); the HQ Lore Shelf has a panelled back, scrolled sides and round-spined books. Remove ReplicatedStorage.Art.LoreBook and rejoin: the part book comes back, no errors.
13. Remove ReplicatedStorage.Art.Life and rejoin: the city is today's boxes again, no errors.

### Act 1 choices: one in every mission (2026-11-19, act1-choices)
Lune proves the data, the picks, the save and the line rules (ONLY=Act1Choices); these need Studio:
1. New Vigil hero, prologue: at the spark point the shield pops over the kid, then 1.2 s later the moment freezes: letterbox, the crowd stands still, the world greys a little, the camera eases past the hero toward the kid, and two big buttons show above the line box: PULL THE KID CLEAR / CATCH THE NEWS CREW (no SKIP). The hero cannot move. A pick: Crane's line ("That rogue just grabbed a child!..." or "Cut the cameras!..."), the title or Credits toast, the colour comes back, the crowd runs on, and the leap starts about 3.5 s later. The rooftop QTEs play as before.
2. Same as a Syndicate villain: the shover flies, then GRAB THE MEDAL / GRAB THE KEYCARD; Crane's medal or keycard line.
3. Wait 20 s without picking: the card goes, the prologue goes on; the Journal's Ceremony row reads "not yet"; REPLAY asks again. A REPLAY after a pick does not ask (no second reward).
4. Safehouse as the Vigil: the offer scene ends with Lodestar asking your real name; the choice (TELL YOUR REAL NAME / KEEP IT SECRET), then a 13 s scene: Lodestar answers your pick, Patch asks about the torn photo by the door and his old students, Lodestar dodges. The torn photo (a crate, a brass frame, half a print, the other half on the floor, right of the door) is in shot.
5. Safehouse as the Syndicate: the Chairman (radio, his rendered masked portrait: face plate, wide brim) makes the offer; TAKE THE DEAL / DEMAND A BIGGER CUT; he answers the pick; Patch warns you off the photo.
6. Live (both sides): after Bulwark's battle form falls, the choice (SHOW YOUR FACE / KEEP THE MASK ON, or THREATEN CRANE / SELL THE FOOTAGE), then Patch answers the pick and Crane on air: "Ask your heroes why the Vigil buries its mistakes." Then the ending broadcast as before.
7. A mixed party in Safehouse: each side's lead gets their own card at the same time; the others see "Cover the..." wait text; the after scene plays for everyone (a member who did not pick hears only the shared lines).
8. Journal STORY tab: YOUR CHOICES lists all six missions with the pick in your side's words (or "not yet"); an old save shows its three old picks unchanged and "not yet" for The Ceremony, Safehouse and Live.
9. The titles show in the title picker after their pick (First Save, Medal Snatcher, True Name, Chairman's Pick, Face of Hope, Prime Time Threat).

3. Creator: HEAD > Facial hair is back with 8 catalog items (✂ tiles; the card reads "8 Roblox catalog items, free"), no colour squares; NONE takes it off; each beard sits on the chin and moves with the head in the preview and in game (the Fancy Moustache under the nose). A hat that hides the face hides the beard too. Randomize sometimes adds one.

5. The bearded cast wear their catalog beard in the world: Lodestar (black), Captain Brine and the Harvest King (blonde), Janitor Gus and Old Beacon (brown), the Tidewright, the Toymaker and Master Hoshi (white), Brick and Clawback (black trimmed), Catchpole (stubble), the Pitboss, Morrow and Ambrose (fancy moustache), Sal Scorch (red moustache). A giver or mentor built before the beards load gets it a moment later. Nobody wears our own drawn beard blocks.

### News screens and name card art (2026-11-19, gap-news)
Looks only: where the screens hang, what they show, the card's timing and the TV's timing are as before. The plain grey frame and legs are gone (hidden), nothing new is solid: walk and fly through where the legs were exactly as before.
2. The three Downtown tower screens have a chunky navy bezel with a gold rule, a bulb marquee across the top with the mark and the on-air lamp, two lamp arms and a drip trim; nothing pokes into the tower's wall.
3. Uptown's landmark billboard has the heavy bezel, the marquee with two marks, five lamp arms and a catwalk with rails and struts.
4. Every big screen's news still reads (band, headline, ticker scroll) and the band starts with the lighthouse mark; collapse a screen's tower: the screen and its look go dark together, and come back when repaired.
5. The corner TV is a red cartoon TV set (antennas, cream bezel, grille, knobs): the band, line and ticker show inside its window and do not spill past it; on a phone it fits above the action buttons and clear of the top row.
6. The boss name cards (each of the four) show the boss's comic portrait popping out of the card's left over a comic navy plate; the name and title sit right of it, the slash bar sweeps in the boss's colour; the plate's ink and gold rule stay crisp at PC and phone sizes.

### Ceremony prologue art (2026-11-19, gap-prologue)
Looks only: every beat's timing, positions, collisions and the guided route are as before. Previews: art/preview/prologue.png, prologue_stage.png, prologue_ads.png. DEV > "Prologue: Vigil" (then "Prologue: Syndicate") on PC and the phone emulator.
1. The ceremony: a planked stage with a navy skirt, gold rim and stars, red swags, footlights, two carpeted steps with brass rails, Crane's navy lectern with the gold crest and a microphone, two black truss towers with spot cans aimed at Crane and soft light beams (no beams on the phone preset), six crowd barriers either side of the steps (walk through them: they never collide). Walk on the deck and the steps: you stand on the planks, not floating or sunk.
2. The Hall's banner shows the printed CRANE'S HERO HALL / CRANE INDUSTRIES picture inside a gold rod frame with pennants, fringe and a star medallion.
3. The scanner drone is a white drone with four red-ringed rotor ducts and a red lens; it bobs as before. The scan: a red fan of light from its lens sweeps a red line across the crowd (sparks on the line), slows, and locks on you: a red ring and a turning rune under your feet. Reduce flashing: the ring holds still.
4. Vigil spark: the donut shop sign (both faces) shakes over the kid; on Basic the shield bubble pops open (a shimmering shell, hex plates, a ground ring), the sign hits it (sparks, a ripple) and bounces off; the bubble fades in wisps. Reduce flashing: no ripple.
5. The subway: each slab falls as a broken tiled ceiling slab with rebar and lands as a rubble heap; it still shuts the way back only behind you, and Leap clears it.
6. The title: the billboard shows the printed Crane Industries ad in a dark frame with lamps and a catwalk; a giant Silas Crane (his own rig: the same catalog face and hair as on the stage) stands on the catwalk on the ad's left, turned toward the words with an arm out. Check his feet are on the catwalk and nothing pokes into the building.
7. Output: no errors from PrologueFx or PrologueMeshes; the Studio Explorer shows ReplicatedStorage.Art.Prologue with SurfaceAppearances on the textured meshes.
### Hideout art pass: the HQ room shells and textures (2026-11-19, branch gap-hideout)
The Safehouse, the Foundry and the plaza stairwell head in textured Blender modules (art/blender/hideout.py, hbake.py; Art.Hideout and a re-uploaded Art.Hq; previews art/preview/hideout.png and hq.png; tests: ONLY="Hideout art").
1. As a Vigil hero, walk to the plaza's west edge: the stairwell head is a concrete kiosk with a roof slab, a vent, a riveted steel service door with a push bar, a blank enamel plate and a caged lamp. You still bump into its walls and roof; the door's prompt reads the Safehouse (a Syndicate villain sees "Service Door, Locked"); the Vigil marker still floats over it.
2. Go down: the walls are board-formed concrete with tie holes, the pillars and pilasters have hazard-striped feet, the beams and ceiling are concrete, the floor is a slab with saw cuts under the side's rug. Pipes, cable trays with cables, caged work lamps and the hazard stripe run along every wall; the stair has hazard nosings and a handrail; the map wall has a steel frame and a light bar; the monitors sit on wall arms. Lamps, the map's districts and the monitor screens still glow and light the room; nothing blocks a station, prompt or sign.
3. Every station, the Trophy Wall, WANTED wall, props and the rank dressing now show texture (wood grain, cork, fabric, brushed and diamond plate metal, gold); the plaques still change colour when filled and the name plates with their state.
4. As a Syndicate villain in the Foundry: riveted iron wall bays with I-beams and ledges round the red light band, diamond plate tiles round the edge and a red rug (still the side's colour).
5. Dev "Rep tier: set 3" as a villain: the captured Crane robot hangs on the hook as a Roblox-body figure in the Spark Trooper uniform with a catalog face and hair, limp, a strap to the hook (no navy boxes or C bars left showing).
6. Phone (Settings quality low, or a phone): the ceiling tiles and beams stay plain parts, the rest is dressed; frame time in each HQ about what it was. Medium and high draw everything.
7. Mission 2 (the Safehouse tunnels): the long walls wear the same pipes, trays and caged lamps; robots and players move as before (the kit is walk-through decoration).
8. Remove ReplicatedStorage.Art.Hideout and rejoin: every shell is today's plain parts again, no errors.

### Playtest art2: the first-session list (2026-11-18)
The first session on the overnight build. Use the playtest-art5 place above (the art package is done from the cloud, so skip step 13 and ignore START HERE.txt); steps 1 to 12 still apply, and step 1 now reads "playtest-art5 (2026-11-18)". About 45 minutes; each step names the full list it samples.

3. Play the prologue to the title: the 6-roof chase, a missed hop puts you back on the last roof in about 1.2 s ("Onboarding" 5).

9. HQ: go down the stairwell at the plaza's west edge; stations, Trophy Wall, WANTED wall and dummies are there and usable ("HQ meshes", "WP9").
10. Journal: the Rogues File tab shows a card per page you have met ("Rogues File portraits", "Rogues File and Journal tabs").
11. Side quests: start The Hero Academy and The Gala Heist (Dev jump to step 1 is fine); the givers wear their own outfits and the gala set stands in the OpenNorth square ("Side quest people", "Set piece meshes" 3).
12. Phone: Studio device emulator, a small phone, Low preset: buttons fit, the city runs smoothly, frame time in the Dev panel close to PC Low ("Phone pass", "Perf fixes").
13. After the art package (Part B), repeat 5, 6, 9 and 11: each look is now its Blender mesh, never half a look, with no errors in the Output.

### Rogues File portraits (2026-11-18, art pass)
Each of the Rogues File's 45 pages gets a comic case-file portrait card drawn from the game's own look (a bold ink outline, a flat bright backdrop per group: robots sky blue, famous fakes pink, villains violet, world bosses orange; rays and halftone dots; a black frame with a folded corner), and the 3 world bosses a gold card for their gold page. Contact sheet: art/preview/rogues.png (each card large and at the Journal's 56 px). Tests: ONLY="Rogues File". Until the upload nothing changes in game (every page keeps its Kit shapes).
1. Make the upload package: `python3 art/upload/make_package.py <folder>`. It now also holds the 48 cards (Rogue_<Id>.png and Rogue_<Boss>_Gold.png, 512 x 512, target rogue_portrait). Copy the folder to Documents (a fresh folder), double-click upload.bat, paste resolve_ids.txt into Studio's Command Bar, save Workspace > CapelineImageIds as image_ids.txt, and attach ids.json and image_ids.txt in the chat. Claude runs art/upload/apply_ids.py, which fills Config.Rogues.portraits.
2. With the ids in: a fresh account (Dev Wipe), press J, ROGUES. Every page is still the black silhouette with "???" and its hint (no card shows before a page is beaten).
3. Beat a Crane Trooper: its page shows its card (the blue jet suit with the lightning emblem, glowing robot eyes, on sky blue) in the 56 px box at the row's left, with nothing cut off or stretched; the name, blurb and count sit where they were.
4. Dev panel: fill every page (or beat one of each kind you can reach). Scroll the whole book: every card matches its page's name (robots in their uniforms and gear: the Sentry's arm cannon, the Warden's guard, the diving helmets, the riot shield, the Captain's cap, the Sniper's rifle and visor; the fakes and villains in their own outfits, faces, hats and capes; the Bramblebeast, the Voidhound and the Toymaker's Wind-Up Giant as their finale models; the three bosses whole).
5. Beat Rustjaw Prime at level 40+ as a Contributor or MVP: its row turns gold (GOLD PAGE) and its card swaps to the gold card (gold backdrop, gold frame, two stars). The other bosses' rows keep their plain card until their gold page.
6. iPhone SE and a 640 x 360 Android (Test > Device): every card reads at row size (a face or a shape you can tell apart from its neighbours), and the folded corner shows the row's paper behind it, not a black box.
7. Remove one id (set it back to 0 in Config.Rogues.portraits) and play: that page shows its Kit shapes again with no errors in the output.

As a Vigil hero, go down to the Safehouse: each station (Command, Gizmo's Bench, Mannequins, Training, Trophy Wall, Hall of Legends, Scanner, Exit) has its console on a blue-rimmed pad, facing into the room; the prompts and their signs work where they did. WANTED wall (Dev "Nemesis beaten" a few times): the Vigil's cork board in a wood frame with brass pins and a brass plate, or the Syndicate's steel plate with rivets and red lamps; mounts in the side's style with gold name plates; the heads' and the poster bust's pieces are shaped (rounded skull, faceplate, horns, spikes, visor) in the nemesis's colours. The "?" of an empty mount and the names on the plates still read in front.
7. Props (Dev panel props on, or collect them): every prop from the Cafe sign to the Season One statue stands on its spot, facing into the room (the Hero Pass ones, 19 to 22, now show at all: they had no spot before).
8. Rep tiers (DEV "Rep tier: set 1" to 5): the Safehouse's cork board, notes, rig desk with monitors, server rack, stool, relic case with cape, shield and helmet, spotlight and banner; the Foundry's vault, gold bars, big screen with crown, hook and chain with the captive robot (its C now reads the right way from the room), cash crates, carpet and truss. Each sits where its parts sat; the screens, LEDs and note icons stay glowing parts.
9. Visit a friend's HQ (WP12): their side's look, the same as their own view.
10. Remove one mesh (e.g. HqCommandVBody) from ReplicatedStorage.Art.Hq and rejoin: the Vigil's Command station is its plain post again (never half a look), the rest stays dressed, no errors in the output. Remove Head: the dummies are the bare metal robots again. Remove the whole model: everything is today's parts.

Then A wins a Showdown (DEV > Nemesis 1, then the Showdown): B's copy shows the next nemesis and a new head without leaving.

Copy the folder to Documents\capeline-upload-2 (or a fresh folder), double-click upload.bat, paste resolve_ids.txt into Studio's Command Bar, save Workspace > CapelineImageIds as image_ids.txt, and attach ids.json and image_ids.txt in the chat. It has 57 MeshParts named Cape_<style>_<piece> (Outer, Lining, Yoke, Clasps, Collar, Band, Band2, Stars, BigStars, Mantle, Hood, Feathers, Left, Right). 3. Suits: open the creator and step through every suit with the default colours. Each one shows its drawn pattern (seams, panels, belts, collars and cuffs) in the suit's colours on the torso, arms and legs, with no white or grey shirt showing and no box suit parts doubling up. 5. Masks: wear the domino, half, bandana, cowl, eyepatch, masquerade, ninja and skull masks. Each is drawn on the head's faces over the face (the eyes still show through the domino, half, cowl, masquerade and ninja). The cowl and ninja wrap the sides, back and top with no seams that jump at the edges; the left and right sides match. Take a mask off: the decals go with it. The visor and the helmets still use their parts.
6. Capes: wear all 12 capes on a Medium and a Large build. Each hangs from the shoulders behind the back, flares toward the hem and clears the legs while standing and running. The yoke strips sit over the shoulders and end in clasps on the chest. The outside is the slot's main colour and the lining is its trim. Check the extras: comet's two glowing bands, mantle's fur ring, hood's lowered hood, high collar's standing collar, feather's two rows of feathers, star's glowing stars on dark cloth, night's gold hem and sparkles, split's two panels, tattered's jagged hem. Fly and glide: nothing pokes through the arms badly, and nothing collides or blocks clicks.
7. Remove Art.Capes in a test session: the capes go back to the box capes with no errors in the output.
8. A phone (Studio Device emulator, a small phone): at street distance a hero's suit pattern, emblem and cape style are told apart.

7. Remove one mesh from Art.Hats (e.g. Hat_crown_Crown): the crown goes back to its parts, the other hats keep their meshes, no errors in the output. Do the same with one Hair mesh: that style goes back to its parts.
8. A phone (Studio Device emulator, a small phone): at street distance a hero's belt, shoulders, back bling, hat and hair are told apart, and the eyes still read as glowing.

### Onboarding: the first 60 seconds (2026-11-18)
Fixes from the onboarding check (tests: ONLY="Onboarding"; ONBOARD_PRINT=1 prints the timeline). Use a fresh account or Dev Wipe, on PC, a phone (device emulator) and a controller.
1. Join as a new player: the loading screen reads LOADING THE CITY, then LOADING YOUR HERO, and goes away about a second before the creator opens (no bare HUD in between). On a controller the creator's first button is selected; press Select and it goes back into the creator, not the top row.
2. Make the hero and press DONE: the ceremony is about 22 s (was 30), every Crane line is readable, and the scan's two lines and SPARK DETECTED fit its 7.5 s. Control comes about 30 s after DONE.
3. Run beat: the gold marker reads RUN; spark beat SPARK (press the attack button or wait 6 s); leap LEAP; chase LEAP; descent SUBWAY; tunnel RUN. No word on the camera beats.
4. Leap at once in the leap beat: Patch's "Hey! You, the one glowing..." and "I'm Patch. I hacked your phone..." both still play (at the start of the chase), and the chase's own first line follows them. The suit's "Whoa..." line plays again at the start of the descent.
5. In the chase, miss a hop on purpose and land in the street: within about 1.2 s you are back on the last roof you reached, facing the next one. Reset (Esc > Reset) mid-chase: you respawn on the last roof reached, not the second one.
6. Two players: start a prologue on one; on the other, hit the rooftop row's buildings (east of the Hall) with supers while the first is on the leap, chase or suit beats: nothing breaks. Once that prologue goes down to the subway (or finishes), the same hits break them again.
7. At the title: the mentor picker does not pop over the title card. Back in the plaza: the DAILY card shows about 4 s later; the mentor picker comes only after you close the daily card (one card at a time).
8. Rejoin in the middle of the prologue: it starts again from the ceremony with no PLAY / SHOP / SETTINGS menu over it. On an account with another finished hero, make a new hero, leave mid-prologue and rejoin: CREATE FIRST and PLAY THE PROLOGUE show; leave the menu alone for 2 minutes and the hero goes to the city with the prologue's rewards (level 3, Flight, the Skill Point).
9. Controller: on the main menu the first button is selected; on a camera beat you have seen before, SKIP reads SKIP (B) and B skips it (the rewards still land at the title).
10. A top-10 player from last week joining: the TOP 10 card waits until the main menu is closed and the daily card is dealt with, then shows.
11. All subway lanes busy (Dev: start 7 prologues with bots, or set Config.Story.opening.subway.lanes = 0 in a test place): the fight happens at the subway steps, not back in the plaza.
12. Analytics output: first_spark's value is the seconds since the prologue started (about 35), not since joining.

### Travel audit: underwater, vehicles, citizens, travel (2026-11-18)
Fixes from the overnight audit (tests: ONLY="Travel audit"). Use the Dev panel and Studio's network emulation.
1. Fast travel to each of the 12 beacons (MAP): you land beside the post on its roof, standing, never inside the post, a wall or the roof; walk off and nothing snags.
2. Studio > Network: set 1500 ms incoming lag and fast travel: you may see the old spot for a moment, then you are at the beacon and stay there (no snap back to where you left, no strike line in the server output). Same for a respawn at a far safehouse.
3. Start a fast travel and, during the 3 s flight, enter the HQ stairwell or a mission (Dev panel Mission): you stay in the HQ or pocket and the camera comes back.
4. Pick up a Sunken Line commuter, then fast travel: the commuter goes back to the train car ("TRAPPED!" again); swim one to the quay instead and you are paid as before.
5. Old Town, Clock Square: watch citizens for a minute: nobody walks through the Clock Tower, and nobody walks through a safehouse hut. Trigger a collapse near citizens (Dev panel): they run along the sidewalks to a safehouse, or vanish into a doorway; none cut through a block.
6. Flip cars with a heavy hit from each side, the kerb side especially: a car pushed toward the buildings rolls over on the spot and lands upside down on the road, never in a building or on the sidewalk; trams and the Docks trucks too.
7. Flip a car, then stand where it was parked for 40 s: it does not come back on top of you; step away and it resets within 2 s.
8. Trench: find all 3 sunken caches near the factory; the one west of the hall now sits against its wall and opens when you swim close.
9. Underwater, Lift and Throw a Diver or an Anchor onto the beach: it snaps back into the water and never follows you up the street. Swim near a Torpedo Sentry, then leap out of the sea: the torpedo pops at the surface on your screen and you are not hit.
### WP12: visit a friend's HQ (2026-11-18)
A read-only copy of a friend's HQ on the same server (tests: ONLY="WP12"). In Studio: Test tab > Clients and Servers, 2 players (3 for steps 8 and 9); call them A (the owner) and B (the visitor). Give A some HQ to show first: Dev panel "Level 50", a few HQ levels, trophies picked on the Trophy Wall, "Nemesis Intel" and "Nemesis beaten" once.
1. B opens SQUAD (top row): A is listed under IN THIS SERVER with INVITE and VISIT under the name (squadmates show VISIT too). The note at the bottom explains visits. Tap VISIT: B reads "Visit request sent to <A>".
2. A sees a card "<B> wants to visit your HQ" with ACCEPT and NO and a gold bar running down for 30 s. Let it run out: the card goes; nothing happens to B. B taps VISIT again: works.
3. A taps NO: B reads "<A> can't have visitors right now". B taps VISIT at once: "Give them a moment before asking again" (60 s).
4. After a minute B asks again and A taps ACCEPT, wherever A is (in the city, in A's own HQ, in a fight). B whooshes into A's HQ (no loading screen, same server) and reads "Visiting <A>'s HQ. Look around, LEAVE takes you back"; A reads "<B> is visiting your HQ". B sees A's own side's HQ (the Safehouse or the Foundry), the title sign "<A>'s HQ, level N", A's trophy wall picks, A's statues, A's thank-you and collection props, A's reputation dressing, and A's WANTED poster and nemesis heads exactly as A sees them in A's HQ. No Lodestar in the copy.
5. As B, walk to each station pad: the signs read "Owner only" and there are no prompts; the WANTED poster has no Read prompt; the dummies stand but skills never aim at them; there is no boss sim. Only the Exit pad works ("Leave" on B). The Robux shop, Gizmo, creator and loadout swaps all refuse. No robots, incidents, events or drops come near.
6. While B is inside, A changes trophy picks or upgrades the HQ: B's copy redraws within a moment. A switches hero slot: B sees the new hero's HQ.
7. B taps LEAVE HQ (gold, in the top row; also a row on top of SQUAD) or uses the Exit pad: B is back exactly where B stood when A accepted (outside B's own HQ door if B was in it); A reads "<B> left your HQ". Reset while inside: B respawns inside the copy.
8. With 3 players: A's SQUAD panel shows VISITING YOUR HQ with each visitor and KICK. KICK one: they read "Your visit to <A>'s HQ is over" and are back where they were; asking A again says to wait (5 min). A visitor count past 4 reads "Their HQ is full (4 visitors)".
9. Mutes and blocks: A mutes B (B's plate > MUTE): B's VISIT says "sent" but A never gets a card; if B is inside, B is sent home within a second. B mutes A: VISIT says "Unmute or unblock them first". Repeat with Roblox's own Block (the player list's Block): same results (the device sends its block list, GuardBlocks). A Report from A also stops B's asks to A.
10. A leaves the server while B is inside: B reads "<A> left the server. Back you go" and is back where B was. Two hosts at once (3 players: B visits A while C visits B): C is in a copy of B's HQ in its own pocket, and B and C never see each other.
11. Analytics (Studio output with the analytics debug on): one hq_visit per visit (owner's side, same or cross, visitors), never more than one a minute per visitor.
12. Phone (iPhone SE and a 640 x 360 Android in the Device Emulator): the visit card fits under the top bar with ACCEPT and NO tappable; SQUAD's two-button rows (INVITE and VISIT, KICK and VISIT) show the full name over the buttons; LEAVE HQ stays in the top row (never in MORE).
### Side quest juice and beacon roofs (2026-11-18)
Every side quest moment now has a sound and a stamp or burst (tests: ONLY="SQ juice" and ONLY="Beacon roofs"). The sounds are placeholder clips. Turn on Config.Dev.sideQuestSample (or use the Dev panel's side quest buttons) to run chains fast.
1. Take any chain from its giver: a swoosh, the NEW QUEST! stamp with the chain's name, a gold ring and a little confetti (the top toast still says NEW SIDE QUEST).
2. Finish a step of each kind you can reach (the Dev panel's kind tests run every kind alone): a chime and the step's word stamp (TALKED!, CLEARED!, CAUGHT!, HELD!, FINISHED!, SNAPPED!, LIT UP! and the rest) with STEP n OF m under it, a green ring round you and a +Credits pop. The kind tests' stamps say KIND TEST under the word.
3. In a squad with one hero and one villain on a shared chain, finish your side first: a soft chime, a small ring and the word pop over your head (no stamp); the big stamp comes when the other side finishes.
4. A pocket step (a run in a mission pocket): the result card reads the chain's name then CLEAR! with the pay, not a blank mission name or a replay line.
5. Walk toward a finale's villain: its name card slams in with a low sting. In The Gala Heist's finale, beat the first villain: a whoosh and the ENCORE! stamp with the second villain's name, a ring where it lands.
6. Get Downed by a finale villain: TRY AGAIN! with the villain's name, over the Downed screen's heartbeat. It does not show when Downed anywhere else.
7. Win a finale: fireworks over where the villain fell and VICTORY! with "<NAME> DEFEATED", then about 2 s later the result card: "<CHAIN> COMPLETE!", the pay counting up, NEW TITLE and NEW STYLE lines with their names (e.g. CLASS DISMISSED, CRACKED HALO), confetti and a gold ring. With reduce flashing on there is no confetti and the stamps do not spin.
8. Beacon 2 (Downtown East, on the tall roof near -150, 150): hit its building with supers and the Dev structure hit: nothing breaks, the beacon pad stays on the roof. Its neighbours still break.
9. Start a rooftop race or chase (Rooftop Racers, the Gala Heist's getaways) and hit a course roof with supers while it runs: it holds. After the step ends, the same roof breaks again. A "reach the roof" or "hold the roof" step never sits on a broken building and its roof holds while the step is on.

### City and destruction audit (2026-11-18)
Fixes from the city audit (tests: ONLY="City audit"). Use the Dev panel's structure hit and "Repair all" where it helps.
1. Hit a building once with a skill so it turns Damaged (a hole shows). Stand back and fire a bolt (or Zap Bolt) at a wall panel that still stands: the shot stops on that panel and knocks pieces out of it. Fire one through the hole: it flies inside and breaks the back wall. Keep going with bolts only: the building comes down.
2. Leap onto the roof of a Damaged building: the landing breaks roof pieces (the hero lands on the roof, not on the street below). Robots behind a Damaged building's standing walls are not hit through them; through a hole they are.
3. Cast a damaging field (a super like Blizzard Dome or Sunfall, or a skill like Static Field) against a building: pieces break at once where it lands. A healing field (Healing Rain) breaks nothing.
4. Fly up to a skyscraper with a spire (the 400 to 640 stud towers) and hit the spire or setbacks with supers: the shaft's top storey breaks and the crown disappears with it.
5. Bring down 4 buildings within a few seconds (Dev structure hit at each one's base): 3 collapse, the 4th stays standing with a broken ground floor, then comes down by itself about 6 s after the first; never more than 3 falling at once.
6. Two players: player 2 watches a Damaged building while a drone repairs it; the moment it pops back whole, player 1 hits it once. Both screens show the same single new hole (no old holes come back). Player 2 rejoins: the same hole.
7. On a phone (low preset), knock the middle storeys out of several tall towers within a few seconds: frame rate stays playable; some falling pieces just vanish in the dust. On a PC (high) the same scene shows every piece falling.
8. Change the graphics preset in Settings while flying fast over Downtown: street props rebuild for the new preset with no leftovers (no high-tier props stay on low). Fly away from a Damaged building and back a few times: no errors in the Developer Console, and the stand-in redraws each time.
### Audit: analytics and funnels (2026-11-18)
The funnels now log each step once and in order, the budget is a sliding minute and devices are paced (tests: ONLY="Audit analytics"). Studio prints [Funnel] and [Analytics] lines in the Output.
1. Fresh profile (Dev panel > Wipe profile), play the prologue to the end: the Output shows [Funnel] Opening steps 1 to 7 once each, in order, and no step 9 (level_3) yet even though the prologue paid level 3. Clear your first incident: steps 8 (first_incident) and 9 (level_3) print together, in that order.
2. Cast a few skills: one [Funnel] Combat step 1 skill_cast, never again however many casts. Rank a skill: Combat step 2. Rejoin and cast: nothing more for Combat steps 1 and 2.
3. Turn PvP on: [Funnel] PvP step 1 flag_on. Land a PvP hit on a second flagged test player: step 2 first_pvp_hit once (more hits print nothing). Beat them: step 3 pvp_fight and step 4 pvp_win; the loser's Output shows its own step 3 only if it hit first. Turn PvP off and on again: a new step 1.
4. Open the shop: Purchase step 1 shop_open; tap items: step 2 once. Close and reopen within 5 s: no new step 1 (paced); after 5 s a new one.
5. Rejoin on the same profile (Studio keeps the save in memory, or a published test place): no Opening, Combat, Story or MMO step prints again, and session_start says "returning". (A save that fails to load cannot be forced in Studio, where no API access means memory saves; the Lune tests cover it: only session_device "nosave" and device facts are logged.)
6. Dev panel analytics line ("N sent, M over budget") with 2 players: stays at 0 over budget in normal play.
7. Live dashboard (a day after testers, published): Funnels shows Onboarding, Combat, PvP (flag_on, first_pvp_hit, pvp_fight, pvp_win), Story, Purchase, MMO, Progress and Returns; no step's count is higher than the step before it in Onboarding, Combat, Story and PvP.
8. Live dashboard: confirm Roblox's current limits against what we assume (Config.Analytics budget 120 + 20 a player a minute for all custom events and funnel steps together; 100 custom event names; 10 funnels besides onboarding; 3 custom fields; the distinct custom field values allowed per event, which we keep small). If the dashboard shows a field as "Other" or throttled, tell Claude the event's name.

1. New hero: finish the prologue, open the Journal (J) on STORY. A row of three tabs sits on top: ACT 1 (gold), ACT 2 and ACT 3 (grey). Under them Act 1's 6 missions read as before (1. THE CEREMONY to 6.), then YOUR CHOICES, the PIN row, and two locked rows: "Act 2: coming soon" and "Act 3: coming soon", each with LOCKED on the right.
2. Tap ACT 2: the page shows only "Act 2: coming soon" (LOCKED): no missions, no choices, no PIN, no other words. Tap ACT 3: the same for Act 3. Tap ACT 1: the missions are back. Close and reopen the Journal: it stays on the act you last picked.
3. Phone (iPhone SE size or a real phone): the act row fits on one line under the two rows of main tabs, each act button is easy to tap, nothing overlaps the X, and the STORY page still scrolls to the locked rows.
4. Play mission 2 (Safehouse) from STORY: START, the mission, the reward and the next mission's READY all work as before; PIN still pins the story step to the tracker.
5. A hero who has cleared all of Act 1 (Dev panel: "Act 1 done"): every mission says CLEARED and REPLAY works, nothing new is pinned, the Lodestar talk and Patch's radio read their after-Act-1 lines as before, and Act 2 stays "coming soon". Rejoin: the save loads with the same clears and choices.
6. Developer Console shows no errors from Journal, StoryService or StoryClient while switching acts and tabs.
### WP8: live tuning from Balance_v<N> (2026-11-18)
The server lays a live key over the bundled Config at boot (tests: ONLY="WP8"). Only the numbers in Config.Balance.keys can change (pay, robot hp and hit size, the world boss's HP and wait). How to write the key: notes/Launch Checklist.md "Live tuning".
1. Studio with Game Settings > Security > Enable Studio Access to API Services OFF: Play. The Output shows "[Balance] v1.0 studio (Balance_v1/live): 0 set, 0 refused" before "[Capeline] boot, balance v1.0", and the game runs as before (an unpublished file shows "unpublished" instead of "studio").
2. Turn API access ON (a published place) with no key written: the line reads "v1.0 none". Open the DEV panel, press "Balance: print": the same line and "(the bundled Config, nothing changed)".
3. Write the example key from the Launch Checklist into the Balance_v1 DataStore (key "live") with a DataStore editor plugin, then Play: "[Balance] v1.1 live (Balance_v1/live): 3 set, 0 refused". "Balance: print" lists each number old -> new. A Brute Bot (Dev panel) now has 500 HP instead of 600; an incident pays about 1.5 / 1.4 of before.
4. Add a bad number to the key (for example "Economy.scale.credits": 40, or "Loot.sources.incident.chance": 0.9): Play. The good numbers still land; the line shows "1 refused" with a warning, and "Balance: print" shows "refused Economy.scale.credits (range)" or "(locked)".
5. Analytics: with Studio's Output, each join prints "[Analytics] balance_live 3 v1.1 live 0" once (not again later in the session).
6. Delete the key (or set Config.Balance.on = false): Play: "v1.0 none" (or "v1.0 off"); everything is back to the bundled numbers.
7. On a phone, after step 3: the contract board's pay matches what the contract pays when finished (the board shows the server's numbers).
### WP9: HQ WANTED poster and nemesis heads (2026-11-18)
Your nemesis's WANTED poster and a head per nemesis rank beaten, on the HQ's east wall by the Trophy Wall (tests: ONLY="WP9"). Dev panel: "Level 50" or add levels, "Nemesis Intel", "Nemesis beaten".
1. A fresh hero below level 8: go down to your HQ (the Safehouse stairwell at the plaza's west edge, or the Foundry door as a villain). On the east wall right of the Trophy Wall: a pinned paper WANTED poster on a wooden board with a black silhouette, "???", RANK 1, a grey LEVEL 8 stamp and "Your nemesis finds you from level 8". Above it five dark plaques with "?" and plates RANK 1 ? to RANK 5 ?.
2. At level 8 or more (leave and come back in): the poster shows your nemesis's bust in its colours with its head piece (visor, dome, horns or a single eye) and cape if it has one, its name (the same as the WANTED button's panel), RANK 1 and a star, INTEL 0 / 3 and "It hunts you in the city". As a villain the name starts with Hunter.
3. Walk up to the poster: the prompt reads "Wanted Poster / Read" on B. Press it: the WANTED panel opens (the HQ station panel closes if it was open). TROPHY WALL's panel shows "Nemesis heads: 0 / 5 on the east wall" and WANTED POSTER opens the same panel.
4. Dev panel "Nemesis Intel", then leave the HQ and come back: INTEL 3 / 3 and a red SHOWDOWN READY stamp; the trait pieces it has earned show on the bust (chest plates, a shoulder cannon, a green heal drone, an antenna).
5. Dev panel "Nemesis beaten" while in the HQ: the wall redraws: the first plaque holds the rank 1 nemesis's head in its colours with its name and one star on a gold plate; the poster shows the rank 2 nemesis (a new name, two stars). Press it four more times: five heads, each a different nemesis, left (rank 1) to right (rank 5); a sixth press adds no sixth head.
6. Win a real Showdown (Dev panel "Nemesis Intel", then SHOWDOWN on the poster panel out in the city): back in the HQ the next plaque has its head and the poster shows the next nemesis as a silhouette with an ON ITS WAY stamp for a day.
7. Look at the wall in both HQs: nothing clips the pilasters, the work lamps, the Foundry's red light band or the collection props below; the text is readable from the floor in front of it and from the Trophy Wall pad. Rejoin: the poster and heads are the same.
8. Phone (iPhone SE): the "Read" prompt shows near the poster and opens the WANTED panel fitted to the screen; the TROPHY WALL panel's note wraps and WANTED POSTER is tappable.

### WP6: robot combat depth (2026-11-18)
The Warden's brace, the War-Frame's telegraphed block, the Sneak Bot's lunge and downed robots' wrecks (tests: ONLY="WP6"). Use the Dev panel to spawn robots (Sneak Bot, Warden, Brute Bot) near you.
1. Sneak Bot lunge: spawn a Sneak Bot and stand about 12 studs from it. Within a few seconds it stops, shows itself, sinks low with its arms swept back and red eyes, a rising whine plays, and a red lane fills along the ground from it to just past you over about 0.65 s. Then it dashes down the lane and strikes once.
2. Step out of the lane during the wind-up: it dashes past and misses. Hold Block in the lane instead: SHATTER! (the lunge is a breaker) and you reel.
3. Hit it with a basic (a light hit) during the wind-up: CUT OFF! pops, it reels for a moment, the lane fades at once and no lunge comes. On a phone the lane and the coil read clearly at the default camera.
4. Warden brace: spawn a Warden and fight it. Every few seconds it shines blue for about 2.5 s; basics from the front and the sides do much less; get behind it and hits land (and break its guard as before). A heavy or a full charge from the front during the shine: SHATTER!, it reels, the shine goes and it does not shine again for a while.
5. War-Frame: play the Act 1 mission with Bulwark's battle form (or the task force's War-Frame rematch). About 6 s into the fight a blue ring fills under it while its shine fades in with a low hum (about 1.2 s), then it holds a big blue shine for about 3.5 s: front hits do a quarter, the back weak point still takes full. A heavy during the shine: SHATTER!, the shine goes, and its next attack comes a moment later. It never shines during its phase-2 shield. The ring and shine sit on the boss, not off to one side, as it turns.
6. Wrecks: beat a Brute Bot, a Sneak Bot and a Crane Trooper in the street. Each leaves a heap in its colours with sparks; walk up: GRAB shows (every archetype, no Awakening). Grab it (overhead, 80% speed), throw it at robots: the medium crash and knock. After about 3 s on the ground it flickers, then pops apart in sparks; an untouched wreck goes after about 10 s.
7. Wreck cap: beat 8 robots quickly in one spot: at most 6 wrecks lie there at once (the oldest go first, never one you are carrying). Swarmers, Wind-Up Toys, the famous fakes, twins, finale villains and sea robots leave no wreck. Beating a robot over a deep gap (a flyer high over the street) leaves the wreck on the ground below, or none past 80 studs.
8. Phone (low preset): after a big fight with wrecks around, the frame rate holds; the sparks thin out when the debris budget is busy. Developer Console shows no errors from ThrowService, ThrowFx, BlockService, GuardFx, CombatFx or EnemyRenderer.
### Rogues File and Journal tabs (2026-11-18, WP3 and WP7)
The Journal's ROGUES, BADGES, MENTOR and SHOWDOWN tabs (tests: ONLY="Rogues File" and ONLY="Journal tabs"; the UI sim opens each tab at phone, tablet and PC sizes). DRAFT TEXT for nate's OK: the 45 page blurbs and hints in Config.Rogues.
1. Fresh account: press J, ROGUES: "ROGUES FILE 0 / 45", every page a black silhouette with "???" and a hint (Swarmer: "Street fights from level 3"); the top says "Next: 10 pages for the Antenna Helm".
2. Beat a Crane Trooper: a NEW PAGE! stamp with its name and a ping; the ROGUES tab shows its page in its colours with "Beaten x1 | First: <today>, level <yours>". Beat two more: x3. Your squadmate who did not land the defeat gets no page.
3. Beat 10 different kinds (Dev panel spawns): the 10 PAGES! stamp and confetti, "NEW LOOK PIECE: ANTENNA HELM", and the helm is in the creator's Masks tab.
4. Finish a side quest chain (Dev panel: The Gala Heist's finale): REDLINE and MADAME MASQUE-A-RADE pages fill for every member of the group; the finale robots in a dress (The Pit's Pitboss) never fill their suit's robot page.
5. A world boss with your hero at level 40+ (the fight's level is the median of the heroes near; start it from the Dev panel, get Contributor or MVP, then beat it): its page and a GOLD PAGE! stamp; the page has a gold frame and "GOLD PAGE". At level 20, or as a Participant: the page only, with the gold hint under it. A retreat fills nothing.
6. Rejoin: every page, count and first date is still there (and on a second character: the Rogues File is the account's).
7. BADGES tab: BADGE BOOK, COLLECTIONS and TITLES switch the view inside the Journal; picking a title still works. MENTOR tab: the card with its quest line (PICK and CHANGE work at a safehouse). SHOWDOWN tab: the WANTED poster; with full Intel, SHOWDOWN starts the fight and the Journal closes.
8. One panel at a time: with the Journal open on any tab, open GEAR: the Journal closes; WANTED, MENTOR and BADGES still open their own screens.
9. iPhone SE and a 640 x 360 Android (Test > Device): both tab rows sit left of X, every tab readable, the ROGUES rows' blurbs fit on two lines.
### Weekly boards (2026-11-18, WP2)
Boss MVPs, rescues and PvP wins on Weekly_<week> boards (tests: ONLY="Boards"; the screen at phone sizes: ONLY="Phone: every screen"). Studio needs Game Settings > Security > Enable Studio Access to API Services off for the memory boards, or on to try the real stores.
1. BOARDS in the top row (or MORE on a phone) opens WEEKLY BOARDS: three tabs (BOSS MVPS, RESCUES, PVP WINS) and ALL. Each tab shows "Reading the board" for a moment, then "Nobody is on this board this week yet. Be the first!", the reward line (TOP 10 EARN THE TITLE ...) and RESETS IN with the days and hours to Monday 00:00 UTC.
2. Rescue a citizen in a rescue incident, then reopen RESCUES after a minute: you are #1 with 1 RESCUE, in gold, and pinned under the list. The Sunken Line's commuter rescue counts too.
3. Win a world boss as its MVP: BOSS MVPS counts 1. Beat a flagged hero in the Crater: PVP WINS counts 1; beating the same hero again and again (the anti-farming ledger's repeat or trade) stops counting when it stops paying Fame.
4. DEV > Boards: test rows: every board shows 40 Test Hero rows, you at #6 (tied with Test Hero 6). Scroll the list; FRIENDS shows only you (the test heroes are not your friends) ranked #1.
5. The plaza board stands on the plaza's west edge, south of the Safehouse stairwell, facing the Beacon Tower: THIS WEEK'S TOP 10 with three columns. After test rows it fills within a minute. Respawn (Reset) and walk back: it is still drawn (no flicker, no blank).
6. DEV > Boards: end week: a TOP 10! card slides in with one line per board ("#6 ON BOSS MVPS LAST WEEK: TITLE BOSS BREAKER") and the fanfare ping. WEAR IT opens BADGES > TITLES, where Boss Breaker, City Saver and Crater Champion can be worn under your name. The boards now read empty (a new week). Press it again: no card (claimed once).
7. On a phone (Device emulator, iPhone SE): the screen fits, rows read, the tab buttons are big enough to tap, the pinned row sits under the list.
8. Leave and rejoin within a minute (real stores): your totals are on the board on the next look; Output shows no "[Boards] write failed".
9. Live (after publishing, two servers): a stat on server A shows on server B's board within about two minutes; the week's titles arrive on the first join after Monday 00:15 UTC.

### Perf fixes (2026-11-18)
Measured fixes for 30-player servers and phones (tests: ONLY="Perf"). Needs Art.Street imported for step 2, and the Roblox look (Dev panel "Robots:" roblox, uploaded clothes) for steps 4 and 5.
1. MicroProfiler on a phone: publish to a private test place, join on a real phone (Studio's emulator runs on the PC's CPU, so it cannot show this), open the MicroProfiler (Settings > MicroProfiler, or Ctrl+F6 on a PC) and pause on the first seconds after the city loads. Before: one frame of several hundred ms where CityProps ran WorldMap.build. Now: the CityProps label is spread over about 30 frames, none much over 30 ms on the phone; the street props still appear around you within a few seconds.
2. Same props: on a PC, look at a Downtown corner, a Docks quay, an Old Town street, Uptown's park and a suburb lot. The benches, bins, lamps, bushes, mailboxes and power poles stand where they did before (the tests check the list is the same for 3 seeds; this checks the device builds it).
3. Server: run LoadBots (30 bots) with a phone and a PC joined. Server FrameProf (workspace:SetAttribute("FrameProf", true) on the Server command bar): EnemyService's replication share is lower than before, and BlockService now has its own line. In the Developer Console > Network on the phone, the Enemy.Delta packets are about 320 B (24 robots); on the PC about 840 B (64), never over 900. Settings > Graphics > High on the phone: its packets grow to the PC's within a second.
4. Texture count (the clothes): in Play with 20+ robots and citizens around, run this on the Client command bar: local n=0 for _,d in workspace:GetDescendants() do if d:IsA("Texture") then n+=1 end end print(n). On low it was about 3,300 (66 a dressed rig); now only rigs within 60 studs keep clothes (20 on low) and the 21 hidden faces only within 20, so expect a few hundred. Walk up to a robot and a citizen: nothing pops on the front or back as they come near; from 10 studs the clothes look exactly as before (inner legs, soles, hips all dressed).
5. Far rigs: from 100+ studs a dressed robot or citizen shows its body colours (shirt on top, pants below, skin on the hands), close to its clothes. Hit a near robot: the white flash still shows through its clothes; a disguised Mimic still swaps to street clothes.
6. Robot bodies: after a big fight (an event or the boss), fly away from the robots and wait 35 s. The client's Developer Console > Memory > Instances count falls back: each kind keeps 4 spare bodies (2 on low) and destroys the rest. Fly back: new robots still appear at once.
7. Side quest tracker: during a step with a timer (a chase or a race), the seconds count down smoothly and the HUD stays right after a step changes, though the server now sends the state only when it changes.
8. Faces: the next upload (the pending package in /mnt/project-files/capeline/upload/) has the 17 side quest faces at 256 px. After nate's upload, each person's face still reads clearly up close in Studio. The 18 shared faces already uploaded at 1024 still work; re-uploading them at 256 (art/export/clothes/faces) would save their texture memory too (optional).

### WP4 and WP5: MMO funnel, favorite prompt, REMATCH, Suspicious Hero (2026-11-18)
The gap check's funnel firsts, the once-per-account favorite ask, the PvP REMATCH ping and incident 25 (tests: ONLY="WP4" and ONLY="WP5").
1. Funnel: on a fresh profile (Dev panel > Wipe profile), block a robot's hit, throw something, meet a nemesis, finish a task force and earn a badge: the Output prints one [Funnel] MMO line for each (first_block, first_throw, first_nemesis, first_taskforce, first_badge), and doing each again prints nothing. Rejoin: still nothing for those steps.
2. Pass: buy the Hero Pass in a Studio test purchase: one [Funnel] MMO step 6 pass_bought line.
3. Favorite: win a world boss (Dev panel boss, then weaken): about 8 s after the result, when nothing is hitting you, Roblox's own Favorite prompt shows once, with no reward text. Win another boss, and rejoin and win again: it never shows again. On a phone (Device Emulator) it shows the same, and a failed prompt (Studio may refuse it) prints no error.
4. Rematch: two players flagged in the Crater (Test > Clients and Servers, 2 players). Player A defeats B: B's Defeated screen shows REMATCH above the respawn picks (where GIVE UP and HELP were), and after respawn at the bottom middle until 45 s pass, on PC and on iPhone SE (Android 640 x 360 too: clear of the top bar). B taps it: B sees "Rematch ping sent to A"; A gets "B WANTS A REMATCH" with a countdown, ACCEPT and NO, in the result card's place (the win card makes way); on iPhone SE the whole card is on screen.
5. A taps ACCEPT: both get the REMATCH ON toast and a ping at the other's spot for about 10 s. No one is moved. B's REMATCH button is gone after one tap or after 45 s.
6. A taps NO, or lets the 15 s run out: the card goes and B is not told (no rejection screen). A defeats B again within 2 minutes: B's REMATCH tap says "You pinged them a moment ago". B unflags, then taps REMATCH: "Switch PvP on first, then tap again" and the button stays. A unflags before ACCEPT: A gets the same line. A mutes B (plate > MUTE): B's tap shows the usual sent line, but A never sees a card.
7. Suspicious Hero: Dev panel > Inc: SuspiciousHero (as a level 6+ hero). A crowd of 7 stands in the street, one dressed as Gleamheart, Steelvow or Quickstep (generic suits, never Skylance or the famous three). Hit it to half health: its face cracks to a robot, "THE MASK CRACKS: IT'S A ROBOT!" shows, the crowd runs, and the news ticker shows the fake hero line within a minute.
8. As a Vigil hero the next step is "Scrap the fake before it slips away": beating the same robot ends it with FAKE SCRAPPED! As a Syndicate hero it is "Let the panic spread: hold the square" (8 s) and the PANIC beat plays. Both give a medal and the result card.
9. Badges: after 10 unmasks (helpers count too) BADGES shows Fake Spotter. No errors from IncidentService, FavoriteService, FlagService or PvpHud in the Output.

### WP1: daily login streak and daily quests (2026-11-18)
The daily card, the 7-day track, 3 daily quests a UTC day (rules in Shared.DailyRules, numbers in Config.Daily; tests: ONLY="WP1"). The Dev panel has Daily: next day, Daily: skip a day, Daily: finish quests and Daily: start over.
1. A new hero: play the prologue to the end. About 4 s after you stand in the city the DAILY card opens on day 1 (300 Credits ready, the CLAIM button in gold). It never opens over the prologue or a mission scene.
2. Press CLAIM: the button shows "..." at once, then the DAY 1! stamp with confetti and a ping, the wallet goes up 300, and the card reads "Claimed! Tomorrow, day 2: 20 Dust". Press DAILY in the top row again: no second claim is offered.
3. Leave and rejoin (and, on a published test place, join a second server): the card does not open again today and the streak still reads 1.
4. Dev panel > Daily: next day, six times, claiming each day: day 3 and day 6 pay Renown (it scales with your level), day 7 shows NEW TITLE: SEVEN DAYS STRONG on the stamp, and the title is in BADGES > TITLES to wear.
5. Dev panel > Daily: skip a day: the streak goes on (the grace day) and the card says this week's grace is used. Skip again right away: the streak starts over at day 1, the title is still yours and BEST still shows 7.
6. Leave a day unclaimed and press Daily: next day: that day's reward is paid at once (a DAY N! stamp) and the new day waits for CLAIM.
7. Quests: TODAY'S QUESTS lists 3 different jobs with counts and pay. Clear an incident, rescue a citizen, beat robots: on PC and iPad the DAILY n/3 pill under the top row opens its rows for about 6 s with the new count; finishing one shows the DAILY! stamp and pays at once; all three add the ALL DONE! stamp and the bonus. Tap the pill: the rows stay open; tap again: they close.
8. Phone (Device Emulator, iPhone SE and iPhone 14, both hands): the card fits the screen and scrolls; the tracker pill does not show, and the DAILY button reads DAILY n/3 (with ! when a reward waits) in the top row or MORE.
9. Studio with API access off (memory saves) works the same. On a published test place with saving down (Data fixes step 3's long keyPrefix): the card shows the error state ("Your save is offline...") with RETRY, and nothing is paid.
10. In Studio the Output prints the analytics lines daily_login, daily_claim, daily_quest and daily_all_done, and no errors from DailyService or DailyHud.

### Combat fixes: speed caps, leaps, PvP rules, cooldowns (2026-11-18)
Review 5 fixes in MoveGuard, MoveCheck, CastPlan, CombatService and GearService (tests: ONLY="Combat fixes").
1. Speed buffs: a Super-speed hero holds the sprint while a Mystic friend casts Weightless with Feather on them: 6 s at full sprint, no snap-back. Same with Tide Mirror and Glide Step. The Output has no "[MoveGuard] strike" line.
2. Dashes: with Studio Settings > Network > Incoming Replication Lag at 0.2 s, use Shadow Step, Spirit Swap and Unstoppable 10 times each, then the Grappling Hook and Meteor Leap: no snap-back after any of them.
3. Meteor Leap aimed 30 degrees up at a robot 90 studs away on flat street: the hero lands next to it and the landing burst hits it (it used to land 13 studs short). Phoenix Dive the same.
4. PvP: two flagged heroes in the Crater. One Awakens: their hits on the other are about 10% stronger, not 20%. A super field (one standing in it) ticks at the 60% super rate. Opponents casting supers from paired paths side by side never trigger a team-up combo; two friends still do.
5. Cooldowns: mash a 0.5 s skill (or tap a gadget) as fast as you can for 10 s: it fires about 20 times, never 24; a normal press when the sweep ends always fires.
6. Charge: hold a chargeable skill just short of the full glow and release: it is a partial charge at any ping, a full hold is full.
7. No casting: during fast travel's flight and in the creator, the skill keys do nothing (no cast on the server, no error).
8. Leaving: drop a field (Healing Rain, Ember Ring) and Soul Chains, then leave the game: the field stops at once on the other players' screens and no error shows. With 40 fields up (Dev panel or bots), a new field of yours replaces your own oldest one, not another player's.
### Comic UI look (2026-11-18, branch quality-ui)
The whole UI restyled to nate's bold comic mockup (project files capeline/ui/HUD mockup B; renders "comic UI after - *.png" there; `lune run tests/uishot.luau build/Capeline.rbxl out.json` + `python3 tests/uishot.py out.json dir` redraws them). Visuals only: Kit.ink outlines and shadows, cream paper, navy panels, halftone, tilted tabs and toasts.
1. HUD at 1280 x 720 and iPhone SE: every panel and button has a thick ink outline and a hard ink shadow down-right; the round action buttons and the radar have round shadows under them; Super sits on a red starburst; no shadow or outline shows on an invisible container, a bar's fill or a full-screen backdrop.
2. A padded button (any Kit button, e.g. SHOP, CLOSE): its shadow hugs the outline just outside the bottom and right edges. If the shadow sits inside the button or a few px off, set Config.Ui.ink.padsFolder = false (whether Roblox lays a Folder's GuiObjects out inside the owner's UIPadding). Lists still lay out exactly as before (UIListLayout and UIGridLayout ignore the shadow Folder).
3. Halftone dots (image 89303571654854, uploaded 2026-11-18, may sit in review a while) show faintly on cream and navy panels, under their text and buttons, never over them; blank until review passes is fine.
4. The top row's tabs lean left and right alternately, with square-ish corners; the MORE drawer's rows sit straight. Toasts are speech bubbles (white, or their colour) leaning alternately, the newest with a tail; they fade out cleanly (outline and shadow fade too).
5. The status card is cream with ink lettering, its health bar striped (red when low), and taps, cooldown sweeps, charge rings and Kit.bounce still look right on the cluster (the round shadow scales with the bounce).

### UI fixes: panels, keys, phones (2026-11-18)
Client UI bugs from the review (tests: ONLY="Phone"; the UI sim now loads every Ui module, opens the Hero MMO panels and walks every hidden part of every screen at 640 x 360 too). Use Test > Device for the phone steps.
1. News screens: stand by a city news TV, reset your character (Esc > Reset) and respawn: every news screen still shows the channel and its ticker.
2. Keys: K opens the Task Force board (and nothing else); N opens Photo Mode and N or B closes it. On a controller the HUD hints read: Skill 3 ◀, Skill 4 ▼, Super ▲, Awaken ▶, Block LB (the POWERS button shows no pad hint).
3. Photo Mode refuses during a cutscene (play a story beat, press N: nothing) and while Downed; a cutscene starting, or going Downed, while in Photo Mode closes it and gives the camera back.

10. Downed with the shop or settings open: they close and the Downed screen shows on top (GIVE UP and HELP work).
11. Map: tap MAP on and off quickly ten times, then leave it open: the YOU dot moves smoothly (one follow loop, not several).
12. Controller: open each panel above with a pad: its first button is selected at once and the D-pad moves between its buttons; closing it gives the D-pad back to the game. Also check the I key (GEAR) does not zoom the camera in a way that bothers players (Roblox's camera uses I and O to zoom).
