2. In Studio, paste resolve_ids.txt into the Command Bar; save Workspace > CapelineImageIds' text as image_ids.txt. Attach ids.json and image_ids.txt in the chat (Claude runs art/upload/apply_ids.py; it now leaves the 22 city materials alone when a batch has no city maps).
3. Import the models: File > Import 3D, art/export/finales/Bramblebeast.fbx, Voidhound.fbx and WindupGiant.fbx, each as one Model named Bramblebeast, Voidhound, WindupGiant into ReplicatedStorage.Art (its MeshParts keep the R15 names and the extras: BrambleCrown, BrambleMane, BrambleBack; HoundSnout, HoundEars, HoundSpines, HoundTail; GiantShako, GiantKey). Check each MeshPart has a SurfaceAppearance with the colour map's AlphaMode set to Overlay (the green bark, the hound's dark shell and the giant's red coat take the part colour).
4. Pull the models back into the repo like the Trooper (art/models), so every build has them.
5. Givers: Dev-teleport to each chain's giver (Gus, Benny, Scoops, Dot, Nell, Pearl, Brick, Vanta, Nora, Okafor, Ruth, Moth, Tess, Kiko). Each wears their own shirt and trousers over their hat, coat and boots, and Gus, Nell, Brick, Ruth, Moth and Kiko their own face. No one is missing a body part or shows a white or grey shirt.
6. Helpers: play The Hero Academy to step 4 (Wren), Wind-Up Lane step 3 (Toby), The Pit step 2 (Flint), The Salt Dogs step 6 (Finn, carried), The Sunken City step 3 (the diver), The Last Owner step 2 (Ambrose) and The Taken Master step 5 (Hoshi). Each is the named person in their outfit and face (not a generic citizen); the carried one still rides along and is set down at the goal.
7. Finale villains: Dev-jump each chain's finale. Pitboss, Tidewright, Vesper, Catchpole, Scorch, Censor, Holloway, Morrow, Clawback, Gale, Headmaster Halo, Redline, Masque-a-rade, the Harvest King and Captain Brine wear their own uniform; Vesper's face cracks to her own cracked face. The costumed ones still tear off at their health, and the plate still reads their name.
8. Models: The Harvest King's finale shows the Bramblebeast as the pumpkin-headed bramble creature with its antler crown, leaf mane and thorny back; hits flash it, it never sparks and bursts into light. Starblood Calling's finale shows the Voidhound (hound helm, one violet eye, snout, ears, back fins and tail); Wind-Up Lane's shows the tin soldier with his shako and key, and his key no longer doubles up. Shell pieces break off the two machines as they lose health (the extras go with their part).
9. Remove one imported model from ReplicatedStorage.Art: that finale goes back to today's look with no errors in the output.

### Data fixes: saves, Robux, moderation, HQ (2026-11-18)
Receipts, leave-saves, kicks and Legacy (rules in Shared.ShopRules, GuardRules, MoveCheck, HqRules; tests: ONLY="Data fixes"). Needs a published place with Studio API access on for steps 1 to 4.
1. Leave-save: play a few minutes (earn Renown, rank a skill, change a loadout), wait under 2 minutes (no autosave), then leave. Rejoin: the Renown, the rank and the loadout are all there. Do it 3 times (the old order lost them on most leaves).
2. Receipt: buy a pack with a test product id. It lands once, and the Output has no "[Purchase] ... not saved yet". Rejoin: still owned, and the same receipt never grants twice.
3. Save down: for this test only, set Config.Data.keyPrefix to a 60-letter text (DataStore keys stop at 50, so every load fails) and join: after the load tries the "saving is down" toast shows. Open the Robux shop and press BUY: no Roblox prompt; it reads "Saving is down on this server. Rejoin to shop". Put keyPrefix back after step 4.
4. Slot switch on that no-save session: earn some Renown, switch to slot 2 and back: the first hero keeps the Renown it had.
5. Kick once: in a live test server (Studio never kicks) teleport with an exploit tool 3 times inside 10 minutes: one kick, the account's kick count goes up by 1 and no review row is written. In Studio the Output shows one "would kick" line, not several.
6. Lag spike: Studio Settings > Network > Incoming Replication Lag 1.5 s, boost-fly in a straight line for 5 s: at most one "[MoveGuard] strike" line, never a kick.
7. Legacy: a level 50 hero with origin Starborn, Web-swing mobility, a carried weapon, a worn title and travel styles retires: after it they are all the same (not Training, not Flight, not Bare Fists).
8. Hero Pass at the boundary: set Config.Pass.startWeek so a season ended an hour ago, rejoin, buy the Hero Pass: the pass card shows the paid track on the new season, and it stays paid after a minute's roll.
9. Slot switch travel style: two heroes with different travel styles on the same mobility; switch slots: the new hero's style shows at once, and swapping mobility at a safehouse many times never slows the server.
10. Nemesis Showdown: start one while a squadmate is still loading in (no hero yet): the Showdown starts, no NemesisService error.

### Story fixes: missions and the prologue (2026-11-18)
Soft-locks and pay bugs fixed in MissionService and OpeningService (rules in Shared.MissionRules; tests: ONLY="Story fixes").
1. Task force: start one with a squad of two. Each leg plays all its stages, the comic panel shows for 4 s between legs, and each member gets one result card per leg (not every leg in half a second, not a burst of pay at the start).
2. First stage: enter any Act 1 mission (Dev "<mission>: Vigil"). Its stage 1 plays after the fade in (the run used to skip it during the 0.6 s fade).
3. Full server: Dev robot spawns up to the 90-robot cap, then Dev-jump a mission to a boss, chase and defeat stage. Nothing comes yet and the tracker waits; clear some robots and the boss, runner or squad appears within a second. A defeat stage counts only the robots that came.
4. Last one out: in a task force, every other member leaves, then the last presses Leave. They fade back to where they entered, in the city, and never fall from the pocket at y 3,000.
5. Squadmates: a squadmate who is Downed, mid fast-travel, or hit in the last few seconds stays behind when the leader enters a mission; a free one comes along.
6. Party pay: a level 40 member leaves a run with a level 20 and an 18; on the clear the level 20 reads no SIDEKICK bonus.
7. Prologue, first time: on a new character the ceremony shows no SKIP. Dev "Prologue: Vigil" again (a replay) shows SKIP as before.
8. Prologue, returning account: wait 2 minutes on the main menu, then PLAY THE PROLOGUE. The whole prologue plays (its 330 s clock starts at PLAY, not at the menu).
9. Prologue safety: during the ceremony, let a city robot reach and hit the hero: health never drops below about 18%, and the hero is never Downed in the prologue.
### Open-world fixes (2026-11-18)
Boss, contract, event, incident and Downed fixes (tests: ONLY="World fixes"). Two players (Studio Test > 2 players) where it says so.
1. Boss pay: DEV start the boss with 4 or more heroes (bots from the load test are fine), DEV weaken it and finish it: every hero gets the result card and pay, the Hero Pass XP lands, the Output shows no BossService error, and the next boss timer starts after the result.
2. Boss leaver: player 2 does the most damage, then leaves before the end: player 1's card shows them as MVP #1 and the MVP names never show the leaver.
3. Boss retreat: let a boss time out (8 minutes): the card pays 25% Credits only, and the Fame bar does not move.
4. Boss result PvP: both players flagged in the arena; land a PvP defeat in the few seconds after the boss falls: the winner's PvP card pays in full (not the halved boss-fight pay).
5. Bounty: take a Bounty contract and beat the elite 10 times (DEV re-take it each time): every kill counts on the tracker; a new elite never stands up where the beaten one fell.
6. Your double: DEV start Your double and beat the twin 5 times: it always ends as a win and pays.
7. Robot rampage: DEV spawn robots to near the 90 cap, then DEV start Robot rampage: the tracker asks only for the robots that came, and beating them all wins and pays.
8. Incident at the cap: DEV spawn robots up to the cap, then take an incident whose first step is a fight: nothing there yet; clear a few robots and the squad appears within about 3 s, and the step finishes.
9. Reset while Downed: get Downed by robots, then press Esc > Reset: you respawn standing, can fly, and do not crawl.
10. Reset mid PvP: two flagged players; player 2 hits player 1, then player 1 Resets within 15 s: player 2 gets the PvP win card once. Player 1 leaves within 15 s of a PvP defeat: player 2 is not paid a second time.

### Side quest server fixes (2026-11-18)
Seven soft-locks in SideQuestService fixed (tests: ONLY="SQ fixes"). Use the Dev panel's side quest jumps (300 + chain, then 100 + step) to reach each step.
1. Finale: beat a chain's finale villain 10 times (DEV jump to the last step each time): every kill finishes the chain; the villain never stands up again at full HP after its fall. A chain with an encore (The Gala Heist): beating the first villain always brings the encore.
2. Escort: try the escorts at Gull Point Pier (The Salt Dogs step 4), Tick-Tock Toys (Wind-Up Lane step 4) and the Academy (step 3) a few times each, on two weekly seeds: the truck always drives the whole way and the tracker reaches 160 of 160. Sometimes it drives back along its own street; it never stops short.
3. Pocket: walk into a pocket step's purple ring, then Leave the run (or let it fail): you are back at the ring and are NOT pulled in again; step out and back in and the run starts again.
4. Co-op: a Vigil hero and a Syndicate villain on a split step; the Vigil finish first ("waiting" toast), then the villain leaves the server (or abandons the chain in the Journal): the Vigil hero's step completes and pays at once.
5. Photo: on a photo step beat two Sneak Bots before any photo: new ones slip in round the spot and the step can still be finished.
6. Full server: DEV spawn robots up to the 90 cap, then start a fight, chase or photo step: nothing is there yet; clear some robots and the squad, runner or Sneak Bots appear within a second and the step finishes. A fight's tracker never asks for more kills than robots it puts out.
7. Slot switch: two characters (one Vigil, one Syndicate) pinned on the same chain and step; switch slot at a safehouse: the old side's goal goes and the new character gets its own side's goal.

### Lore step 12: the city remembers (2026-11-17, nate's lore)
Vigil Trust and Syndicate Fear now open their own styles, titles and hideout upgrades (5 ranks each, looks only), citizens and Patch react to high ranks, and three side-vs-side events run when both sides are on the server.
1. As a Vigil hero: DEV "Rep tier: set 1" (each tap goes up one rank, "Rep tier: 0" resets). You get VIGIL TRUST RANK n, then "OPENED: <hideout upgrade>, TITLE "<title>", 2 NEW STYLES AT GIZMO'S", and Patch on the radio names you (side-flavoured; off with Settings "Guide radio").
2. Enter the Safehouse at each rank: 1 the thank-you wall on the south-west wall (a cork board, 6 notes with coloured icons, no text; 6 more notes a rank), 2 Patch's hacking rig on the south-east wall (desk, 3 green screens, rack, stool), 3 the hero relic case by the east pillar (torn red cape, cracked blue shield with a gold chevron), 4 the gold helmet joins it under a spotlight, 5 a gold banner and a lit frame on the full wall. Every station pad and prompt is where it was and none is covered. Setting the rank while inside redraws the room.
3. As a Syndicate villain, the same in the Foundry: 1 the loot vault (round door, gold bars), 2 the big trophy screen with a gold crown, 3 a captured Crane robot (navy, the Crane C) hanging from a hook, 4 more bars, cash crates and a lit door trim, 5 a gold frame on the screen, a red carpet and a spotlight.
4. Gizmo's Bench: your own side's 10 styles show under "<TRACK> RANK n: side styles", locked ones read RANK n, open ones a Credit price; buy one (Credits only, never Robux) and equip it in GEAR > Show Style; stats and gear score do not change. A rank below the style's: the buy is refused ("Your side's reputation rank opens that style"). The other side's styles never show.
5. Titles: the Badge Book's title list has your rank titles (Friendly Face to Heart of the City, Street Menace to Boss of the City); pick one, it shows on your plate.
6. Citizens: at rank 2 and up walk near citizens: the Vigil get arms-up cheers with little hops, the Syndicate get flinches (lean back, arms up); from rank 4 from further away; fleeing citizens never stop to react.
7. News: reach rank 4 or 5 (or join with it): the ticker and big screens read "THE CITY TRUSTS <name>: VIGIL TRUST RANK 5, "HEART OF THE CITY"" (or FEARS / SYNDICATE FEAR); a second player hears Patch mention that hero by name once.
8. DEV "Side event: Data truck standoff" with a hero and a villain: everyone gets the card DATA TRUCK STANDOFF / ESCORT IT OR CRACK IT and BREAKING NEWS; the navy truck with the Crane C rolls fast while a Vigil hero is within 60 studs and crawls otherwise; only Syndicate hits crack it (a Vigil hero's hits do nothing); the tracker reads VIGIL n% SYNDICATE n%. Delivered: the Vigil win; cracked: the Syndicate win; time up: no winner. Both sides get the event's pay and a gear roll plus Trust or Fear, the winners more; the ticker names a hero of the winning side.
9. DEV "Side event: Uptown vault standoff": the vault in Uptown cracks only while more Syndicate than Vigil stand in its ring; time up uncracked is the Vigil's win. DEV "Side event: Spark crystal raid": 9 crystals fall at the Docks, each counts for the side that grabs it, more wins.
10. PvP: in every side event unflagged players cannot hit each other (they fight the robot waves and the objective); flagged players can, as anywhere.
11. Leave a server with only one side for an hour: no side event starts by itself; with both sides, about one an hour (never in the first 10 minutes, at most one a 45 minutes).

### Lore step 9: The Crater and your double (2026-11-17, nate's lore)
The Crater is now the blasted Crane Industries lab from the battle where the old heroes fell (still the forced PvP zone, same bounds and beacon); the evil twin from The Copy comes back as a rare world event, Double Trouble.
1. DEV "Go to the Crater", then walk in: the 5 s warning bar and LEAVE work as before, then PVP ZONE and +50% Renown; the first time this session a card slams in: THE CRATER / WHERE THE OLD HEROES FELL. Walk out and in again: no second card. DEV "Crater card" shows it on demand.
2. Look round the bowl from the rim and from the floor: a scorched foundation in the middle, broken white lab walls with ragged tops, the Crane C (navy plate, white C) on the north and south walls, three cracked glass tanks with a faint blue Spark glow, two lab towers lying blown outward, dark burn scars running out from the middle, five robot husks, and west of the lab the old heroes' relics: a torn red cape on a rebar, a cracked blue shield emblem with a gold chevron, a dented gold helmet half buried. Nothing reads "comet" or "meteor".
3. PvP feel: two players (or DEV bots) can see each other across the bowl when flying, the middle is open floor, the walls and towers give cover on foot; nothing floats or clips through the rim; the Crater beacon on the south rim still works.
4. Phone (low preset): the Crater still runs smoothly (83 parts, was 21).
5. DEV "Event: my double" (Output prints whether the rare tier would have picked you and why): your twin (your look, your power path, your name on its plate) appears 35 to 60 studs away; you get the toast "EVENT: DOUBLE TROUBLE!", the card DOUBLE TROUBLE / A FAKE WEARING YOUR FACE, and Patch on the radio (hero and villain lines differ). Every player sees BREAKING NEWS "A FAKE OF <your hero name> IS LOOSE IN THE CITY" on the corner TV and the big screens; players within 300 studs get the "help" toast; the tracker and marker follow the twin.
6. Beat the twin with a second player helping: both get "DOUBLE TROUBLE DONE!" with three times an event's Credits and Renown and a gear roll; the ticker reads "<name> CRACKS THEIR OWN DOUBLE". Let one run out (180 s): the twin slips away and nothing is paid.
7. Rules (Output line of step 5 on a fresh character): "level" under 6, "copy" before The Copy is cleared, "prologue" during the Ceremony, "mission" in a mission, "crater" in the Crater, "afk" after 2 minutes without moving; after a double, "cooldown" until 3 hours more play. Settings "Guide radio" off: no Patch line (the card and toast still show).

### Lore step 8: world bosses (2026-11-17, nate's lore)
The three world bosses get nate's looks; names, fights, hit boxes and numbers are unchanged: Rustjaw Prime, THE JUNKYARD TITAN (a giant of wrecked cars: car-body legs, chest and fist, tyre knees and shoulders, a chrome bumper jaw, round headlight eyes, four exhaust stacks, a yellow scrapyard crane arm with a grab claw, glowing weld seams); Lumen Maw, THE LIGHT EATER (a sleek indigo night creature with cyan stripes, three gold light sacs on its back, a huge maw with a glowing spiral of light streams pulled into its throat, its stolen lights round the pit); Leviathan Engine, THE DEEP MACHINE (an ancient barnacled stone hull ringed with brass, porthole lights, gear wheels, a brass gear-ring neck, a diving-bell head with porthole eyes, a tide-turbine heart, two vent hatches). Previews (phase 1 from the front, phase 3 from behind; the part-built look; the lineup): art/preview/boss_RustjawPrime.png, boss_LumenMaw.png, boss_LeviathanEngine.png, boss_<Boss>_parts.png and bosses.png (also in the project files, capeline/art/bosses/). Re-render thumb_boss (`bash art/store/make.sh boss`) once you like them.
Import (replaces the Remade bosses import below):
1. Studio: delete the old ReplicatedStorage.Art.Bosses if you imported it. Home > Import 3D, pick `art/export/Bosses.fbx`, "Import as a single mesh" OFF. The 16 textures are inside the file.
2. A model with 28 MeshParts appears, named `<Boss>_<Piece>` (RustjawPrime_Hull, RustjawPrime_Plates2, ..., NullWarFrame_CannonGlow). Rename the model `Bosses` and drag it into ReplicatedStorage.Art.
3. If the textured parts (Hull, Plates2, Plates3, Cannon, Generator, Bay) come in white: Asset Manager > Bulk Import the 16 PNGs from art/export/bosses (`<Boss>_Color`, `_Normal`, `_Roughness`, `_Metalness`), make one SurfaceAppearance per boss with its four maps, and put it in that boss's textured parts. The Glow, Weak and lamp parts need no texture.
4. Right-click the Bosses model > Save to File..., save Bosses.rbxm and attach it in the chat; Claude puts it in the repo (art/models/Bosses.rbxm, mapped by Rojo) so every build has it.
Checks (DEV "Boss now" brings the next boss in the rotation). Do 5 to 8 once BEFORE the import (the part-built looks) and once after (the meshes):
5. Each boss faces the way it walks or turns and stands on the ground (the Leviathan's hull under the water with its head, neck, turbine and hatches above); nothing floats or sits inside the body. If one comes in turned or mirrored, tell Claude.
6. Weak points pulse gold where hits do double damage (Dev damage numbers on): Rustjaw's chest engine core and both tyre-hub knees, the Maw's three light sacs, the Leviathan's two porthole eyes and its turbine heart.
7. Objectives: Rustjaw's two shoulder junk launchers, the Leviathan's two vent hatches, the Maw's three stolen lights round its pit; each red lamp goes dark with a burst when broken.
8. Phases (DEV "Boss to 1 HP" shows both at once; joining mid-fight shows the plates already gone): Rustjaw's headlights and weld seams go from headlight white to orange to red-hot, the Maw's stripes and light streams from cyan to violet to hot pink, the Leviathan's portholes and turbine ring from aqua to amber to red. With the meshes, plates fall off at 66% (Rustjaw's shin doors and hood panels, the Maw's flank shell, the Leviathan's stone cheek fins) and 33% (Rustjaw's chest doors, forearm plate and crane cab, the Maw's shoulder and brow plates, the Leviathan's stone neck sleeves): machines burst in sparks and plates, the Maw in light, never anything else.
9. Look effects: welding sparks fly from Rustjaw's seams, claw and exhaust tips; streaks of light fly into the Maw's mouth and the screen dims and cools as you near its pit (back to normal as you leave); sea mist drifts off the Leviathan's turbine, hatches and horn. All three only while it lands or fights.
10. Entrance: as it lands, a burst in its look's colour (orange, cyan, aqua) after the slam; fly to the arena: the name card reads RUSTJAW PRIME / THE JUNKYARD TITAN, LUMEN MAW / THE LIGHT EATER or LEVIATHAN ENGINE / THE DEEP MACHINE with the slash bar in the same colour; every player's BREAKING toast and corner TV read e.g. "BREAKING: IT'S RUSTJAW PRIME, THE JUNKYARD TITAN! HEROES AND VILLAINS ON THE WAY".
11. Performance: 9,300 to 13,200 triangles a boss (one at a time) and one texture set; before the import 19 to 27 plain parts; the frame time with a boss on screen is about what it was, on PC and on a phone (Low preset too).
### Origin lines: The Ashgrove Test, Starblood Calling, The Last Owner, Suit Recall, The Taken Master (2026-11-17, nate's lore, build plan step 11)
Start: `rojo serve`, Play. Make five heroes, one per origin (Lab, Alien, Magic, Tech, Training), or change one hero's origin in the creator. Tap the Dev panel's "SQ chain:" button until it reads each line's name (starts it at step 1); "SQ: start step N", "SQ: finish step" and "SQ: finish chain" as before. Play each line once as the Vigil, once as the Dread Syndicate and once in a squad with one of each (both of that origin).
1. QUESTS page: an ORIGIN LINES heading with only your own origin's line (the other four never show). It opens after the Safehouse mission at level 5; ACCEPT from the page or from the giver. A hero of another origin holding B on that giver hears the "nothing for you" line.
2. Sites: ASHGROVE LABS (a Downtown block south-east of the plaza, not on the museum or opera runners' roofs), THE MOTH & MOON CURIOS (an Old Town shop, far from Lark's flat, the Opal Room and the Torch Crew garage), RADIO HILL (the first terrace of the hill west of the city, on its town side: Ruth and the sign stand on the terrace, not inside the rock), WILLOW STREET DOJO (a Suburbs shop west of the hub) and TINKER'S GARAGE (a Suburbs house south of the hub). Each giver stands at the door, not in a wall or a car lane; the map marks each site.
3. The Ashgrove Test (Lab): Dr. Okafor (lab coat, locs, spectacles portrait). Find the volunteer / chase the keycard courier; shield Okafor 20 s / lift 3 Kindling cells. Inside Ashgrove Labs: clear reception, hold the test chamber in the Kindler's ring of light, take the stairs through the sealed door down to the sublevel, smash 4 Kindlers, beat the containment Grappler. Step 5: the comic THE ASHGROVE TEST, then carry a volunteer / race the cores. Finale: the hollowayRig scene and the card DOCTOR HOLLOWAY / DIRECTOR OF ASHGROVE LABS: a 7-stud Warden in a lab coat with steel plates, a gear rig and a visor, front shield, Light-path attacks, never cracks. Reward: Test Passed and the Kindling Core (chest).
4. Starblood Calling (Alien): Radio Ruth on the hill. Hold the hilltop beacon 20 s / grab 3 star-metal shards; drive off the hunter drones / chase one; find the pod / sell the shards; guard the pod 25 s / race the core (the comic STARBLOOD). Finale on the hill: voidhoundLands and the card THE VOIDHOUND / HUNTER FROM THE STARS: an 8-stud Captain in a starfield shell, antenna helm, one-eyed visor and meteor cape, Pulse attacks, rallying its drones. Reward: Starborn and the Starfall Wings (back).
5. The Last Owner (Magic): Auntie Moth (hooded cape portrait) at her shop. Find Ambrose (his beard portrait) / pick 3 trunks; photograph 3 shadow bots / chase the stagehand; escort the show van out of Old Town / fence the gold; stop the troupe / race to the vault (the comic THE LAST OWNER with TING! and POOF!). Finale: morrowCurtain and the card MORROW THE MIRAGE / THE JEALOUS ILLUSIONIST in Madame Masque-a-rade's suit (tuxedo, top hat, masquerade mask, high-collared cape), no FAKE crack. Reward: Relic Keeper and the Moonstone Circlet (head).
6. Suit Recall (Tech): Tinker Tess (overalls, goggles) at the garage. Chase off the repo bots / steal back the parts; escort her parts truck / chase the tow keys; photograph the fake orders / sell the tow truck's parts; guard Tess 20 s / race the yard's gear (the comic SUIT RECALL). Finale: clawbackClaim and the card CLAWBACK / THE REPO KING: an 8-stud Grappler in an exo frame, ball cap and iron gauntlets whose hook pulls you in, Gadgeteer attacks. Reward: No Returns and the Clawback Gauntlet (hands).
7. The Taken Master (Training): Kiko (gi, headband) at the dojo. Find the witness / swipe 3 purses; both run Hoshi's 5-ring rooftop course. Inside the Iron Hall: the locker room, hold the training floor 20 s, the iron stairs down to the inner dojo, break 4 chains round Hoshi's cage, beat the Iron Gatekeeper (a Warden: hit its side). Step 5: the comic THE TAKEN MASTER, carry Hoshi home / ransom his scroll. Finale: galeChallenge and the card GALE / THE MASTER'S FALLEN STUDENT in Redline's suit (sash, headband, wraps, wind scarf), dashing fast. Reward: Lesson Learned and the Master's Belt (belt).
8. Portraits: Okafor and Holloway wear spectacles, Kiko, Hoshi and Gale headbands; Holloway, the Voidhound, Morrow and Clawback speak in the ON AIR style.
9. Seen set: the ten scenes use wide seen bits 192 to 201 and can be skipped once seen, after a rejoin too.
### Character lines: The Last Partner, Finding Lark, Hostile Takeover, The Scoop (2026-11-18, nate's lore, build plan step 11)
Start: `rojo serve`, Play. Tap the Dev panel's "SQ chain:" button until it reads each line's name (starts it at step 1); "SQ: start step N", "SQ: finish step" and "SQ: finish chain" as before. Play the three two-sided lines once as the Vigil, once as the Dread Syndicate and once in a squad with one of each; Hostile Takeover as a Syndicate villain, alone and with a second villain.
1. QUESTS page: a CHARACTER LINES heading with the four lines. None has an ACCEPT button (they are taken only from the giver in person): The Last Partner after mission 4 at level 8, Finding Lark after mission 3 at level 6, Hostile Takeover after mission 2 at level 6 (a Vigil hero never sees it offered), The Scoop after mission 6 at level 10. The WHERE line names the site and its district (Downtown, Uptown, Old Town, the Docks).
2. Sites: THE NIGHT OWL DINER (a low Downtown block south-east of the plaza), VESPER'S PENTHOUSE (a tall Uptown tower), LARK'S OLD FLAT, THE OPAL ROOM and THE TORCH CREW GARAGE (three Old Town buildings far apart), THE SPARK POUND (a Docks warehouse), BHN 24 STUDIOS (a Downtown tower). Each giver stands on the street at its door, not in a wall, a car lane or the Safehouse or Foundry doors, and the map marks each. No site sits on another chain's building or square.
3. The Last Partner: Lodestar in his own look (the Safehouse's) at the diner. Step 1's diner scene (his line, Patch's). Uptown: the Vigil photograph 3 escort drones, the Syndicate grab 3 gold awards (one guarded). Back at the diner: the Vigil find the fire captain, the Syndicate chase a Sneak Bot; then the Vigil hold the roof ring 20 s (the two-star signal), the Syndicate race 4 rings. Step 5 in Uptown: the comic THE EVENING STAR (two stars, the diner, the fire with FWOOM!, a TV); the Vigil scrap 4 drones and a Shield Bearer, the Syndicate deliver the chip.
4. The Last Partner finale: the twoStars scene, then the card VESPER / THE EVENING STAR. Vesper wears her own look (starfield suit, star cape, long white hair, a star mark) and flies and fights like Radiant; at low health her face cracks to metal and her plate turns red FAKE over VESPER. No "Radiant unmasked" headline on the TV or ticker for it. Reward: the title True North and the Twin Star Cape (back).
5. Finding Lark: Patch's relay (a box on a post with a cyan screen and a blinking aerial, PATCH'S RELAY over it) outside Lark's flat; hold B on it: Patch's offer in his radio portrait. Step 1's flat scene; Lark's radio line. Steps 2 to 4 (find the neighbour / bounty files; photograph the net vans / chase the catcher bot at the pound; fight at the gate / hold 20 s). Step 5: the comic THE SPARK POUND, then walk into the Docks warehouse: the intake office (3 Burglar Bots, a Shield Bearer), the kennel hall hold (Spark Drone waves), smash 4 collar scanners, reach the cell, the warden (a big Grappler) with 2 drones. Robots never stand on the roof.
6. Finding Lark ends: carry a freed Spark to the medics / race the money out; the netGun scene and the card CATCHPOLE / THE SPARK CATCHER: a big man in a ranger harness, duster coat and tracker lens who lobs like the Harvest King, never cracks. Reward: Found Family and the Relay Visor (head).
7. Hostile Takeover (villains only): a Vigil hero holding B on Duchess Vanta hears "A hero in the Opal Room? You're lost, darling..." and gets nothing. A villain before level 6 hears her "Not yet, darling..." line. Then: the Opal Room scene, fetch the protection money at the garage, chase the bookkeeper bot, deliver the ledger (the comic THE TORCH CREW), fight at the garage, the torchOut scene and the card SAL SCORCH / BOSS OF THE TORCH CREW (magma suit, flame gloves and hair; Redline's dashes). Two villains share every step. Reward: Fireproof and the Scorchproof Gauntlets (hands).
8. The Scoop: Nora Bright at BHN 24 Studios. The news desk scene; find the eyewitness / fetch the cut tapes; escort the news van / chase the key bot; photograph the blackout drones / deliver to a rival channel; then both hold the roof uplink 25 s (the comic THE STORIES THAT NEVER AIRED with CUT!). The onAir scene and the card THE CENSOR / HE DECIDES WHAT YOU SEE: a tuxedo, a black cape and a black mono visor, Masque-a-Rade's shadow moves. Reward: Front Page and the Press Vest (chest).
9. Portraits: Vanta's pearls, Nora's mic and the Censor's black bar draw on their guide portraits; Lark is a radio portrait like Patch's; Vesper and the Censor speak in the ON AIR style.
10. Seen set: the twelve scenes use wide seen bits 160 to 171 and can be skipped once seen, after a rejoin too.
### Chain 7: The Sunken City, Underwater (2026-11-17, nate's lore, build plan step 11)
Start: `rojo serve`, Play. Tap the Dev panel's "SQ chain:" button until it reads "SQ chain: The Sunken City" (starts it at step 1); "SQ: start step N" jumps through it, "SQ: finish step" and "SQ: finish chain" as before. Play it once as the Vigil, once as the Dread Syndicate, then once in a squad with one of each.
1. Before Act 1's Safehouse: the QUESTS page lists THE SUNKEN CITY "After the mission Safehouse"; after it, under level 12 it says so; at 12 it opens. The Journal says it is on the Coral Shelf.
2. The Reef Station: Doc Pearl (wetsuit, flight goggles, air tanks, flippers) stands on the seabed of the Coral Shelf south of the city, about 10 studs down, with the REEF STATION sign beside her; she is not inside a reef, kelp stand or wreck, and far from the sunken caches, the commuter rescues and the Leviathan Engine's arena. Same spot on every server.
3. Hold B on Pearl: her offer line (each side its own). Step 1: talk again; the lights scene plays (a dolly over the reef, then an orbit; Pearl's line, then the Tidewright's).
4. Step 2: the Vigil fight 4 Divers and an Anchor in the open water near the station; the Syndicate grab 3 relics off the seabed, one guarded by 2 Divers. Nothing is put on a street, a roof or under the city.
5. Step 3: the Vigil find Dace, the lost salvage diver, in the search circle on the seabed (his line at step 4); the Syndicate chase a Diver carrying the gate key: it swims straight legs through the water (no hops) and loops until caught.
6. Step 4 at the Drowned Gate (deep water, about 42 studs down, well away from the station): the Vigil take the scanner to the pad; the Syndicate hold the ring at the gate for 25 s. Step 5: the comic page THE LAST GUARDIAN OF VELMARA (4 panels: bronze towers by the bay, the storm (WHOOSH!), the guardian waiting, the salvage drill (KRAK!)).
7. Inside Velmara (an air dome, you walk): start at the drowned gate with the sea glowing behind it; clear 2 Anchors and 2 Torpedo Sentries in the west market street; hold the fountain (a bronze statue of the Tidewright) for 20 s against Anchor waves; walk the great stair down (no jump needed); jam the 4 tide engines (3 hits each); beat the engine warden (a big-health Anchor) and its 2 Torpedo Sentries. No Diver in the dome. Robots never stand on the engine hall's ceiling.
8. Step 6 back at the Reef Station: the Vigil carry a stranded reef keeper out in the water back to the pad by the station; the Syndicate swim 4 rings in 45 s (rings at mid depth, all in open water). Step 7 at the Tide Spire: the Vigil hold a ring for 25 s; the Syndicate search the seabed for the treasury.
9. Step 8: the rising scene (the Tidewright's line, Patch's), then the name card THE TIDEWRIGHT / LAST GUARDIAN OF VELMARA once. The Tidewright: a tall bronze-and-teal guardian in plated armour, a long cape, an iron crown, fin ears, a full beard and glowing ice eyes, a bronze trident in his right hand; he fights as a heavy sea brute (slams) with 4 Divers. His name plate reads like a hero's (no robot tag), never cracks and never reads FAKE. The slow-mo finisher plays on him.
10. The end: Pearl's done line, Credits and Renown, side rep on every step and more on the finale, the title "Tide Turner" and the Coral Crest head style (a coral fin crest) in GEAR (never in the shop or loot). Talking to Pearl again: nothing more.
11. Co-op (one Vigil, one Syndicate together): split steps show each their own goal and wait; Velmara and the finale are shared.
12. Seen set (save v12): a save from before this build keeps every scene it has seen (the opening's, the missions', chains 1 to 6). The three Sunken City scenes use the new wide seen bits 128 to 130 and can be skipped once seen, after a rejoin too.
13. Underwater check: every goal marker, ring, pad and search circle in this chain is under the water on the Coral Shelf or the open sea ring, never in the harbour basin, the Sunken Line, the Trench or the arena.
### Chain 6: The Pit, the Old Quarry (2026-11-17, nate's lore, build plan step 11)
Start: `rojo serve`, Play. Tap the Dev panel's "SQ chain:" button until it reads "SQ chain: The Pit" (starts it at step 1); "SQ: start step N" jumps through it, "SQ: finish step" and "SQ: finish chain" as before. Before Act 1's Safehouse: the QUESTS page lists THE PIT "After the mission Safehouse"; after it, under level 20 it reads LEVEL 20; at level 20 it reads "Talk to BRICK in THE OLD QUARRY, the Old Quarry" with its page line, and the map shows a gold "THE OLD QUARRY" square at the quarry's north gate and Brick's "!", plus THE MINE LIFT and THE QUARRY FLOOR markers inside.
2. The gate: Brick stands in the gap of the quarry's north wall, facing town, beside THE OLD QUARRY sign, in his look (tank top, track bottoms, hand wraps, beanie, buzz cut, boxed beard, whistle). Nothing blocks the gate; the sign does not stand in the way of walking in.
3. Hold B on Brick: his offer line (each side its own). Step 1: talk again; the quarry night scene plays (a dolly then an orbit; the Pitboss's line in red ON AIR style, then Brick's).
4. Step 2: the Vigil find Flint in the search circle; the Syndicate grab 3 cash bags, one guarded by 2 Burglar Bots. Step 3 at the mine lift: the Vigil photograph 3 bouncer bots; the Syndicate chase a Sneak Bot. Step 4 at the gate: the Vigil fight 3 Burglar Bots and a Brute Bot; the Syndicate deliver the book to the drop. The ranges land on open ground (on the quarry floor or just outside the gate), never inside a wall or on a terrace edge.
5. Step 5: Flint's line, then the comic page THE BOSS OF THE PIT (4 panels: the quarry, a stone fist with CRACK!, the ring with DING DING!, the ring again); stand in the purple ring at the mine lift.
6. Inside the Pit: you start on the gallery by the lift; clear the bouncer bots in the mine tunnel (west); hold the betting hall's ring for 20 s while Burglar Bots come in waves; smash the 4 odds machines; walk the rock steps down to the sand (no jump needed); step into the ring on its stage (red glowing ropes, floodlights, bench tiers, the box seat); beat the house champion (a big-health Brute Bot) and its 3 Burglar Bots. Robots stand on the gallery and floor, never inside the benches.
7. Step 6: the Vigil carry a fighter to the pad; the Syndicate fly 4 rings in 45 s; the Pitboss's "My Pit!" line. Step 7 on the quarry floor: the Vigil hold a ring for 25 s; the Syndicate grab 3 belt crates, one guarded by a Shield Bearer.
8. Step 8: the fight night scene (his line, Patch's), then the name card THE PITBOSS / HEAD OF THE GRAVEL FAMILY once. The Pitboss is the Bramblebeast's suit dressed in his own look: a titan-size man with grey stone skin, a top hat, tuxedo, handlebar moustache, metal boxing gloves and shoulder pads, a gold champion belt, his plate reading THE PITBOSS / Head of the Gravel Family (never Bramblebeast, no leaves, no glow), red-and-gold suit colours. He slams, shockwaves and charges; 2 Brute Bots with him. He sparks apart like a machine (no light burst); no face crack, no FAKE plate. A player who joins mid-fight sees the same look. The slow-mo finisher plays on him. Then the Harvest King chain's Bramblebeast still looks like the Bramblebeast.
9. The end: Brick's done line, Credits and Renown, side rep on every step and more on the finale, the title "Undisputed" and the Undisputed Belt (a gold buckle belt) in GEAR (never in the shop or loot). Talking to Brick again: nothing more.
10. Co-op (one Vigil, one Syndicate together): split steps show each their own goal and wait; the Pit and the finale are one shared run.
11. Scenes seen once can be skipped next time (their seen bits are 125 to 127).
12. The finale on the quarry floor while the Lumen Maw world boss is up: both fights still work (the arena is shared).

The Drowned Gull sits in its pool with glowing sails. Robots never stand on the grotto's ceiling.
8. Step 6: the Vigil carry Finn to the pad (Finn's line that the ghost ship is just lights); the Syndicate fly 4 rings in 45 s; Brine's "my ghost lights!" line. Step 7: the Vigil hold a ring for 20 s; the Syndicate dig up 3 caches, one guarded by 2.
9. Step 8: the Brine scene (his line, Patch's), then the name card CAPTAIN BRINE / CAPTAIN OF THE SALT DOGS once. Captain Brine (a heavy hero-look pirate in a metal rig, wide brim hat, eye patch, white beard, maroon duster, an iron anchor in his right hand) slams with a warning ring, every 3rd slam a wide wave ring, and charges down a lane; 4 Salt Dogs with him. His coat tears over his last half of health; no face crack, no FAKE plate. The slow-mo finisher plays on him.

### Chain 1: the Hero Academy (2026-11-17, nate's lore, build plan step 11)
Start: `rojo serve`, Play. The Dev panel has "SQ chain: The Hero Academy" (starts it at step 1), "SQ: start step N" (jumps to a step of the tracked chain), "SQ: finish step" and "SQ: finish chain". Play it once as the Vigil and once as the Dread Syndicate, then once in a squad with one of each.

Dev "Act 1 done" (or clear Safehouse): the row reads "Talk to JANITOR GUS in HALO ACADEMY, Downtown" with its page line under it, and the map shows a gold "HALO ACADEMY" square and Gus's "!".
2. The building: west of the Beacon Tower's plaza along the main street (the 141-stud Downtown block two blocks west, its east face toward the plaza). It is an ordinary city building (wreckable like the rest). Gus stands at its plaza-side door next to the white HALO ACADEMY sign with a gold halo on top; nothing is in the ceremony area, the chase row or by the Safehouse stairwell. Gus wears his look: flannel shirt, cargo trousers, ball cap, mutton chops, utility belt.
3. Hold B on Gus: his offer line plays in the guide box with his portrait (a different line for each side). Step 1: talk to him again; the academy gates scene plays in a quiet moment (an orbit, then a push up; Headmaster Halo's ON AIR line, then Gus's line).
4. Step 2: the Vigil photograph 3 recruiter bots (camera range like the Stakeout contract); the Syndicate pick up 3 Spark files, one guarded by 2 Burglar Bots. Step 3: the Vigil walk the field trip bus home (the motorcade box, PLACEHOLDER ART: it only rolls while you are near); the Syndicate catch the bursar bot hopping across the roofs. Lodestar and Patch each have a line.
5. Step 4: the Vigil find Wren in the search circle; the Syndicate run the files to the drop pad. Step 5: Wren's line, then her comic page (4 panels: the school, the honours class, the scanner and the copy robot with ZZZAP!, the city); stand in the purple ring by Gus.
6. Inside the academy pocket: you start in the entrance hall by Halo's statue on the school floor; clear the hall monitor bots in the west classroom (desks, blackboard); hold the ring in the training hall's middle for 20 s while drone waves come; go through the gap in the back wall by the bookcase and walk the hidden stairs down (no jump needed); under the school, smash the 4 scanner pods (Vigil text "Free the kids!", Syndicate text "Rip the copier cores"), then beat the foreman (a Grappler) and its 2 drones. You come back to the city and the step pays. Robots never stand on the workshop's ceiling.
7. Step 6: the Vigil carry a freed kid to the pad; the Syndicate fly 4 rings in 45 s. Halo's speaker line plays. Step 7: the Vigil hold a ring on a roof for 20 s (Patch's upload); the Syndicate loot 3 safes, one guarded by a Shield Bearer.
8. Step 8: the bell scene (Halo's line, Patch's line), then walk to the marker: the name card HEADMASTER HALO / THE FAKE TEACHER shows once. Halo flies, fires bolts in 3s and dives at a ring under you every 8 s (Mind path shots), with 2 Shield Bearers and 2 drones. At half health his face cracks to metal, his plate turns to FAKE in red, the slow-mo finisher plays and every screen's corner TV reads "... CRACKS HEADMASTER HALO'S FACE. IT'S A ROBOT FAKE". He wears his look: white suit, azure cape, a halo, a pencil moustache.
9. The end: the chain's toast, Gus's done line, Credits and Renown, the title "Class Dismissed" on the title list and the Cracked Halo head style in GEAR (never in the shop or loot). The creator's chains:1 pieces (Heart Badge, Tool Belt) unlock. Talking to Gus again: he has nothing more.
10. Co-op (one Vigil, one Syndicate in a squad near each other): split steps show each their own goal and wait for each other; the pocket and the finale are one shared run for both; both saves finish together.
11. The lore step 10 list's step 9 now reads: with sideQuestSample off the QUESTS page lists The Hero Academy (no TEST GIVER).

### Lore step 10: side quest framework (2026-11-17)
Start: `rojo serve`, Play. The Dev panel has the SQ buttons. Set Config.Dev.sideQuestSample = true for steps 2 to 9.
1. An old save with side quests (Mayor finished, Pip on step 2): on load the "Old side quests retired" toast; the Gold Sash and Salute stay; 200 Credits for Pip's finished step, once (rejoin: no second payout). The Journal pin falls back to the next mission.
2. The TEST GIVER stands in the plaza with a "!" over it and a "!" dot on the map. Hold the prompt: its lines play through the guide box with its portrait and blip; ACCEPT on the Journal side quest page starts the chain and the tracker shows "Framework sample 1/5".
3. Walk each step as the Vigil and again as the Syndicate: step 2 is a fight for heroes and a delivery for villains; step 3 an incident or a chase. The tracker text and the map dot follow the step.
4. Step 4: the pocket ring takes you to the arena pocket; defeat the wave and the Captain; you come back to the city and the step pays.
5. Step 5: walk to the finale marker; THE TEST VILLAIN name card shows once; the boss has more health; when it falls the chain finishes with the toast, the pay and the Bow emote.
6. Journal HOLD mid-chain: the tracker clears; RESUME picks up the same step.
7. Co-op: two players in a squad (one Vigil, one Syndicate) on step 2: each sees their own goal; the step completes for each on their own goal, and the shared steps wait for both ("Your part is done. Help the others finish theirs").
8. Dev panel: give sample chain, start step N, restart, finish step, finish chain, forget sample and each "SQ kind: X" (16 kinds) set up a goal that can be finished.
9. Set sideQuestSample = false: no TEST GIVER and no dots; the Journal side quest page shows "New side quest chains are on their way".

### Lore step 7: cinematic layer (2026-11-17, nate's lore K1 to K3)
Start: `rojo serve`, Play. The Dev panel (DEV, top right; it scrolls now) has a button for each piece.
1. Prologue (Dev: prologue as the Vigil): the ceremony, scan, rescue and title camera beats play the same shots as before, with the letterbox sliding in and out. First time on a save: no SKIP. Play it again (Dev: prologue): SKIP shows bottom right after a moment on each beat you have seen, and skips it.
2. Act 1 (Dev: mission 2 as each side): each camera beat (the safehouse door, Lodestar) plays the same slow push-in with the letterbox; on a replay SKIP shows and skips (with a squadmate, only once both of you have seen it).
3. Dev "Scene: city": in a quiet spot the camera orbits you, rises and looks out over the city with two Patch lines under the letterbox; it ends by itself (12 s). Tap it again: SKIP now shows. Next to a robot or right after a hit the button reads "Scene waits: enemy/hit" and it plays once things are quiet (within 30 s); a hit during it ends it at once.
4. Dev "Comic: last stand": a full-screen comic page, "THE OLD HEROES' LAST STAND", 4 panels sliding in with ink borders, captions and KRAK!/BOOM! words (PLACEHOLDER ART). SKIP the second time.
5. News: tap Dev "News: boss" six times (boss, boss down, unmask, streak, heist, act 1): the boss and the unmask flash BREAKING NEWS on the corner TV (top centre, under the boss bar), the boss also as a red toast; the rest show on the TV's ticker. Every line reads your hero name (title + noun), never your account name. With nothing new, Crane's segment airs on the TV every 3 minutes (Dev "Crane on TV" now).
6. Big screens: on the plaza (a screen on a frame north of the Hall), on the three Downtown towers nearest the plaza and the Crane billboard on Uptown's landmark: BREAKING NEWS while fresh, then Crane's lines and the ticker in turn; the ticker scrolls near you. Collapse a screen's tower: the screen goes dark until the drones repair it.
7. Boss entrance: Dev boss now. Every player gets the BREAKING toast and the TV; fly to the arena: a 3 s name card (name, title, the red slash slam) shows once as you arrive. Dev "Name card" cycles all four (Bulwark's battle form too); in Live the battle form's card shows as it lands.
8. Finishers: Dev "Finisher (mine)" and "(nearby)" in Comic and in Anime style (Dev style switch). Comic: a big impact word and a short hit stop; Anime: a pale freeze frame and speed lines; both slow every hero's animation and the effects for under a second, the camera pushes in; nearby is shorter. Then for real: the killing blow and each phase's end on a world boss, the battle form in Live, an elite robot, a fake's face cracking. nate: judge the feel (length, slow amount, words: Config.Cinema.finisher).
9. Dynamic camera (Settings > Camera motion, or Dev "Camera:"): Full: a slight lean into dashes, a gentle push on crits and heavy hits, a small orbit on supers. Reduced: the same at half. Off: none of it, and no shakes. nate: turn it up and down in a fight and judge each level.
10. Reduce flashing on (with Camera motion Full): no shakes, no hit-pause, no finisher hit stop, freeze or white frame; the impact word, the speed lines and the lean/push/orbit still show.
11. Phone (Device emulator): the letterbox, SKIP, the corner TV and the name card fit the screen and leave the stick and buttons free; frame time steady during a finisher.

### Vigil HQ under the plaza (2026-11-17, nate's lore plan Q1/Q2)
The Vigil's HQ is The Safehouse, the hideout under the plaza; the Beacon Tower is Crane's Hero Hall. Start: `rojo serve`, Play.
1. As the Vigil, stand on the Downtown plaza: at its west edge, north of the middle, a small concrete stairwell head with a steel door, a yellow stripe and a lamp; over it a small blue "V THE SAFEHOUSE" marker (gone past about 70 studs). Nothing is at the foot of Crane's Hero Hall.
2. Walk up: the prompt reads "Go down / The Safehouse" (B); you arrive in the HQ beside the Exit, with the stair coming down from a shaft in the ceiling behind it.
3. Inside: concrete walls and pillars, pipes and cable trays under a concrete ceiling, warm work lamps, monitors on the side walls and a glowing city map behind the command table; no sky or windows anywhere. Every station pad, the Hall of Legends, the dummies, trophies and props are where they were; Lodestar stands near the command table with his Talk prompt. Exit puts you back outside the stairwell.
4. Switch to a Syndicate hero (or a second player): the same door reads "Locked / Service Door", no marker; B shows "Locked tight. Just a maintenance stair" and nothing else. The map (M) has no Safehouse dot for them; the Vigil see a blue dot labelled THE SAFEHOUSE at the stairwell.
5. The Syndicate's Foundry door in the Docks and the Foundry inside look exactly as before.
6. DEV > "Prologue: Vigil": the ceremony crowd, the stage and the rooftop chase never reach the stairwell; the fast-travel beacon on the Hall reads "Crane's Hero Hall" on the map.
7. Play mission 2 (Safehouse): the tunnels have the same concrete colour, pipes, cable trays and lamps as the HQ; robots never stand on the pipes; it reads as the same place.

1. DEV > "Guide: lodestar", then tap it again for patch and crane: a box with the speaker's portrait (Lodestar silver hair, beard, star; Patch cap and headset with faint static bars; Crane slick hair and tie), the name in their colour, RADIO on Patch and ON AIR on Crane, and a different blip for each.

Open world, Guide radio on: Patch speaks on your first incident, the first world boss landing (DEV > boss now), a level-up that opens a mission (DEV > level, e.g. to 4 after Safehouse), after 75 s standing still with the next mission pinned, and a few seconds after Act 1's broadcast. Never two lines within 45 s; none while a mission or the prologue runs. Each line differs as the Syndicate.

9. As the Vigil: enter your HQ: Lodestar stands near the command table; B (or tap Talk) gives a line, again for the next (3 lines, then they repeat); the lines change after Safehouse, after The Copy and after Live. As the Syndicate: The Foundry has no Lodestar; he only appears where the Safehouse deal is offered.

### Weighty hits (2026-11-17, build playtest-hits)
nate: "Make hits weighty" (flash, white, sound, particles, knockback, shake and freeze). Numbers in Config.Combat.weight (tune there).
1. Punch a Grunt with your basic: it flashes white, it and you hold still for a blink, then it jolts back a step and rocks; sparks spray out the far side; a thud and a clank; the camera shakes a little.
2. A heavy skill or a crit: all of it bigger (longer freeze, bigger jolt and shove, more sparks with a ring, a lower thud, a bigger shake).
3. A channel or a damage field (ticks): numbers and flashes only, no shake, freeze or shove, so it doesn't jitter.
4. A big crowd fight (10+ robots): it still feels clean, not a constant buzz; frame time in the Dev panel close to before (phone emulator, Low).
5. Settings > Reduce flashing on: no shake and no freeze; flash, sparks, sound and shove stay.
6. Feel check: if a hit still feels light or now feels too sticky, say which (freeze, shove, shake or sound) and Claude tunes that one number.

5. City: building fronts show brick, office, glass, stone and panel textures with windows (no flat window bands), sidewalks and roads are textured, Docks warehouses are corrugated in colours, Old Town is cobbled with tiled roofs, Uptown has ribbon and prism glass. Smash a building: the falling panels keep their texture.

Start: `rojo serve`, connect the Rojo plugin, press Play (or Test > Clients and Servers with 2 players). The DEV button (top right, Studio only) has the test tools.

### Lore step 5: Act 1 (2026-11-17)
nate's lore, build plan step 5. Play each mission once as the Vigil and once as the Syndicate, then one co-op pair (Test > Clients and Servers, 2 players: one on each side, in a squad). DEV > "Safehouse: Vigil" (tap again for the Syndicate, then the next mission) starts a mission with everything before it cleared; "Mission stage n" jumps the running one to a stage; "Finish step" ends the current stage.
1. Journal > STORY: 1 THE CEREMONY (PROLOGUE), 2 SAFEHOUSE, 3 FRAMED, 4 THE COPY, 5 THE SIGNAL, 6 LIVE, each with a one-line blurb; no START on mission 1; YOUR CHOICES lists Framed, The Copy and The Signal with your own side's labels. No old names anywhere (Shardfall, Null, War-Frame, Hold the Line, Signal Tower, The Informant, Under the Docks).
2. Safehouse: a camera beat (letterbox, the HUD buttons hide, the camera pushes in on the safehouse door): Lodestar stands there in his look and plate; his line, then Patch with the RADIO tag and a blip. Vigil: clear the scanner drones in room 1; Syndicate: smash 3 scanner relays. Then the security line (sentries), the door, and the offer beat (Vigil: "Stand with us"; Syndicate: "Share the hideout"). Result card, back to the city.
3. Framed: a bridge over water. Radiant blames a rogue Spark (subtitles). Vigil holds 3 rescue rings while waves come; Syndicate breaks 3 trucks guarded by sentries. Radiant fights until its face cracks (FAKE), then holds still for the proof beat. The choice card shows your side's two labels. Vigil: Save the Bridge (hit the moving train 30 times) or Chase the Fake (the cracked Radiant hops the towers); Syndicate: Grab the Data Core (chase Radiant) or Grab the Cash (break the van). Crane's news line ends it.
4. The Copy: reach the middle roof; the scan beat focuses on the next roof; your own evil twin appears (your look, your name, your path's moves); beat it; the core choice (Vigil: Spare the Core / Destroy It; Syndicate: Reprogram It (the Echo Drone follows you after) / Sell It).
5. The Signal: climb Crane Tower; the CRANE INSIDER's radio lines; the choice (Vigil: Trust Her / Test Her, Test adds a guard squad on the deck; Syndicate: Share the Feed / Hold It for Ransom); Vigil guards the mast ring 20 s, Syndicate holds the relay ring 20 s, waves for both; the Warden; the on-air beat with Crane's line about Bulwark.
6. Live: Vigil sets up 3 camera rings around the stage; Syndicate breaks 3 stage screens. Crane presents Bulwark (beat). Fight Bulwark: at half health its face cracks and every player in the server (also a third player outside the mission) gets BREAKING: BULWARK IS A ROBOT FAKE. The unmasked beat, then Bulwark's battle form (the boss bar reads BULWARK'S BATTLE FORM): cannons, the shield and 3 back hits, the laser behind pillars. With Destroy It picked, the pillars crackle.
7. After Live's result card you fade back to the city; about 1.5 s later the news screen: CRANE NEWS NETWORK, BREAKING NEWS, HERO REGISTRATION ACT PASSES, three Crane lines, then PUBLIC ENEMY #1 over your hero's name and TO BE CONTINUED; CLOSE leaves early; nothing flashes. On the phone emulator it fits, the text is readable and CLOSE is tappable.
8. Co-op pair (one Vigil, one Syndicate, in a squad; the leader starts it): both enter the same pocket; on split stages each tracker shows its own goal at the same time (rescue rings and trucks together on the bridge), each side gets its own choice card, and whoever finishes first reads "Cover the Syndicate..." or "Let the Vigil...". Shared stages (beats, fakes, boss) play once for both; in The Copy each player fights their own twin. Both are paid; the leader leaving ends it for both; the other leaving alone lets the leader carry on.
9. Replays: a choice already made shows "Your first choice stands"; only the base pay comes. An old save mid-way through the old Act 1 (if nate has one) lands on the mission at the same number.
10. Safety: walk away mid-mission: after twice the mission's minutes plus 5 the run ends and you go home. Get defeated: you come back at the pocket spawn (the battle form keeps its HP). Settings > Reduce flashing: nothing flashes in the beats or the news screen.
11. Output (Analytics in Studio): mission_start and mission_done with the mission id, party size and side; choice_pick with the side; act_done after Live.

### Lore step 3: the Ceremony prologue (2026-11-17)
nate's lore, build plan step 3. About 4 minutes. Play it once as each side, on PC and with the phone emulator (Test > Device: a phone, landscape). DEV > Wipe starts it as a new player (the creator first); DEV > "Prologue: Vigil" or "Prologue: Syndicate" starts it again as that side; "Beat: ..." jumps to the next beat (tap again for the one after).
1. After the creator's DONE: you stand in the crowd before Crane's Hero Hall in plain clothes (hoodie and jeans, your own face and hair). Letterbox bars, no HUD. The camera shows the crowd and stage, then Silas Crane at the podium (his name plate), then Radiant, Bulwark and Skylance in their hero looks with hero plates (name and title, no robot tag). Crane's five lines show as subtitles with his name in gold. The crowd cheers, the fakes wave.
2. The scan: a red sweep crosses the crowd and stops on you; SPARK DETECTED fills the screen; the crowd turns to stare; Crane: "Well, well..."
3. The run: the HUD shows only Leap; a marker leads down the street while the crowd flees and fades; the fakes vanish in a burst.
4. The Spark (Basic pulses): Vigil: a sign shakes loose over a kid; press Basic (or wait 6 s): a bolt, a shield bubble, the sign bounces off; the kid runs. Syndicate: a man in red near you; Basic sends him flying across the plaza in a cartoon arc with a big word; he lands, sits a beat and runs off. Crane's line differs by side.
5. A quick fade: you are on the first roof of the rooftop row (six purpose-built roofs in a line across the street from the crowd, east of the Hall: AC units, a tank, a billboard frame, skylights, a washing line). Press Leap: a slow, high arc to the next roof with your path's super look, a tinted slow-motion moment and a FOV push. Patch's first radio lines (RADIO tag, cyan name, a blip). Skylance appears one roof behind.
6. The chase: four more Leaps along the row, one marked roof at a time (two short hops, the long one over the street, two more); each roof's middle is clear to land on and the washing and billboard face are walk-through. Skylance keeps one roof behind; red rings warn where spears land (always a stride away; nothing hits). Patch has four chase lines (the first, "Roof to roof" and "Almost clear" differ by side). The chase should take about 20 to 30 s; a full prologue still about 4 to 5 minutes. Also free roam: the row is there for everyone, and a blast on a row roof wrecks it and the drones repair it like any building.
7. On the last roof the suit snaps on piece by piece (feet up to the head), each piece with a pop and a click, then SUIT UP.
8. Glide down to the subway steps (marker): a fade into a dark tunnel. Slabs fall on a timer with warning rings; under one you are knocked back (no damage); the rubble is solid: Leap over it.
9. The chamber: Skylance fights you (Basic, two skills and Leap on the HUD). At half health its face cracks (FAKE). The fight ends there.
10. Patch: "it's METAL!" A squad of Crane Troopers arrives; your health falls but never below about a fifth; the fight is lost on purpose.
11. Lodestar crashes down beside you (a shockwave, a big word, a shake), his plate LODESTAR / THE VIGIL; his line differs by side; Patch: "Old Star!"; he steps in and grabs you.
12. Cut to the Crane Industries billboard (Crane's smiling face, CRANE INDUSTRIES, the slogan), Crane's voice line, then the CAPELINE title card. Then you are back in the plaza in your suit with the normal HUD; level 3, Flight and a Skill Point; the Journal pins mission 2.
13. Safety: stand still in any beat: it moves on by itself (the run after 45 s, the chase after 90 s); after about 5.5 minutes it jumps to the title. Reset your character mid-prologue: you come back where the beat starts.
14. Returning account: make a second hero: the main menu shows CREATE FIRST (straight to the city with the rewards) and PLAY THE PROLOGUE (the ceremony starts then); on that replay SKIP shows on the camera beats only.
15. A hero who finished the old opening never sees it; an old save that stopped mid-opening starts the prologue from the ceremony.
16. Settings > Reduce flashing on: no flashes or shakes; the slow-motion tint is softer. On the phone: the subtitles sit under the top bar clear of the stick, SKIP is a full-size button, the crowd is smaller.
17. Output: spark_detected, first_spark, fake_cracked and opening_done print in order (Analytics in Studio).

### Lore step 4: famous fakes and your twin (2026-11-17)
nate's lore, build plan step 4. Fakes and twins are elite mini-bosses: expect a long fight (about 10 s to down a level-matched hero if you stand still).
1. DEV > "Fake: Radiant": a golden hero (Chevron Suit, long cape, halo, sparkle trail) appears 25 studs in front, flying. Its plate reads RADIANT with "The Golden Dawn" under it, with no robot tag. No metal shows yet. It fires bursts of gold light bolts (Light's look) and every few seconds marks a red ring under you and dives into it (a dash streak, then a slam).
2. Hit it to half health: the face cracks (a steel half and a glowing red eye on the head), a click sound, a white flash and rings with a "FAKE!" pop, and a short hit-stop if you are within 60 studs. The plate turns red: FAKE over RADIANT. Below half the costume pieces break off and the robot shell shows like any robot's, all metal near defeat.
3. DEV > "Fake: Bulwark": a giant titan in plated armour (The Unbreakable). Hitting it from the front is half blocked (the guard); hit from behind or break the guard. From range it charges in a straight line (a red lane first) and stops at walls; close up it slams, and every third slam is a big shockwave you can jump over.
4. DEV > "Fake: Skylance": a slim hero with a spear (The Silver Streak). It keeps its distance, aims (a laser) and throws a fast steel spear, then dashes to one side (Blade afterimages) before the next throw. It never dashes through walls.
5. DEV > "My evil twin": a copy of your own hero (same pieces, colours, materials, build and height) appears in front. Its plate shows your hero name and origin title (no @name) until it cracks, then FAKE over your name. It fights with your path, drawn in your path's look and colour: bolt bursts for Volt, Frost, Fire, Tide, Pulse, Mind, Spirit or Light, grenades for Earth or Gadgeteer, aimed shots and a side dash for Gunslinger, cloaked stabs for Shadow, quick sword strikes for Blade, slams, shockwave and charge for Titan, Martial, Juggernaut and Colossus. Try heroes on 2 or 3 paths.
6. With 2 players: the second player sees the twin in the first player's look and name, including after joining late. A twin of a hero in classic clothes shows the clothes' colours (rigs have no Humanoid).
7. The "3x" robot button skips the fakes. A defeated fake or twin leaves no plate or parts behind.
8. Once nate uploads the cracked faces (Placeholder art): the crack shows the uploaded face and the part-built overlay stays hidden.

### Lore step 2: creator first and origins (2026-11-17)

Build plan step 2 (https://claude.ai/artifact/9F2uydBzqv4KhcPG1aFdp5). The old opening still runs after the creator until step 3 replaces it. DEV > Wipe starts over as a brand-new player.
1. Brand-new player (DEV > Wipe, or a fresh test account): about 1.5 s after loading the creator opens at once, before any cinematic or story: SIDE first (The Vigil or The Dread Syndicate), then ORIGIN, MOBILITY, POWERS, WEAPON, APPEARANCE, PLAY. No robots, incidents or events bother the hero while it is open. Nothing from your Roblox avatar shows on the hero.
2. ORIGIN tab: five rows (Lab accident, Alien blood, Magic relic, Tech suit, Training), each with its title on the right (Lab-Born, Star-Blooded, Relic-Bound, Suit-Forged, Self-Made). Hovering (PC) shows the line and the crest on the preview; tapping picks it and puts that origin's crest on the chest (the Symbol slot; change it under APPEARANCE). The card says "Looks only: every origin has the same Spark and the same stats". RANDOMIZE on this tab picks a random origin.
3. DONE without an origin says "Pick an origin (ORIGIN)". With everything picked, DONE saves; a toast unlocks the crest ("Lab Crest"); then the cinematic and the opening start. At the shard the opening asks nothing: a Vigil hero gets the save-her step, a Syndicate villain the grab-the-shard escape. At the end of the run (Suit Up) nothing opens: level 3, Flight and the Skill Point arrive and the guided flight starts.
4. Check the hero's power numbers (Dev panel stats) with two heroes of different origins and the same path: identical.
5. Another player's name plate shows the origin title before "@name" (e.g. "Lab-Born  @Player").
6. Returning hero: open the creator at a safehouse or HQ: ORIGIN shows your origin; pick another and DONE: it saves free (no cost), the plate title changes. SIDE says "picked at the start" and cannot change.
7. Old save: a hero made before this update loads with the Training origin (Self-Made) and the Dojo Crest unlocked on join (toast); nothing else about it changed.
8. New hero on a returning account (HQ > a new character slot): the creator opens first; after DONE the main menu offers CREATE FIRST (straight to the city, no cinematic, no disaster) or PLAY THE OPENING. The PLAY, SHOP, SETTINGS menu does not pop up after DONE.
9. Street outfit: DEV > "Street outfit: off" toggles it. On: the hero wears the slate hoodie and navy jeans with its own face, hair and skin; no suit, cape, mask, gloves, boots, emblem, aura, gear looks or weapon. Respawn: still on. Off: the designed look comes back exactly. Abilities and stats work the same either way.
10. Phone (Device Emulator): the creator's 7 step tabs fit on one row and every ORIGIN row is tappable.

### New robot types: review fixes (2026-11-17)
The review's fixes (Decisions.md, 2026-11-17 robots review). DEV > robot button cycles the types.
1. Sniper: let it start its laser on you, then stun it (any stun power): the red laser, the red screen edge and the lock sound all stop, and no round comes. Do the same with a Pull: no round right after the pull ends.
2. Medic Unit: stun it while its green beam is on a robot: the beam goes out at once (not up to a second later).
3. Grappler: stun it while the hook is flying: the hook reels back and you are not pulled. Defeat one while the hook flies: the hook vanishes and you are not pulled.
4. Grappler on a flier at 30+ studs: you are pulled to it and down, the speed check never pulls you back (Output has no MoveCorrect), and you land on the street, not inside it.
5. Interceptor: fly so it chases you, then land: for 2 s it may still shoot, but its net never comes at you on the ground.
6. A site with a Mimic (level 9+ incident or event; or DEV robot Mimic next to a site): defeat the rest of the squad from far away or while hovering over it: the Mimic shows itself (glitch burst) and can be hit, so the site finishes.
7. DEV robot button with a type switched off in Config.Enemies (on = false): the button skips it.
8. Swarmer or Captain next to a building (DEV robot at a wall): no Swarmer or escort stands on a roof.
### More activities (2026-11-17)
DEV panel, all buttons near the bottom: "Inc: <name>" starts that incident at your feet and moves to the next, "Inc next" skips one; "Ev: <name>" and "Ev next" the same for events; "Con: <name>" starts that contract for you and "Con next" skips; "Heroic OFF/ON" makes the next ones Heroic. Props are plain parts (PLACEHOLDER ART). Press "Level 50" first so every robot type is unlocked (use "Level +5" from a fresh save to check level 40).

Captain warband: down the Captain (beat sound) and it is done. Mimic crowd: 6 bystanders stand still, one is the Mimic; hitting it shows it. Bomber barrage: each shell shows a red ring where it will land; stand in it to block the shell (5 blocks wins); 3 misses fails. Grappler tow: the cable pulls a car away; down the Grappler, then carry the driver. Scaffold, crane load, depot raid: hold the ring by the prop. Water main and signal jam: break the valve or relay.

Airship drop over a downtown, Bridge siege in Old Town, Stadium lockdown (3 gates) downtown, Power plant overload (4 clamps) and Harbour fog in the Docks (fog banks and grey colours only while you stand in the Docks), Parade gone wrong (a float drives through the streets). With the enemies9 merge, check some downtown events land in Uptown too.

Bounty shows a name (e.g. "Bounty: Rust Baron") and its elite robot is much tougher; Demolition: break 3 pylons; Rooftop courier: grab the parcel and touch 5 rooftop drops within 75 s (a clock on the tracker); Defusal (hero only): reach the charge before the fuse runs out and hold the prompt; Photo recon: take a Photo Mode shot within 14 studs of each outpost; Generator hold: stay in the ring 45 s while waves come.

### New robot types: looks and effects (2026-11-17)
Needs the server AI for the nine new robots (each one spawns in the ambient mix and in open-world squads). Best with a second player to see the effects from outside.
1. Shield Bearer: a big dark riot shield with a window and a gold stripe held out in front on the left arm; a hit from the side or back knocks it away in a burst of chips and the arm drops; it comes back when the guard does.
2. Captain: a peaked officer's cap with a gold badge over its cape; on a rally its arm goes up, then a gold ring of the rally's size spreads with a "RALLY!" pop, and the robots in it get gold eyes and core and a gold chevron over the head until the rally ends.
3. Sniper: a long rifle held low in both hands and a glowing visor; while it aims the rifle is shouldered and a thin red laser runs from the rifle tip to the hero's chest and follows them; for the last part it stops following, thickens and blinks, then a bright long tracer fires. The aimed-at player sees a red edge pulse round the screen that blinks hard at the lock (steady with Reduce flashing) and hears a ping. The laser goes when the Sniper dies.
4. Bomber: a strap of three grenades across the chest and a satchel on the hip; it throws (arm back) and a small spinning grenade arcs to the spot, its red light blinking faster, with a red ring where it lands; the blast is a fireball with flames, smoke, a ring and rubble bits (camera shake within 40 studs), then an orange burning patch with flames and embers for the zone's time.
5. Medic Unit: a white backpack with a green plus (never red) and a green light on top; while it repairs, its right arm points out, a green beam with running green packets joins it to the robot it mends (both ends follow), and the backpack light pulses.
6. Swarmer: a small (3 studs) robot that scuttles crouched and leaning forward with quick steps and arms swept back; a lunge throws it flat out with arms reaching.
7. Mimic: until it shows itself it looks like a plain citizen (street shirt and trousers in its own colours per robot, or the citizens' uploaded clothes once those are on, a citizen's face, no health bar, no robot gear) and auto-aim skips it. When it shows itself: a cyan and magenta glitch burst, its eyes turn, and it is a normal robot from then on.
8. Interceptor: swept jet wings on the back with glowing tips; its net shot is a spinning square net, bigger than a pellet.
9. Grappler: the right forearm is a big hook launcher with a claw; the hook flies out on a cable from the launcher, grabs a hero and stays on them while the robot reels (a flying hero is pulled down toward it), and the claw is gone from the arm while it is out; a miss sticks in a wall for a moment or reels back.
10. Low preset with several Bombers and Medics fighting: the frame stays smooth (FrameProf) and nothing errors in the Output.
