1. Weapon Rack and creator: no weapon says "Coming soon"; every Rack weapon can be bought and carried, and the creator's free pick offers all 31 Rack weapons.
2. Try one per family on robots, all 5 moves each: Martial Arts (Counter Stance parries), Power Gauntlets (Ground Pound breaks walls), Katana (Thousand Cuts channels), Spear (long M1 reach), Scythe (Reaping Pull pulls robots in), Tower Shield (Cover Allies shields squadmates nearby), Bow (Arrow Rain), Crossbow (Net Bolt roots), Throwing Stars (Ricochet bounces to 4), Chain Whip (Pull Lash), War Fan (Glide Step speeds you up), Yo-yo, Rope Dart. Every move hits, shows its visual and its comic word, and nothing errors.
3. Feel: no weapon kills robots clearly faster than a power of the same role; the M1 combos all take about the same time to kill a Spark Trooper (fists and daggers fast and light, hammers and great axes slow and heavy).
4. Exotic weapons (Chain Whip, War Fan, Yo-yo, Rope Dart, Boomerang) show their Robux price and need product ids before they can be bought (Launch Checklist).
5. Known placeholder: weapon moves reuse the power visuals in a steel colour and there are no swing animations yet (PLACEHOLDER ART).

### Body sets (2026-11-17)
The Output at boot says build body-sets (2026-11-17). Needs art/export/Bodies.fbx imported first (Studio 3D Importer, into ReplicatedStorage.Art, named "Bodies"; preview art/preview/bodies.png). Before the import everything looks as it does now.
(2026-11-17: heroes no longer choose a body (Config.Creator.bodyStep = false), so steps 1 and 2 only apply if that is turned back on; citizens and robots in step 3 still use the sets.)
1. Creator: pick each of the 8 bodies (Male and Female, Small, Medium, Large, Titan). The torso, arms and legs change shape, not just size: tapers, a broad V on Large and Titan, a soft chest and wider hips on the female bodies. Nothing stretches oddly or leaves gaps at the joints.
2. Switch body back and forth in the creator, then press Play: the hero in the city has the same body as the creator preview, and hats, coats and armour still sit right.
3. Citizens: men and women, slim to big, walk around (no Titans). Robots: Brutes and Sentinels have the Large body, drones and Sneak Bots the Small one. The big HQ dummy is Large.
4. The Output has no "[LookKit] body set" warnings (MeshPart:ApplyMesh is the one new Roblox call here).

### Everyone on R15 (2026-11-16)
The Output at boot says build r15-everyone (2026-11-16). Works before and after the Hero.fbx import.
1. Robots: every robot is the blocky R15 body (same shape as a hero, wider for Brutes and Sentinels, slimmer for drones and Sneak Bots). In the Explorer, Workspace.Enemies has models with HumanoidRootPart, the 15 R15 parts and Motor6Ds named Root, Waist, Neck, LeftShoulder and so on.
2. They walk with arms swinging, elbows bending forward and knees back; Sneak Bots crouch; drones fly with knees back; a windup raises both arms; a knock-up tumbles; a stun wobbles with the yellow ring spinning over the head.
3. Hit one: arms, legs, hands and feet break off first (chips and sparks) showing metal bones, then the torso (ribs, spine, the glowing core), the head last (skull, jaw, eyes). The hair, eyes and mask go with the head. Nothing floats in the air or pokes through.
4. Capes hang from the shoulders and sway; sea robots' helmets and tanks, the Sentinel's cannon and base, the guard plate and the bubble sit where they should.
5. Citizens: every citizen is the blocky R15 body with the classic face, a shirt, pants, shoes and hair; they walk, idle and run with arms up when fleeing. Quest givers, witnesses, stranded citizens (arms up) and the HQ training dummies are R15 too.
   Checked by Claude in Studio on 2026-11-16 (Capeline-r15c.rbxl): your hero is the R15 hero body (no skeleton avatar), robots, citizens and quest givers are R15 rigs with the hero meshes, a beaten robot shows its metal skeleton, 0 errors. Still to check by eye: steps 2 to 4 and 6.
6. Performance: in a busy street (30+ robots and the citizens), the Dev panel's frame times for EnemyRenderer and CitizenRenderer stay under about 2 ms on the desktop.

### 17 paths, mobility and underwater (2026-11-16)
Pick a path at the creator, then use the DEV panel to level up (15+ to borrow). Everything new is plain parts (PLACEHOLDER ART).
1. Creator: 4 archetypes with their paths (17 in all); each path's starters show and a path change works once before level 10. At 15+ a hero can learn up to 5 skills from other paths in the same archetype; the basic and super stay the main path's.
2. Each path's TRAVEL power (marked in Loadout) moves you: Flame Jets, Rock Ride, Psi Platform, Spirit Ride (ride in the air), Wave Surf and Hoverboard (surf the ground and water), Light Rail, Recoil Jump, Sky Step and Giant Stride (launches), Shadow Step and Unstoppable (dashes). No speed-check pull-backs while riding.
3. Mobility (swap free at a safehouse): Super-speed sprint, wall run, water run and slide; Leaping super leap, wall bounce and ground pound (a real fall only); Acrobat double jump, climb, wall run and glide; Swing lines from buildings and zips; Flight as before. The HUD buttons change with the pick. Phone and gamepad too.
4. Summons and decoys: Turret Drop, Fix-It Drone, Shadow Clones, Ward Totem, Ascended Guardian, Holo Decoy and Tide Mirror draw and act; Mech Suit, Grow, Titan Form and Shrink Dodge change the hero's size.
5. Water: jump into the sea off any beach or into the harbour basin at the Docks: you swim (Terrain water), the screen tints blue and darkens deeper down, bubbles trail. No air meter.
6. The Sunken Line: from the basin floor swim west through the tunnel mouth to the flooded station and 3 train cars; each has a commuter in an air bubble. Swim close to pick one up (they follow you), swim up to the green pad on the quay: Credits and Renown. They come back after 4 minutes.
7. Coral Shelf (south of the south beach): reef, kelp, 3 shipwrecks, 7 sunken caches. Swim into one: Credits, Renown, sometimes gear; the marker shows a refill timer (15 minutes per hero).
8. The Trench (past the shelf, very deep): the sunken Null factory with chimneys and a glowing gate, 3 more caches.
9. Underwater robots: Divers (jet packs, helmets), Anchors (heavy seabed slammers) and Torpedo Sentries (slow torpedoes that turn after you) appear only while you swim; land robots stay out of the water and Spark Troopers skim the surface.
10. Element reactions in water: Storm chains jump farther, Fire blinds and pushes, Frost skills leave an ice raft at the surface you can stand on, Earth skills hide you for 2 s, Tide is stronger with shorter cooldowns.
11. Leviathan Engine: DEV boss controls start the third world boss; it rises over the Trench, sweeps its tail, fires torpedo volleys, pulls with a whirlpool and sends a surge ring. Break the 2 torpedo bays; eyes and reactor take double damage. MVP gets Leviathan Coils.
12. Performance: the world builds (the Output says how many underwater fills and parts and the seconds); phones hold 30+ fps swimming the shelf.

### M12 Test and launch prep
The playtest week itself is in notes/Playtest Kit.md (42 checks). These prove the M12 code in Studio first. In Studio every analytics event prints as `[Analytics] name value fields` in the Output.
1. Join: the Output shows `[Analytics] session_start 1 pc new hero` (or returning) within 20 s; a brand-new save also logs the Opening step (no print for steps; check the Dev panel's analytics count goes up).
2. Opening (since lore step 3, the Ceremony prologue): play it through: spark_detected, first_spark, fake_cracked, choice_made (prologue shield or blast) print in order; creator_done prints with class, side and set when you press DONE.
3. Combat: cast skills for a minute: about 1 in 5 casts prints skill_cast. Rank a skill (skill_rank), save a loadout at a safehouse, equip and upgrade a gear piece (gear_upgrade with tier, level, dust); every robot drop prints gear_drop.
4. World: clear an incident (incident_done with the template, medal and helpers), finish a contract (contract_done), fast travel and skip the flight (fast_travel ... 1), join the boss (boss_join) and finish it (boss_done with tier and win or fail).
5. PvP (2 players, level 10+): flag on and off (flag_on, flag_off with city or crater), hit each other (pvp_hit once per session), win (pvp_win); go Downed and get revived (downed N revived) or give up (downed N gave_up, then defeat pve).
6. Story and squads: enter a mission (mission_start), finish it (mission_done with seconds); answer a choice (choice_pick); join a squad (squad_join); change a setting (setting_change preset 2); take a photo (photo comic).
7. Shop: open the shop, tap a few cards, press BUY on a Studio test item: purchase prints with the item and Robux.
8. Leave: stop the test: `leave N idle` (or boss, pvp, downed, mission).
9. Dev panel: "analytics events N sent, 0 over budget", "perf sample on (median/p5 fps)" after a minute in Studio, "server script errors 0".
10. Rejoin the next day on the same save with Studio API access: no second session_start "new"; after 24 to 48 hours the d1_return step logs once.
11. Creator Dashboard (published private place, a day after testers): Analytics shows the custom events and the Onboarding, Combat, PvP, Story and Purchase funnels (the M12 "analytics visible" gate).
12. The playtest week: 20 testers, no crash report (Playtest Kit), results in notes/Build Log.md.

### M11 Juice, audio and wait fillers
Sounds are built-in placeholders (listen for each one, not for quality). Dev panel stats now show "idle waits >5 s". Use Test > Clients and Servers with 2 players for the clash and voice checks.
1. Movement: Leap (whoosh, dust ring), a big landing (thud, crack, a buzz on phone or gamepad), Glide (soft air), flight (a wind that grows with speed and stops when you land), Boost (a burst).
2. Combat: basic attacks ping, skills whoosh, supers boom; a crit adds a high ping; a defeated robot pops, sparks and throws 6 gold coins. Other players' casts are heard near them only.
3. Collision freeze: two players hit the same robot with heavy attacks (Titan Seismic Slam, Blade Moonsplit, supers) at the same moment: both freeze for a moment, a white shockwave rings out, the camera pushes in. It does not happen again for that pair for 5 s.
4. World: a building collapse rumbles (a buzz when within 80 studs), a crater crunches, a car flip whoomps; the boss countdown sounds a siren (music ducks), each boss phase roars, a boss defeat sets off fireworks over it and confetti on screen (no confetti with Reduce flashing on).
5. Rewards: a rescue shows a green ring and "+N RENOWN"; an incident clear slams a GOLD, SILVER or BRONZE stamp; a contract slams DONE!; a level up spins the level badge and lights NEXT AT n: the unlock; a gear drop shows a beam in the tier colour (Legendary and Mythic chime twice); a purchase slams NEW!; a skill rank or loadout save clicks.
6. Death: Downed plays a slow heartbeat loop that stops when revived or respawned; a revive shows gold rings; a respawn shows a blue shield bubble for 5 s; on the Defeated screen CHOOSE LOADOUT opens POWERS right after you are back.
7. Dazed: a PvP stun on you shows spinning stars over your head until it ends.
8. Photo Mode: PHOTO (top left) or N (was K; K is the Task Force board, 2026-11-18): every other screen hides, the hero holds still, drag or the right stick orbits, scroll, pinch or the triggers zoom. POSE steps through Ready, Look Out, Take Off, Thinking and your owned emotes (a second player sees only the emotes). LOOK steps through NONE, COMIC (frame and name caption), SPEED (lines), SUNSET, NOIR, NEON. HIDE UI hides the bar until you tap. SNAP clicks and flashes (no flash with Reduce flashing). EXIT, K or B puts everything back. Nothing is saved.
9. Friend boost: join with a Roblox friend: both see "<name> is here! FRIEND BOOST +10% Renown" and FRIEND BOOST +10% under the radar; kill rewards show the bigger Renown. It goes away when the friend leaves.
10. Offline drones: DEV > HQ level 5, Away 8 h, rejoin: a YOUR DRONES ARE BACK card with the Credits and COLLECT; tapping banks them ("Your drones brought back ..."). Leave without tapping and rejoin: the Credits were banked anyway.
11. Reward cards: a boss result and a mission result count the Credits and Renown up in 1.5 s; the first tap finishes the count, the next tap closes the boss card.
12. Settings: Music and Effects volumes change the sounds at once; Effects 0 silences every effect and voice line.
13. The M11 gate: 8 testers play 30 minutes; the Output shows no "[Capeline] idle wait" line longer than 5 s (each line says why: no incident near, dead or respawning), and every action above had a sound and a visual.

### M10 UI and phone pass
DEV panel (M10 rows): Reset settings, Text 160% / 100%. Use Test > Clients and Servers with 2 to 4 players for squads, and Studio's device emulator for phones.
1. Settings: the gear button (top left, next to the wallet) opens SETTINGS. Each row changes at once: Graphics (Auto shows the picked preset), Text size 100 to 160% in 10s (every screen's text grows, nothing overflows its box at 160%), Left-handed, Reduce flashing, One-hand flight, Tap to fly, Aim assist, Music, Effects, Damage numbers. Rejoin: every value is kept.
2. Left-handed (phone emulator): the action buttons move to the bottom left and the move stick works on the right half of the screen; turning it off puts both back.
3. One-hand flight: the stick only flies forward and its left-right turns the camera; the action buttons stay on the right.
4. Reduce flashing: hits, crits, supers and the Awakening burst flash much less and the camera barely shakes. Damage numbers off: no numbers on hits.
5. Mutes: mute a player from their plate, open SETTINGS: they are under MUTED PLAYERS with UNMUTE; rejoin: still muted. With nobody muted the list says so.
6. Radar (top left under the status card): turns with the camera; incidents are circles, events stars, the boss a diamond, danger pings triangles, squadmates squares in their colour; far ones sit on the rim; tap it for the map. Under it: DAY or NIGHT and the hour, and the boss countdown. It is hidden during the opening.
7. Ping wheel: PING > HELP, HERE (where you aim) or DANGER: others see the ping; DANGER is a red triangle on everyone's radar for a few seconds.
8. Map: incidents, events and the boss have their shapes; with Tap to fly on, tap one (or a beacon outside fast travel): your hero flies there on its own and stops when you touch the stick or land.
9. Squads: tap a plate > INVITE TO SQUAD (or SQUAD > INVITE). The other player gets a card with JOIN and NO and a 30 s bar. JOIN: both see the squad list under the radar in slot colours, the emblem glyph and SQUAD tag on plates. A 5th player can't join ("full"). The leader can KICK; LEAVE works; a squad of one is gone ("Your squad broke up").
10. Squad play: squadmates can't hurt each other even with PvP on; clear an incident with a mate within 250 studs: both are paid; a squad ping shows for mates anywhere on the map with SQUAD on it; mates show as squares on the map and radar even when far.
11. Party missions: the leader enters a mission with 1 to 3 mates within 120 studs: everyone comes in, robots and the War-Frame are scaled to the party's median level, each member is paid at the end, and only the leader's story moves. A member's LEAVE takes only them out; the leader's ends the run for all.
12. Pending buttons: tap a button that asks the server (ACCEPT on a contract, EQUIP, UPGRADE, a station row, INVITE): it shows "..." until the answer, a double tap does nothing, and with no answer in 5 s it says TRY AGAIN.
13. Loading, empty and error states: Journal before the save loads says "Loading your journal..."; the Contract Board with no offers says so; the map opened before the world is built says it is still loading; the creator's DONE with no answer in 8 s says "Could not save. Try again".
14. Main menu: a returning hero joins to CAPELINE with PLAY, SHOP and SETTINGS over the city; PLAY closes it at once. A brand-new player never sees it (the opening starts). Make a second hero (slot 2): the menu offers CREATE FIRST (straight to Suit Up, no cinematic) or PLAY THE OPENING (the cinematic and the run).
15. Controller: every screen above works with a gamepad (Settings rows, squad card, radar buttons, main menu).
16. Device matrix (the M10 gate): a low-end phone, a mid-range phone, a tablet, a PC and a controller each finish the opening, a contract and a boss with no blocked button, unreadable text or overlapping HUD.

### M9 Creator, HQ, shop, passes
DEV panel (M9 rows): Creator (opens it in "first" mode), Enter HQ, HQ level (cycles 1 to 6), Away 8 h (pays the offline drones), All pieces, Buy (grants the shown shop item with a fixed test receipt, then steps to the next), Level 50 (the cap; Legacy needs it).
1. Fresh profile (DEV > Wipe, rejoin): at Suit Up the creator opens (WHO ARE YOU?) with a turning hero. Every tab (Suits to Eyes) changes the preview at once; locked pieces are grey with how to get them (Level 15, a boss tier, a pack). Hair and Eyes tabs exist; COMIC or ANIME and VOLT or BLADE sit under HERO.
2. DONE stays grey until a Title, a Noun and a class are picked; a blocked pair (e.g. Captain + Falcon) shows "taken". RANDOMIZE fills everything from owned pieces. Time it: a new tester makes a hero in under 3 minutes.
3. After DONE: "<NAME> IS READY", the opening carries on, and a second player sees the same look and the hero name on the plate with the Roblox name small under it.
4. Pick BLADE as the start: the POWERS screen shows Blade abilities at level 1. Pick ANIME: effects use the Anime style.
5. A hero made before M9 (created 0): a few seconds after joining the creator opens once in "first" mode (no Hero/Villain switch, class can change).
6. Gear styles: wear gear with a Show Style picked: that style shows over the creator piece; with no Show Style the creator piece shows.
7. HQ: walk to the beacon (hero) or the foundry door (villain): B "Enter". You stand in Beacon Tower (blue) or The Foundry (red) with 6 station pads and an exit. Villains cannot use the hero door ("That is the other side's base").
8. Stations: Command Table opens the Contract Board, the Journal and HEROES; Gizmo lists 40 styles with prices (buying takes Credits, says NEW STYLE, shows OWNED after); UPGRADE HQ takes Credits and says HQ LEVEL n; Mannequins save and wear looks (2 to 4 locked until level 2); Training shows DPS and TOTAL when you hit a dummy, with aim assist on touch; the Exit puts you back where you came in.
9. Boss sim (DEV > HQ level 3): BOSS SIM starts a big robot, a top bar with HP and seconds, red rings every 6 s that hurt for 10% if you stay in and never down you; at the end a toast with the time or the damage dealt.
10. Trophy Wall: earned trophies list (DEV > Boss now and finish one for First World Boss); pick up to 3 (12 at level 4), SAVE THE WALL, plaques appear.
11. Offline drones: DEV > HQ level 5, Away 8 h: "Your drones brought back N Credits" (about 960 at level 5).
12. Legacy: DEV > Level 50 (the cap), Hall of Legends > RETIRE > YES: LEGACY RANK 1, a marble statue of the old hero in the hall, the hero is level 1 with the same look, name and side and a star on the plate.
13. Robux shop (P or SHOP, at HQ or out of a fight): 5 tabs; a pack card previews its pieces on your hero; tapping R$ in Studio grants the item at once ("THANKS! ... IS YOURS") and the card says OWNED; DEV > Buy the same item twice: the second says "nothing (replay)". Slot 4 says LOCKED until slot 3.
14. Effects tint: buy one, EQUIP: your power effects change colour for every player; Prism cycles colours. Landing pose: EQUIP, then fall from a roof: the pose plays and others see it.
15. Emotes: H opens the picker with owned emotes (Salute after Fame rank 2); the pose shows on other screens. Spam does nothing beyond one per 1.5 s.
16. Starter pack: the first world boss reward is followed 6 s later by FIRST BOSS DOWN! with GET IT and NO THANKS; it never shows again.
17. Hero slots: buy slot 3, Command Table > HEROES > + NEW HERO: the creator opens for the new hero; switching back to slot 1 restores that hero's level, gear and story.
18. Shop refused: casting at a robot then pressing SHOP from the city within 10 s says "The shop opens 10 s after a fight".

### M8 PvP, death, anti-exploit
Use Test > Clients and Servers with 2 (or 3) players. DEV panel (M8 rows): Force PvP on (level 10 and the flag, skipping the 5 s and 60 s rules), Down me, Go to the Crater, +200 Fame.
1. Below level 10 there is no PVP button. At 10 (DEV > Level +5 twice) it shows under the wallet. Press it at a safehouse: "Step out of the safe area". Outside: PVP ON in your side colour, a blue (League) or red (Syndicate) ring grows under you and others see it and a PVP tag on your plate.
2. Toggle spam: tap PVP fast: it only changes once per 5 s and nothing kicks you. Hit a flagged player, then try to turn off: "Can switch off in Ns" counts from 60 and restarts on each PvP hit.
3. Unflagged safety: a flagged player's bolts, chains, novas, fields, beams, echoes and supers never hurt an unflagged player standing in them (no numbers, no health loss), and an unflagged player cannot hurt a flagged one. A flag switched off while a field is on the ground stops its hits.
4. Two flagged players fight: numbers show, supers hit for less, stuns and roots stop the other's movement and buttons (a second stun within 8 s is half as long, then 3 s of immunity), slows slow, knockbacks and pulls throw the other player a short way, and nobody gets pulled back by the speed check for it.
5. Safe areas: inside a safehouse's radius, at HQ or in a mission pocket no PvP damage lands either way.
6. PvP defeat: the loser bursts into sparks with no Downed screen, OVERLOADED and a 5 s count, then respawns with SHIELDED for 5 s (hits do nothing until they attack). The card: winner "+N Credits +15 Fame"; loser "-N unbanked Credits. Gear is never lost." (or "Nothing lost" with 500 or fewer on hand).
7. Farming: beat the same player 3 times inside 30 minutes: the third card says no pay. Two players trading 3 wins each inside an hour: both see no pay, and the Output shows two "[GuardService] review: ... trade" lines. Same side pays half.
8. Combat logging: hit a flagged player, then have them leave within 15 s: their body stays standing for 10 s and the attacker gets the win card.
9. Support: an unflagged player's heal or shield (e.g. Controller buffs) skips a flagged ally who is in a PvP fight; it works again 15 s after their last PvP hit.
10. Downed (PvE): DEV > Down me (or let robots win): grey pulsing edge, DOWNED 10 count, GIVE UP, HELP; you crawl slowly and cannot cast or fly; robots stop shooting you. A second player gets a REVIVE button within 8 studs; staying close 3 s (1.5 s as Controller) brings you back with about a third of your health and gives them +40 Renown. Casting or walking away stops it. GIVE UP respawns 2 s later. Waiting 10 s: DEFEATED, the safehouse picker (NEAREST plus visited ones) and a respawn 5 s later at the pick. HELP shows a "HELP!" marker on the other player's screen.
11. The Comet Crater (DEV > Go to the Crater, then walk in): red bar "PVP ZONE IN 5" with LEAVE (puts you just outside); stay: PVP ZONE, your flag is forced on, the PVP button cannot turn it off, Renown from incidents and boss tiers inside pays +50%. A level 9 sees the bar but never gets flagged.
12. Plates: every other player has a plate with name, FAME n (INFAMY for villains), PVP and DOWNED tags; tap it: REPORT (4 reasons; a second report on the same player within 10 minutes says "already"), MUTE hides their chat for you. Three accounts reporting one player within 10 minutes prints a review line.
13. Fame: DEV > +200 Fame: rank up toasts (FAME RANK 2, 3), the Spotlight Visor style shows in GEAR at rank 3.
14. Strikes: speed hack test (DEV teleport tools or a CFrame jump far away): pulled back each time; 3 big jumps inside 10 minutes would kick on a live server (Studio prints "would kick"). Remote fuzzing (wrong types, NaN, floods) only drops calls and, if flooding, counts strikes. No ban anywhere.
15. Gate: a 30-minute exploit test with 2 to 3 players (toggle spam, combat logging, alt farming, speed hacks, remote fuzzing, duplication via fast equip and salvage) finds nothing that pays, hurts an unflagged player or duplicates gear.

### M7 Opening, Act 1, missions
(Steps 1 to 5 were replaced by lore steps 2 and 3, and steps 7 to 12 by "Lore step 5: Act 1" above; steps 13 and 14 still stand.)
DEV panel (M7 rows): Opening (replays it), Open step (jumps the running opening to a step), Mission (cycles 2 to 6 and enters it), Finish step (the current mission or quest step), Leave mission, Quest (cycles the 8 arcs and talks to that NPC), Act 1 done, Reset story. DEV > Wipe profile then rejoin gives a fresh character.
1. Fresh profile: the cinematic plays (comet, rooftops, the shadow over the moon, the street); SKIP shows after 2 s. Underneath you stand on a roof at dusk with only a pulsing LEAP button and a gold diamond over the next roof. No other text or numbers on screen.
2. Leap across; in the air the button reads GLIDE; the diamond moves to the tram car hanging off its rail below. Glide down to it: a big CATCH button pulses (C on PC). Catch: the car settles, 3 citizens run off, +50 RENOWN pops.
3. ZAP pulses and 2 Spark Drones come: the first falls to 2 hits and never shoots; the second takes 4 and hits lightly. The no-tutorial test: 5 first-timers stop the first drone within 30 s with no help (fail if 1 of 5 needs help).
4. A shard and a trapped citizen glow nearby; go to them: SAVE HER and GRAB IT appear. Save: carry her (hold B) to the green beam pad on a roof. Grab: drones chase you to the purple beam toward the Docks. Both reach Suit Up within about 3 minutes.
5. Suit Up: pick a class and Hero or Villain (preset by your choice), SUIT UP: level 3, +1 Skill Point, FLY appears, the full HUD returns and a marker leads to the nearest beacon (SKIP works). The side shows on the HUD and incidents start.
6. Rejoin mid-opening: it starts again from the roof. Get defeated during it: you come back on the roof or at the tram.
7. Journal (J or JOURNAL): STORY lists 6 missions (Shardfall cleared, Hold the Line READY at level 3, the rest LOCKED or LEVEL n), START enters; QUESTS shows the 8 NPCs and where they are; REP shows 3 bars; PERKS is empty until Spare the Core.
8. Mission 2 hero (Hold the Line): fade into a sealed glass pocket; stand in each of 3 rings 10 s while waves come; the train-or-thief choice; Save the Train (hit the yellow train 30 times) or Chase the Thief (beat the Sneak Bot hopping the roofs). The result card shows Credits, Renown, gear and +2 Skill Points; you fade back to where you started. Villain (DEV > Side): break 3 shard crates (14 hits each) guarded by 2 sentries.
9. Mission 3 Signal Tower: fly to the top, beat the Null Sentinel (much tougher) and its drones, hold the mast ring 6 s. +600 Renown on the first clear.
10. Mission 4 The Informant: reach the contact roof, Trust Her or Test Her (Test adds a 4-bot ambush), chase the Sneak Bot round the roofs. Mission 5 Under the Docks: clear room 1, run the sentry gauntlet (2 sentries if you trusted her, else 4), beat the vault guard, then Spare the Core (Null Echo buddy follows you; others see it) or Overload It (+800 Credits, +600 Renown).
11. Mission 6 The War-Frame (level 8): it drops in; break the 2 shoulder cannons; at 66% 6 drones arrive and a purple bubble shields it until 3 hits on the glowing back core; at 33% fire lines and a charged laser: hide behind a pillar and it misses. Get wiped: you respawn in the pocket and it keeps its HP. Defeat: it falls apart; Rare plus Epic gear, +1 Skill Point. With Overload, the pillars crackle.
12. Replay a cleared mission: the choice step says your first choice stands; only the mission's base Credits and Renown pay.
13. Side quests: talk to each NPC (hold B): a speech bubble, the tracker on the left of the top and a marker; finish all 3 steps of the Mayor (records, the speech ring, the motorcade that only rolls while you are near), Pip (helmet, clear 3 real incidents, carry Pip to the pad), Maribel (rebuild ring, defend, pick a menu), Old Beacon (4 rings in 45 s, 5 drones, land on a high roof; villains talk to the Velvet Paw next to him), Tamsin, Quill, Iron Dee and Skye. Rewards: Mayor's Sash and Dock Coat styles in GEAR, the Salute emote, the cafe sign and photo frame unlock toasts, a Skill Point, a Rare piece, the Decoy gadget.
14. Gate: 7 players in 7 missions at once; an 8th gets "every mission pocket is in use". The server frame holds and nobody in a pocket gets incidents or boss banners.

### M6 Incidents, contracts, events, boss
DEV panel (M6 row): Inc (cycles the 10 templates, spawns that one next to you), Clear incidents, Ev (cycles the 6 events), Boss now (lands in 3 s), Boss to 1 HP, Finish contract, Clock (day, dusk, night, dawn), Side (hero or villain for this session).
1. Play: no errors. Within a few seconds 2 incidents appear within 300 studs: a "!" marker (blue League, red Syndicate) with name and distance, and rows in the tracker on the right (time left, progress bar). Clear one: a CLEARED toast with a medal, Credits and Renown, sometimes a drop; a new one appears within 10 s. Walk 600 studs away: it quietly goes and another spawns near you.
2. Each template once via DEV > Inc: Robot heist (squad on a street); Tower fire (citizens on 3 balconies of a tall building with flames, carry each to the Rescue pad: hold B on a citizen, fly to the pad); Runaway tram (yellow tram rolling east or west along a Downtown tram line, hit it to brake; it fails if it reaches the end of the line); Falling sign (stand in the ring 5 s; on a fail the billboard drops); Rooftop chase (a Sneak Bot hopping roofs, or the street out of town; it slows when nobody chases); Cat on a crane (Docks crane top); Raid prep (only during the 5 minutes before a boss); villain side (DEV > Side): Vault breach, Shard crate, Comm sabotage (relay dish).
3. Citizens: the prompt shows only within 12 studs; carried ones ride on your back; get defeated while carrying and the citizen goes back to where they were, unhurt. Two players cannot grab the same one.
4. Contracts: at level 4+ (DEV > Level +5) use the Contract Board terminal at a safehouse (hold B): 3 offers with pay (x2 for the first 5 a day), ACCEPT up to 3. Try each type: Drone sweep (3 squads), Rescue run (carry 5 citizens to any safehouse), Shard recovery (the tracker shows the nearest shard's distance and compass direction; only you can pick yours up), Escort (the truck moves only while you stay within 40 studs, 2 ambushes), Vault job (villain), Stakeout (hero: keep a Sneak Bot in view within 40 studs for 2 s for a SNAP). Rejoin: accepted contracts come back with their progress. ABANDON removes the sites.
5. Events: DEV > Ev for each: an EVENT banner toast for everyone, a gold marker and tracker row; Meteor shower (warning rings across the plaza, shards after 4 s), Robot rampage (4 waves), Tidal surge (water rises over the Docks while you carry 12 citizens to 2 boats), Vault breach (hold the ring 30 s while robbers come), Train hijack (stop the train before the end of the line), Blackout (Old Town goes dark; clear the 2 drones at each of 4 generators, hold each 5 s). Everyone who helped gets the EVENT DONE toast. Left alone, a new event arrives every 4 to 6 minutes, at most 2 at once.
6. World boss: DEV > Boss now (or wait 10 minutes on a fresh server; a wiped profile pulls it to 6 minutes). At T minus 60 s a red banner counts down (Raid prep incidents start 5 minutes out; each clear takes 3% off its HP). Rustjaw Prime drops from the sky south of the plaza; the bar shows HP, phase marks, time left and your tier and rank. Its core and knees take double damage; the 2 shoulder cannons shell heroes until broken. Telegraphs: red rings and cones fill before each Stomp and Swipe; Scrap Rain circles in phase 2; Eye Laser lines in phase 3; a stomp next to a building damages it.
7. Lumen Maw (next boss): rises in the Old Quarry; 3 generators around the pit heal it until broken; Gravity Pull drags you toward it in phase 3 while its ring warns.
8. Contribution: a healer or shielder who never hits it still reaches Contributor in a 4-player fight; a player who arrives with 1 minute left still gets at least the floor. Heroes and villains (DEV > Side) hit the same bar.
9. Result: DEV > Boss to 1 HP, then hit it: it falls apart in sparks and plates; the result card (5 s, tap to close) shows tier, rank, Credits, Renown, drops, a style unlock (GEAR > STYLES) and the MVP names. Let it run 8 minutes instead: it flies off and the card says it got away (Credits only).
10. Clock: DEV > Clock steps day, dusk, night, dawn; the sky glides there and night is darker blue but readable. At night more red Syndicate incidents show up (with Side: villain).
11. MAP shows incident dots, event diamonds and the boss.
12. Gate: with 30 bots plus you, the Dev panel's "schedulers" and "boss" times stay low and the server frame holds; nothing idles more than 5 s (an incident is always within reach).

- Rifle robots still aim, shield robots hold the shield, Sneak Bots crouch, Swarmers run low.
- Flyers, turrets, lunges and knock-ups look as before. No Output errors about animations.

- Your hero is the standard Roblox blocky body; every Shirt and Pants sits right (front on the front, sleeves on the arms, no stretched seams).
- In the creator, Face and Hair list only Roblox catalog items; hair sits on the head like on a Roblox avatar; Eyes, Face details, Makeup and Facial hair no longer show.

- 40 titles and 56 nouns on PLAY; 10 origins, each with its crest.
- Gloves, boots, masks, capes and weapons on the new body: check they still sit on the hands, feet, head and back.

## Studio list: kid-proof prologue (2026-11-18)
Play a new hero's prologue on a server with a second player (Studio Test > 2 players) fighting robots near the plaza.
1. From the ceremony to the title, the second player, their name plate, their powers, city robots and city citizens never show on the prologue hero's screen, and nothing hurts the prologue hero except Skylance and the swarm.
2. Every beat you play shows one gold instruction at the top with the right key (keyboard, controller, phone).
3. Leap, chase: one Leap press on each roof lands you on the next roof. Descend: one Leap press lands you at the subway steps.
4. Tunnel: just run. Each slab falls behind you with a shake and blocks the way back; none falls on you.
5. Fight: Skylance stays inside the chamber (never through a wall) and the fight can be won with Basic alone.
6. Swarm and rescue play as before; the title card ends it. Patch says "Security bots", Crane says "Guardians of Brightharbor!".

## Studio list: Boss pass (2026-11-19: boss animation, telegraphs, the surge and the Blackout)
The bosses' meshes are now split by rig segment (85 meshes in Art.Bosses, uploaded from the cloud as Model 106094769700641 and pulled into art/models/Bosses.rbxm; nothing to import). DEV "Boss now" brings the next boss; DEV mission 6 the War-Frame. Render check: art/preview/boss_poses_<Boss>.png.
1. Rustjaw Prime: breathes and looks round while idle, walks (legs and arms swing) when it closes in; Stomp lifts the right leg high with arms up and slams down exactly as the ring fills (the ring and cracks fill to the hit); Swipe winds the left fist back and sweeps across the cone; Scrap Rain throws both arms high then forward; Eye Laser drops the head, opens the jaw and snaps up on the beam. No piece floats apart or pops at the hit.
2. Lumen Maw: tail sways, jaw pants; Bite coils back and lunges; Shard Spikes rears and slams the forepaws; Lumen Beam lunges along the beam with the jaw wide; Gravity Pull rears and gapes, chevrons crawl in toward the danger ring, then it snaps shut.
3. Leviathan Engine: bobs and rolls on the swell, the flank gears, turbine and tide wheel keep turning (faster in a wind-up and in phase 3); Tail Sweep coils the neck one way and whips across; Torpedo Volley dips and surges; Whirlpool sinks and roars up; Surge rises high and crashes down.
4. War-Frame: braces for Rockets, sweeps the blade arm, stomps, sprays the floor, hunches over the Charge Laser; when its shield breaks it kneels and shows its back core.
5. Big hits (about 4% of max HP in one update, e.g. a super) make it flinch, at most every 1.4 s; after a hazard attack (Scrap Rain, Eye Laser, Lumen Beam, Gravity Pull, Whirlpool, Surge) it shows its weak points for about 2 s (Rustjaw kneels, the Maw arches its back sacs up, the Leviathan drops its head to the water).
6. At 66% and 33%: plates break off, it staggers, then roars (enrage); phase 3's idle is faster and bigger. On defeat it collapses (about 2.5 s), then sinks as before.
7. Telegraphs: every shape (ring, cone, circles, lines, pull) has a faint tint, a fill that reaches the edge exactly when the hit lands, and a dashed red-and-cream edge that pulses faster near the end; slams crack the ground and the cracks linger and fade; beams have chevrons along them. With Reduce flashing on: the edges are steady, no gold heat at the end, no eye blinks.
8. Hit timing unchanged: stand at a telegraph's edge and step out just before the fill completes: no damage; inside when it completes: damage (server truth, same as before).
9. Tidal surge (DEV start the event in the Docks): the water still rises and sinks with rescues; waves roll in from the harbour across the district, curved, with a white foam lip and spray, two on the water at once, lower as citizens are saved.
10. Blackout (Old Town): the district goes deep blue-black; pairs of glowing slit eyes open round you at a distance, watch you, blink and close; light motes drift off toward them; fewer eyes as generators come back.
11. Phone (low preset): 30 Hz boss animation (less far away), fewer dashes, wedges and wave segments; FrameProf WorldFx and EventFx stay low with a boss up and a surge running.
## Studio test list: gap polish (2026-11-19)
- Fast travel: pick a found beacon on the map. Your hero takes off from where it stands (a shockwave and dust), flies the whole route in the flight pose with a white comet trail, the camera following behind, then dives in and lands with the thud, the ring, the crack and the hard landing pose. SKIP: the hero stops, the server sets it down, it lands the same way.
- Fast travel, second player: watch a friend fast travel. You see them leave (burst), fly across the sky with the trail, and arrive (dust ring and shockwave). Nobody is pulled back mid-flight (no MoveCorrect in the output); towers on the way are flown through, not rammed or broken.
- Fast travel edge cases: a beacon on a tall tower, a trip across the whole map, a trip right after another (cooldown message), dying or respawning is impossible mid-flight; no beacon is discovered by flying past it.

Decoy: a see-through blue copy of your own hero (same face, hair, suit, gear) stands where you were, flickers more near the end, pops in sparks after 5 s; other players see the same copy of you. Shield Drone: a small drone circles you with a blue bubble round you for 8 s, and goes when the shield breaks.
- Landing crack: a hard landing leaves a comic ink crack (one of three, turned), fading over its last 4 s; it lies flat on asphalt, roofs and grass with no z-fighting.
- Snow Wall, Ice Slide, Frost's raft: shards on the wall's top, snow on its cap and foot, snow rails on the ramp, frost motes on medium and high; shots still stop at the wall and heroes still walk through it; the ramp still launches the same.
- Lift and Throw with nothing lifted: a planked crate flies the arc and breaks into planks and dust where it lands.
- Loading screen: the comic splash with the logo popping in, the tips changing every 4 s, on a phone and a PC; it fades out cleanly when the hero loads.

4. Phone (iPhone SE): portraits read at the guide box's size; panels' faces are visible on the comic page.

## Studio list: Wrecks and the Crater (2026-11-19, gap-wrecks)
- Beat a few robot kinds (Spark Trooper kinds, Brute Bot, Sniper, Medic Unit, Wind-Up Giant): each leaves its own heap in its suit colours (the chest, an arm and a leg, the metal under-head with its dark visor, the kind's gear), never a face or hair; it sits on the ground, not floating or sunk.
- Grab, carry and throw a wreck: it rides overhead and lands as before (the hidden Body box is still what collides); nothing in the heap blocks or snags the hero.
- Near a wreck: small textured sparks every second or so and the visor lights for a blink; at the end it flickers, then pops in tumbling nuts in its suit and metal colours, sparks and a puff of smoke. Check the frame time on a phone next to 6 wrecks (FrameProf ThrowFx).
- Delete ReplicatedStorage.Art.Wrecks in a Studio test: wrecks fall back to the 4 plain parts and still spark and pop.
- Fly to the Crater: the white lab walls with soot and rebar, the Crane C plate, the cracked tanks with their glow, the toppled towers, the husk pile, the cape, shield and helmet; walk into every wall, tank and tower: you stop exactly where you did before (the boxes are hidden, not gone), and you can still fly across the open middle.
- Medium and high: loose rubble, pipes, plates and a console lie on the bowl's floor (none on low), never inside a wall; they go when you fly far away and come back near.
- Textures: the walls, towers and relics show their baked panels, soot and rust (not flat grey).

Nothing blocks you or the robots.

## Studio list: Pocket props and the plaza board (2026-11-19, gap-props2)

Start any mission or side quest pocket with props (Live, The Copy, the Halo Academy, the Spark Pound, Ashgrove Labs, Iron Hall) on high: desks, chairs, lockers, cabinets, tanks, cages, dummies, punching bags, pads, posts, braziers, screen desks, camera rigs, crowd barriers, chimneys and water tanks draw as meshes with their textures, standing exactly where their boxes stood, and no box shows through or double-draws.

6. The plaza's weekly board: the gold bezel, cabinet, crest star and posts draw round the screen; every row and the countdown read clearly from the plaza and at 220 studs; walking into the legs and frame still stops you as before.

## Studio list: Crater ground and throw particles (2026-11-19, gap-fx3)
1. Fly to the Crater: the floor is cracked ash-brown ground with dark scorch blotches, pebbles and a few glassy blue-sheened spots, no visible repeating grid (Organic pattern); the rim is layered tan and rust rock strata with fractures. Walking, landing and fighting on both feel exactly as before.
2. The 10 burn scars are comic charred streaks with an inked edge, broad at the lab end and tapering outward. If any scar's broad end points away from the lab, or a scar looks squashed across its strip, note it (the decal's orientation on a Top face is the one thing Lune cannot check).
3. Pick up a bench: a dust puff at your feet. Throw it: a flat dust ring, puffs and tumbling rubble chips in the ground's colour where it lands; a heavy throw kicks up the most. Watch a prop come home: a small pale shine with stars.
4. Ram a wall at speed and get thrown through one: chips in the wall's colour and a puff of dust fly out from the wall (no physical chip blocks any more).
5. Drop to the phone preset: fewer particles, still readable; no frame drop with several throws at once. A collapse's dust cloud uses the drawn smoke sheet.

## Studio list: Boss stuck and anti-cheese (2026-11-19, pt-bosses)
1. Dev: start Rustjaw Prime and kite it down a street: it walks the street, and any building it walks into loses pieces where its feet meet it (holes show, citizens run); no more than 3 collapses at once; it never stands inside a building.
2. Stand behind a beacon's host tower (pinned): Rustjaw slides round it, the tower never breaks.
3. Box it in (or wait for it to stall 4 s): the Leap telegraph (a cracked ring) shows where it lands, it crouches, arcs over and lands on the ring; the landing hits low fliers too.
4. Fly high and far and shoot: lock-on reticles (dashed ring and brackets, a ground shadow) appear on you; keep moving and the shots miss, hover still and they hit.
5. Hover by its body (any boss): a tinted column with tape rings at its floor and ceiling and a climbing band; get out or be swatted down (knocked away and dropped toward the ground, no rubber-banding).
6. Stay far away for 10 s on the ground (speed or teleport) vs the Maw, the Leviathan or the War-Frame: a dashed tether beam and reticle reach you; a hit yanks you in close, smoothly, with no MoveGuard pull-back.
7. Nobody in melee for 25 s: "ENRAGED! GET IN CLOSE!" banner and the roar; attacks come faster; walk in close and "IT CALMS DOWN!" shows.
8. A melee hero at the boss's feet sees none of the counters aimed at them (only the old attacks).
9. The War-Frame mission (pocket): the counters work there too and the pillars still block the charged laser.
10. Phone: the reticles, columns and tethers stay smooth with several heroes (dash counts scale with the preset).


## Studio list: lock-on, omnidirectional movement, knockback, block key, drops, swinging robots (2026-11-19, pt-combat)
1. Soft lock: walk up to robots and fire basics without aiming exactly: casts go to the robot nearest the centre in front (thin white corner brackets on it). Leave the camera alone for a second: it eases round toward that robot; turn the camera yourself and it never fights you. Settings "Soft lock-on" off: no brackets, the old aim.
2. Hard lock, PC: middle mouse on a robot: red turning brackets and a diamond; the camera swings round to keep it and your hero both on screen, sitting a little off your shoulder. Tab and a held middle mouse move it to the next robot; middle mouse again lets go. Check Tab also opens Roblox's player list (if that annoys, the list can be hidden or Tab dropped).
3. Hard lock, gamepad: tap R3 locks, flick the right stick sideways to change targets, hold R3 for the Gadget. Phone: the red LOCK button between FLY and the skills: tap locks, hold for the next; check it never covers a skill on an iPhone SE.
4. Locked on the ground: the hero faces the target, the stick strafes and backpedals round it. In the air too. Strafe hard: the camera leans and swings out a little; Settings Camera motion Off: no lean or sway, the lock still frames.
5. Lock the world boss: the camera pulls back to fit it; never inside a wall. A lock drops when the robot dies, flies off past ~170 studs or hides behind a building for 1.5 s.
6. PvP: flagged players can be locked only while you can hit them; unflagged never.
7. Omni flight: fly forward, pull the stick back: you slow and fly backwards facing the camera, no U-turn; strafe sideways; up and down follow where the camera looks plus Leap/Down. Boost forward still swoops and turns like before. No MoveCorrect pull-backs while doing any of it.
8. Glide: drift sideways and back without turning round. Underwater: look up or down and push forward to swim that way; Leap rises, Down sinks.
9. Knockback: a basic shoves a robot a little; a Haymaker, a slam or any heavy skill sends it flying in an arc tumbling; a charged skill or a super sends it far. One that hits a building breaks through the wall (sparks, chunks) and keeps flying, out the far side; a pinned building (beacon host, side quest roof) stops it dazed. Bosses do not move; a mini-boss barely.
10. PvP: a heavy or a super throws a flagged player a short way (half the distance, 30 studs at most); jabs never juggle.
11. Block: hold Q: the shield shows and the Guard ring drains. Alt no longer blocks. LB and the phone shield button as before.
12. Drop smash: jump or fall from a tall roof onto a lower one (34+ studs): the roof under you breaks with a wide dust ring and a big crack; robots round you go flying. A short hop breaks nothing.
13. Swing a robot: stun a robot (Arc Flash, a stun skill) or beat one so it leaves a wreck; GRAB it (B); tap attack: it whirls round in front, hits and throws the robots in the arc and breaks a wall you are next to; after 4 swings, or holding attack, it is thrown; a live one smashes through what it hits. The prompt never shows over a boss, a mini-boss or a small pack robot.

Downtown (980 x 1,120) and Uptown (750 x 750) load in full on PC and on a phone (Low): no hole or late block, the new south-west tower blocks stream in while you fly, and CityBuilder's hub build has no long frame (FrameProf). ## Animation audit and Studio list: pt-anims (2026-11-19, nate's playtest)
nate: "we need weapon and power animations that the player does. Make sure it works even if player is flying or mid air. Some of them might need two handed animations" and "don't forget about not only the player and everything they can do, but npcs, enemies, bosses." Every row below is code-keyed data (PLACEHOLDER ANIMATION until real uploaded animations). Contact sheets: art/preview/anim_clips_1.png (two-handed, robot swing, heavy landing), anim_clips_2.png (ground vs mid-air pairs), anim_clips_3.png (robots), anim_clips_4.png (people, landings); `lune run art/anims/frames.luau && $HOME/tools/bpyenv/bin/python art/blender/clips.py`.

| Who / action | Before pt-anims | Now |
|---|---|---|
| Hero: 136 abilities (skills, supers, basics by path) | own whole-body clip each (HeroMoves, anim_pass_test) | same; casts in the air now keep the travel legs (upper-body layer) |
| Hero: casts while flying, hovering, boosting, diving, gliding, falling, dashing, zipping, swinging, on walls, riding, swimming | the cast's kneel or step legs and root dip overrode the travel legs | upper-body layer: legs stay the travel pose, the Root keeps only the turn (HeroAnim.layer, upperRoot) |
| Hero: casts while sprinting or water running | full leg weight (stride broken) | 30% legs, the stride carries on |
| Hero: 36 weapons x (4-hit combo + Z X C V) | own clips; two-handed weapons keyed arm by arm, the off hand drifted off the hilt | the 8 two-handed weapons (greatsword, great axe, warhammer, spear, scythe, bo staff, rifle, crossbow) play "@hilt", "@pole", "@long" copies with both hands solved onto the weapon every frame (Shared.AnimIK); hammer toss, scythe whip and the staff's sweep kick let go on purpose |
| Hero: weapon ready stances | holdTwo, holdPole, holdLong keyed by eye | holdTwo and holdPole pinned the same way; holdLong (rifle, crossbow) keeps its hand-set arms, since the solve drew the rifle stock into the face (weapon_art_test) |
| Hero: block, parry, guard break, charge-up, throw, carry, lift, heave, 4 gadgets, emotes, revive, Give Up, Downed, interact, catch, Awakening, flinches | own clips (anim pass) | same |
| Hero: mobility poses (fly, hover, boost, dive, glide, leap crouch, dash, arc, swing, wall, wall run, rides by path, water run, swim, sprint) and take-offs, landings, wall kicks, flips | own clips (anim pass) | same, plus poundLandHeavy |
| Hero: a grabbed robot as a weapon (pt-combat) | none | robotHold (loop, CarryGrip "club"), robotSwing, robotSwingBack, robotSlam, all two-handed (HeroAnimator.robotSwing) |
| Robots: wind-ups (13 AI kinds) | arms overhead for every kind (the Sneak Bot's coil) | per kind: overhead, aim, cocked swing, throw, shield brace, claws up, hook wound back (the Sneak coil kept) |
| Robots: attacks (swing, shot, hook, slam, lob, rally, mend) | the arm snapped to a fixed angle for 0.3 s, or held while attacking | per AI kind a strike with wind-up, hit and recovery: hammer, haymaker, stab, sweep, shield bash, snatch, claw rake, zap, rifle kick, brace shot, lob throw, command point, hook throw, yank, mend hand (Shared.NpcAnim via EnemyRenderer.act) |
| Robots: lunges, coils, tumbles | code poses | same (kept) |
| Robots: hit reacts | root recoil only | root recoil plus a joint react (hit; hitHeavy buckles a knee) |
| Robots: stunned | root wobble | plus the stagger loop (arms dangling, head lolling) |
| Robots: deaths | the body swaps for its Blender wreck heap with sparks and a pop (gap-wrecks); creatures burst into light | same (no body fall: the wreck replaces it at once) |
| Citizens | Roblox's idle, walk, run; flee, cheer and flinch code poses | plus idle gestures while standing (look round, check the time, talk, shrug, point) on each one's own beat |
| Quest givers and named helpers | stood in the rest pose | a wave when a hero walks up, the talk while one is close, a beckon or point when one is in sight, otherwise arms crossed, look round, check the time, shrug (NpcGestures, tag NpcGesture) |
| Bosses (4) | every Config.Boss attack has a wind-up and recover; flinch, weak, stagger, enrage, defeat (BossPose, boss pass) | same; pt-bosses adds placeholder poses for its new attack ids |

Studio checks (only Studio can prove these):
- [ ] Fly, hover, glide, fall and swim while casting every power key and swinging every weapon: the legs keep the travel pose, no kneel or dip mid-air; on the ground the whole body still moves.
- [ ] Each two-handed weapon (greatsword, great axe, warhammer, scythe, spear, bo staff, rifle, crossbow): both hands stay on the mesh through the 4-hit combo, Z, X, C, V and the stance, no wrist twisted inside out, on Roblox's own body (its shoulders may sit a little differently from Config.HeroRig, which the solve uses).
- [ ] Robots: each kind's wind-up reads before its attack; the strike lands with its shot, swing, slam or throw effect; hits rock the joints; stunned robots stagger; nothing sticks afterwards (wrists and neck back to rest), with Roblox's walk on (near) and off (far).
- [ ] Givers wave as you walk up, talk while you stand near, and gesture otherwise; helpers likewise; standing citizens gesture now and then. FrameProf: NpcGestures stays small on a phone (4 people within 40 studs).
- [ ] pt-combat's robot swing: CarryGrip "club" shows robotHold; HeroAnimator.robotSwing alternates the swing and the backhand (slam overhead); the heavy pound landing reads.

Attacks never hurt Skylance on the roofs; in the subway he fights and takes damage normally.

## Studio list: attack and power animations (fix-anims, 2026-11-19)
Root cause: Roblox's Avatar Joint Upgrade gave heroes AnimationConstraints, which HeroAnimator never looked for (notes/Decisions.md). Headless proof: tests/fix_anims_test.luau. Only Studio can show these:
1. Play Solo. Output shows "[HeroAnimator] your hero: 15 joints (Motor6D)" (with AnimationConstraint listed instead, the code still animates; no "has no R15 Waist joint" warning).
2. Explorer, while playing: Workspace > your character > UpperTorso holds a Motor6D named Waist (not an AnimationConstraint).
3. Click (Basic) five times standing still: the arms swing a varied combo. Press a skill key (1, 2...) and the super: each plays its own pose. Fly and cast: the legs keep the flight pose.
4. Press 2 for the weapon: the arms take its stance; swing it. Hold Q (block): the guard pose. Use an emote from the picker: the pose holds, then returns.
5. With a second player (Test > 2 Players): each sees the other's casts and swings.

- [ ] Stamina: boost lasts about 14 s; the stamina ring empties at half the old rate; no speed-check pullback in a long boost.
## Studio test list: holding picked-up things (fix-carry, 2026-11-19)
Previews: art/preview/carry.png (the game's own hold sums on the blocky body). Lune proves the maths (tests/carry_test.luau, ONLY="Carry"); only Studio shows the welds and poses on real characters.
1. As a Powerhouse (Titan), grab a car: it sits on both raised fists over your head, the arms straight up under it, not floating above. Walk, sprint, jump and fly with it: it never trails, wobbles loose or sinks into the head. A second player sees the same (their screen poses your arms).
2. Grab a dumpster, a vending machine (laid flat over the head) and a slab: the same. Grab a bench, a bin, a mailbox: up on your right fist beside the head. A sign and a lamp post: gripped upright in the right fist.
3. Stun a robot and GRAB it: it lies face up across your raised fists (no spinning, no lag behind you while you run or fly). Tap attack: it follows your hands round the swing and the backhand, then slams; hold attack: it is thrown from over your head. Same with a robot's wreck.
4. As a Gravity (Pulse) or Mind hero, grab a car, a bench and a robot: each floats over your head with a glow in the path's colour (purple for Gravity, pink for Mind), bobbing, both hands raised to it; a swing whirls it round you. Other paths (Spirit, Light...) hold it.
5. Throw each: it leaves from where it was held (over the head or the fist), lands as before; set it down (B): it drops in front, upright, the glow gone.
6. Output: no errors from ThrowService, ThrowClient or EnemyRenderer; the MicroProfiler shows ThrowHold and EnemyHeld tiny with nobody carrying.


## Studio test list: everything destructible, flying through, craters, billboards (fix-destroy, 2026-11-19)
- Fly at plain cruise (no boost) into a Downtown tower: the hole opens at once, you keep going through the far wall and out the other side with no stop and no MoveCorrect pullback; the knocked pieces fly ahead and never snag you. Repeat with a boost and a glide dive.
- Fly into a beacon host (Downtown East) or a safehouse shelter: the walls hole, the roof and pad never move, it never collapses; drones mend it.
- Punch or super a farm silo: holes show on a round, eight-sided shell (no square box), a few supers bring it down into a rubble heap, the white cap goes with it; drones put it back. Same for a lighthouse (bands and lamp go with it) and the beacon windmill (shell: blades and roof stay).
- Fly through a tree, a lamp, a container and a long fence: each is knocked loose as you pass (only the fence lengths you hit fly), another player near sees the same; all come back after 45 s.
- A super on a road, a plaza and a field: a crater with the ink decal, dark pit and broken rim chunks in the ground's colour; every player sees the same one; it sinks back over its last minute (4 min in all). Check a phone shows at most 6.
- Underwater, a super at the sand seabed: the Terrain is really dug (a bowl you can swim into), the water stays water (no air pocket), and after 4 min the seabed is back exactly. Check WriteVoxels accepts the saved arrays (CraterService heal).
- News screens: the band's channel mark sits left of the words, the headline and ticker stay inside the screen; knock out the wall behind a tower screen: the screen drops off and its words go; a collapse hides it as before.
- Wreck a building with a sign, poster or face decal on its parts: the decal and any SurfaceGui hide with the body (nothing floats in the air), and a flung roof billboard keeps its look as it falls.
- PerfMonitor in a 10-player wreck of Downtown: "craters" stays at 48 or less, "impactBreaks" climbs within 20 a second, frame time on a phone preset holds (falling 240, debris 60, crater chunks 36).

## Studio list: weapons whole in the hand (fix-weapons, 2026-11-19)
1. Play, carry each family (key 2): Sword, Great Axe, Warhammer, Scythe, Spear, Twin Blades, Sai, Blaster Rifle, Crossbow, War Fan, Yo-yo, Boomerang. Each is one piece in the hand (blade meets guard, haft meets head), as art/preview/weapons_held.png and weapons_ingame_after.png; nothing floats beside the hero.
2. Swap weapons at the Weapon Rack and in the creator several times: no old pieces stay behind; swing and run: the weapon stays on the hand.
3. Styles 2 Broad and 3 Jagged on the Sword and Great Axe: same, the blade on its hilt.
4. Another player's weapon looks the same from your screen (built on the server, replicated).
5. If a weapon looks backwards or split after any re-upload of Weapons.fbx, run `python3 -I art/upload/meshcheck.py Weapons` and update Shared.MeshFix.


## Studio list: models placed as Blender drew them (fix-models, 2026-11-19)
Root cause (nate: "the leviathan engine's parts just aren't placed properly ... consistent throughout the game"): Roblox's Open Cloud FBX import stored every mesh of every set turned 180 degrees about y inside its MeshPart (measured on all 2,303 downloaded meshes of 39 sets, art/models/meshturns.json; Hero is symmetric). Offsets, sizes and welds were right; each piece faced backwards in its box. Shared.MeshFix now turns every placed mesh back (no re-upload). Headless proof: tests/model_fit_test.luau (ONLY="Model fit", 1,794 meshes through the game's own code, 0 off; without the fix every one is 180 degrees off). Renders of what Studio shows: art/preview/modelfit_<Group>.png (Blender layout | before | after).
- [ ] Leviathan Engine, Rustjaw Prime, Lumen Maw, the War-Frame: head, jaw, fins, cannons and plates sit on the body as in art/preview/boss_<Boss>.png (no tail cone forward, no eyes floating above); phase plates still break off; the boss poses still turn about the right joints.
- [ ] Spark Troopers (shell style) and the finales (Bramblebeast, Voidhound, Wind-Up Giant): face, chest emblem, belt buckle and boot toes on the front; the hair, mane, spines and key where art/preview/finale_<Name>.png has them; walk and attack clips bend the right way.
- [ ] Creator: a mask, helmet, gloves, boots, belt, shoulders, a hat, hair, eyes, a coat and a cape: each faces forward on the hero (the visor in front, the toe caps forward, the buckle in front); the same for gear looks (LookService) and fake heroes.
- [ ] Vehicles: hub cars, escorts and the towed car drive nose first (headlights in front) and their wheels roll forward (Fx.VehicleRig).
- [ ] Props: street props (benches, billboards, awnings, water towers, lamps), set pieces, HQ stations and dressing, the hideout shells, story props and the Echo Drone, prologue stage, Crater ruins, wrecks, pocket props, news screens and the plaza board: faces and screens toward the street or room as before; the Torpedo Sentry's torpedo flies nose first.
- [ ] A training dummy in the HQ (NpcRig look "Hq") and its visor and target: on the front.
- Known left as is: LookKit.wearBodySet (Art.Bodies via ApplyMesh in place) cannot turn a mesh; it only runs when Roblox's own body is off (Config.Creator.robloxBody), so it is not seen today.

## Studio list: body set torsos face forward (fix-bodies, 2026-11-19)
Root cause: the Body step wears the body sets' torsos on Roblox's own body with MeshPart:ApplyMesh, which keeps the mesh as Roblox stored it (turned 180 degrees by the upload) and cannot turn it. The torsos are now uploaded pre-turned (Art.BodyTorsos, Model 119292912239570) so the stored copy faces forward. Headless proof: tests/model_fit_test.luau "the body sets' torsos on Roblox's own body" (every gender and build through LookKit.wearBodySet; with setMesh on Art.Bodies it fails).
1. In the creator's Body step pick each female build (Small, Medium, Large, Titan): the chest shape is on the front, not the back.
2. Pick Male Large and Male Titan: the broad V is on the front; Male Medium keeps Roblox's own torso.
3. With a suit on, the Shirt and Pants front still sits on the front of the new torso, and the torso joins the arms, neck and hips with no gap.
4. NPCs and robots on body sets (NpcRig) look as before.

## Studio list: smash rule, Hulk climb, Acrobat crawl and flips, swing anywhere, sprint, stamina (move-2, 2026-11-19)
Proved headless (tests/climb_test.luau, ONLY="Climb"): the smash rule's thresholds, a bot bounding up a 160-stud wall and crawling/scurrying up 120 studs at ranks 0 and 5, back-to-back flips, a 10 s sprint on every pick and sky swinging all pass MoveCheck at 10 Hz with no pull-back; poses in art/preview/anim_clips_5.png. Only Studio can prove the rest:
- [ ] Super-speed: hold Shift at full speed into a Downtown wall: you smash straight through (hole, chips, shake) on the ground and mid-hop; let go of Shift and run at a wall: you run up it (no Boost needed, stamina drains); a landmark or safehouse is run up, never rammed.
- [ ] Leaping: a full charged running leap into a wall smashes through; a tapped leap, a fall or a jump from beside the wall grabs it: a crack and dust where the hands bite, the HUD reads CLIMB / LET GO. Hold Space: bound, grab, bound to the top and the vault onto the roof (the vault pose shows). Stick away + Space kicks off; Ctrl lets go. After many grabs on one wall a piece breaks (the chips add up). Nothing pulls you back.
- [ ] Acrobat: walk into a wall: stick and crawl (up is up, the body flat on the face, limbs splayed); crawl over the top onto the roof, round an outside corner, into an inside corner, down onto the street. Jump up under an awning or a tunnel ceiling: stick and crawl on it (forward is the camera's way). Shift scurries (stamina). Space jumps off, Ctrl lets go. The camera follows on walls and ceilings without clipping badly (note any spot it does).
- [ ] Acrobat flips: Ctrl on the ground and in the air flips along the stick (back when still), the tucked flip shows on another player's screen too; a robot's punch or a shot during the flip misses and DODGE! pops; not again before 1.1 s. Air control feels the best of all picks.
- [ ] Swing outside the city (Farmland, the coast, Radio Hill): lines catch trees, poles, rocks and cliffs; on an empty field a shorter sky line still swings (slower than the city's); no sky line inside a tunnel or a mission pocket. In Downtown nothing changed.
- [ ] Every pick: Shift (LT, the SPRINT button on a phone, or the stick pushed to its edge) sprints at 28 with the harder stride; Super-speed still far faster.
- [ ] Stamina: after a boost it waits ~0.6 s, then refills in ~5 s on the ground (~10 s flying); the HUD ring matches; no pull-backs after long boosts.

## Sound audit and Studio list: the sounds pass (2026-11-19, nate's playtest: "need better sounds badly")
Audit before (base gaps7 36fc27a):
- 155 rows in Config.Audio (84 sounds incl. the 31-row juice table and 6 combat moments, 3 impact layers, 68 path layers = 17 paths x cast/super/hit/charge) all played one of 9 Roblox built-in client files pitched per row: electronicpingshort 48 rows, action_jump_land 31, swoosh 26, clickfast 17, action_falling 14, action_get_up 9, button 6, action_swim 2, impact_water 2. So every impact, boom, collapse and power element was the same jump-land thud or ping: placeholder, heavily repeated, weak.
- Silent: the 4 music rows (patrol day, night, boss, victory sting: asset 0); the 3 city beds in Config.Life (asset 0 and never played by any code); the 24 voice pack clips (Config.Shop.voiceSounds, id 0).
- Placeholder (built-in clips) in Config.Life: honk, flutter, spray, cans, pop, drone (6).
- Missing entirely: ground pound slam, awning bounce, splash into water, Acrobat dash, sonic boom, Super Speed run; no variants (one file per row, only a pitch spread); one Effects group, no compressor, no ducking under big booms, no rooftop wind or city ambience.
After (branch sounds):
- 5 Roblox audio uploads, all Approved: Hits 123409640659064 (157 clips: impacts, blocks, robots, every path), World 131543281971555 (45: movement, destruction, city life), Ui 137644467719671 (42: buttons, rewards, stings, boss siren and roar), Loops 113838487045023 (7: flight wind, Super Speed rush, Downed heartbeat, 4 city beds), Music 100160094926729 (patrol day 34 s, patrol night 46 s, boss 27 s loops and a 4 s victory sting, our own composition).
- 255 clips; 171 sounds, 66 of them with 2 to 4 variants (picked at random, no repeat); 177 rows checked by tests (every Config.Audio, Config.Life and music row plays an uploaded clip). Preview: art/preview/sounds.png (spectrogram and waveform of every clip, loudness and peak; none silent or clipped).
- Sources: Kenney's CC0 packs (Impact, Sci-Fi, RPG, Digital, Interface, UI), OpenGameArt/Freesound CC0 packs via EternityForest/Free-SFX (rubberduck, qubodup, Owlish, Superpowers, Electricity, PagDev, ShipsBells, JaggedStone), Meta's CC0 piano, plus our own synth layers (art/audio/dsp.py). Licences: Tools.md "Sounds".
- Still to do: voice packs (24 clips) and the mentor/guide voice lines (nate: voices later). Kenney's Voiceover Fighter pack (CC0, announcer lines) is downloaded-checked but not used: it is an announcer, not a hero voice.
Studio list (only ears and Studio can judge):
- [ ] Ears first: punch a robot 10 times: each hit a weighty thud with a clank, never the same twice in a row; a crit adds a bright ping; heavy hits are deeper. Defeat a robot: crunch, sparks, parts clatter.
- [ ] Every power path (17): cast, hit, charge and super each sound like the element (Volt crackle, Frost crystal, Fire roar, Earth rock, Tide splash, Blade slice, Gunslinger shots, Gadgeteer gizmo, Shadow dark whoosh, Titan/Juggernaut/Colossus heavy, Martial whips, Pulse wave, Mind phaser, Spirit chimes, Light laser). Say which feel weak and Claude remixes those clips.
- [ ] Movement: leap whoosh, heavy land thud, glide air, flight wind that rises in pitch with speed, boost burst, sonic boom with a short shake the first time a boost or a Super Speed sprint passes 95 studs/s (not again for 4 s), the Super Speed rush while running, ground pound slam, awning spring, splash into water, Acrobat dash, swing thwip and release.
- [ ] Destruction: a building collapse rumbles for about 5 s with cracking and a debris rain; craters crunch; ramming a wall smashes with glass; a car flip whomps and rattles. Under each big boom the city bed and the music dip for under a second (the duck), then come back.
- [ ] No sound starts late: the first punch after joining is heard (the atlases preload); no clip plays a neighbour's sound (a region bleeding) or cuts off early. If any does, tell Claude the sound's name.
- [ ] City bed: Downtown and Uptown traffic hum with a far siren now and then; the Docks water, bell, foghorn and gulls; open air in the outskirts; wind grows as the camera rises above roofs; silence underwater and in the pockets and HQ; beds crossfade over 3 s when crossing districts. No audible click where the loops wrap.
- [ ] Music: patrol day by day, night music at night, the boss theme near a live boss, the victory sting after a boss or a side quest chain, then back to patrol. Loops wrap without a click. Settings Music 0 silences it; Effects 0 silences every effect, bed and voice.
- [ ] UI: button taps vary slightly; level up, gear drop (higher by tier, Legendary twice), purchase, daily claim and quest steps chime in tune.
- [ ] Mix: a big fight with 5 heroes: loud but not distorted (the Effects compressor), no buzz from many hits at once; FrameProf AudioController and MoveAudio under 0.1 ms on a phone.

## Studio list: the directed prologue (2026-11-20, prologue-cine)
- [ ] From PLAY in the creator to the subway fight the hero never moves on its own input: WASD, the stick, Space, clicks and the HUD buttons do nothing (the HUD buttons are hidden); no gold marker shows on any beat.
- [ ] The run: after the scan the hero sprints off on its own (camera ahead, the crowd fleeing), a fleeing man charges: DODGE (Q, LB, or tap the prompt) rolls aside; a knocked-over barrier: JUMP (Space, A, tap) vaults it; the hero stops at the kid. The run never passes through a building (it turns the corner if the street bends).
- [ ] The Spark: SHIELD! (Vigil) or SMASH! (Syndicate) on the attack (click, RT, tap) fires it; the Ceremony's choice card still comes 1.2 s later with the crowd frozen, and the camera comes back after the pick.
- [ ] The leap: one JUMP, a slow-motion wide shot of the arc with the power's super flourish, a superhero landing on roof 2.
- [ ] The chase: a prompt per hop, DODGE on roofs 4 and 6 (the spear lands where the hero was), roof 5's hop falls short and GRAB (B, X, tap) catches the ledge and climbs; Skylance follows a roof back and cannot be hit.
- [ ] The suit: HOLD (hold Q, LB, or hold a finger on the prompt) fills the bar and the suit's pieces pop on as it fills; letting go keeps what is on; full: SUIT UP! and the drop prompt.
- [ ] The drop: DROP! sends the hero off the last roof to the subway steps (top shot), the fade, then the tunnel chase: DODGE a falling chunk, JUMP a debris pile, BREAK FREE (mash click, RT or tap) from a beam, the slabs crashing down behind, into the chamber: FIGHT!, the normal camera, the controls and the HUD come back.
- [ ] Miss on purpose: each prompt shows TRY AGAIN with a near miss (no damage), a longer window, and on the 3rd miss GOT IT! and the move happens; no beat ever waits forever (each moves on by its timer).
- [ ] The same on a phone (taps on the prompt, the hold with a finger, the mash by tapping fast) and a gamepad (A, LB, X, RT).
- [ ] A party member and a Journal REPLAY play the same (the replay's SKIP shows only on the ceremony, scan, rescue and title).
- [ ] The server never pulls the hero back (no MoveCorrect) on the runs, the leap, the hops, the climb or the drop; a respawn mid-beat puts the hero back and the beat goes on.
- [ ] Storyboard check against the real game: art/preview/prologue_street.png, prologue_roofs.png, prologue_tunnel.png (boxes stand in for the city's kit; framing only).
