## Studio test list: three new world bosses (bosses-3, 2026-11-20; DRAFT names and looks)
Proved headless (tests/bosses3_test.luau, ONLY="bosses-3"; plus the Boss counters and Boss anim suites over all seven bosses): six bosses rotate one every 20 to 30 minutes; each new one's arena is in the world (WorldMap.bossArena); the Colossus hovers, comes down every 24 s and at each phase, its 4 rods and up to 4 roof pads ride heroes up; its gust pushes at any height, bolts reach fliers, clouds linger; the Queen's 3 formations guard it (x0.45), all broken expose the core (x3, it crashes) for 15 s, then re-form; the Titan's slams dig craters and its lava burns the floor only; a melee-only party of each mobility (flight, speed, leap, acrobat, swing) beats each boss inside 8 minutes, takes real damage and spends 30 to 66% of its time hitting; a mixed bot squad wins each; 30 heroes keep events at 1.4 a second and a step under 0.01 ms; the swarm is 90 / 180 / 320 drone meshes by preset (reads as 540 / 1,380 / 2,120 with the haze); meshcheck: Bosses2 71 of 71 meshes turned 180 about y (MeshFix list). Only Studio can prove:
- [ ] Dev panel: spawn each new boss (StormColossus, SwarmQueen, QuarryTitan): the meshes load in the right places (no turned or floating piece; compare art/preview/boss_<Boss>.png and bosses2.png), the name card shows its portrait (sheet 2) and DRAFT title, the radar marker is its own icon.
- [ ] Storm Colossus over the plaza by Crane's Hero Hall: it flies down in, hovers ~110 up, its fan spins; every ~24 s and at 66% and 33% it comes down to a jump's reach (banner IT'S COMING DOWN!) and goes back up.
- [ ] Stand on a glowing lightning rod (ground) and a roof pad: the updraft throws you up beside its body (no MoveCorrect pull-back), you can hit it with melee before you fall; once every 2 s.
- [ ] Its lightning bolts fall from the sky onto the warned circles and hit a flier above them; the gust warns as a cone and pushes heroes away (also high fliers); storm clouds linger in the sky (phase 2) and crackle; lashes in phase 3; a hovering flier gets the Thunderhead column, a far one the ball lightning, a kiter the Vortex yank.
- [ ] Swarm Queen at the Docks container yard: hundreds of drones form two fists (phase 1), a wall (2) and a giant body (3); a punch, the rain and the stingers fly the drones out to where they land; break the 3 ground nodes: their drones drop, CORE EXPOSED!, it crashes down with its shells open, the core takes big hits; after 15 s THE SWARM RE-FORMS! and the nodes light up again.
- [ ] Phone (low preset): the swarm stays smooth (FrameProf WorldFx; 90 drones at 20 Hz); far away the drones hold still; no drone ever blocks a hero or a click (CanCollide, CanQuery off).
- [ ] Quarry Titan in the Old Quarry pit (never at the same time as the Maw): it walks and smashes, ground slams, the eruption and the boulder leap dig real craters (CraterService) with rock chunks and light, never blood; thrown rocks arc over during the warning and land on the circles; the lava floor (phase 2) glows on the ground and burns only heroes standing in it; embers rise off its cracks.
- [ ] Rewards on a win: Credits, gear through the boss loot rolls, its boss look in the drop (chest Hive Harness, legs Magma Greaves, feet Storm Jets, and their shiny), its Rogues File page (and gold page on a hard win) with its card, its badge (Storm Breaker, Hive Buster, Titan Toppler).
- [ ] A full rotation: after a boss, the next in the six-boss order comes 20 to 30 minutes later.

## Studio list: quieter defaults and powers that break walls (2026-11-20, list-combat)
1. New hero (or a save on the old 7/8 volumes): Settings shows Music 2 and Sound effects 5; the city sounds calmer. A save where you set other volumes keeps them.
2. Stand against a building and punch or slash it (M1): every hit puffs dust and chips off the wall; the finisher or the second hit knocks a panel out with a crunch.
3. A skill (Z, a power's cone or slash) at a wall opens two to four panels; a charged one more; a super a big hole. Robots in front of a wall still take the hit first.
4. A shot that misses a robot breaks the wall it hits (not a point inside the building).

## Studio list: swing trails and the weapon emblem (2026-11-20, list-combat)
1. Hold a sword (key 2) and M1: each swing leaves a thin white-to-steel slash along the blade, gone in a moment; walking with it held shows none.
2. Fists, gauntlets and the whip: the slash shows across the knuckles; a Round Shield bash trails along its face; blasters show none.
3. Creator WEAPON look: SWING TRAIL cycles only earned trails and names the next one's Mastery level; at Mastery 90 Frost shows the sparkle strip in blue; another player sees your trail.
4. EMBLEM on: your symbol shows on the blade's flat (both sides) or the shield's face and hides when the weapon is put away.

## Studio list: the intro film and the loading card (2026-11-20, list-combat)
Storyboard (the game's own camera on the test seed, stand-in figures): art/preview/intro_film_1.png (cold open) and intro_film_2.png (the world). Redraw: `lune run art/intro/storyboard.luau && $HOME/tools/bpyenv/bin/python art/blender/intro_storyboard.py && python3 art/intro/storyboard_sheet.py`.
- [ ] New account: the loading card shows the drifting skyline and the gold streak, and music plays at once (quietly); it fades as the game's music starts.
- [ ] The film starts straight after: the camera sweeps the street, Lodestar (no name plate, gold trail) flies down it with three Crane Troopers firing red bolts; he smashes into the tower's face and a real hole opens (wall pieces fly), then the roof bursts and he rockets out; one drone explodes; the landing cracks the street; CAPELINE slams in with a shake. Boss music for the cold open, night patrol after.
- [ ] The world beats: every figure is in shot and not inside a wall (the runner, the scanner's red cone, Crane on the roof, the three fakes landing, Radiant's metal half-face and red eye, Lodestar, the Syndicate crook, the drones rising, you in the crowd with the spark's glow). Comic captions top left, sound words pop in the world, the white flash, then the creator opens.
- [ ] SKIP shows after 1.5 s and goes straight to the creator; the Journal's REPLAY plays the film again then the prologue.
- [ ] The tower's hole heals after the film; no other player sees it. Phone: no long hitch when the cast is built (built a few a frame ahead of their shots).
- [ ] Captions marked DRAFT (two lines) and the Syndicate crook's look: nate to OK or reword.

## Studio list: fast early levels (2026-11-20, list-combat)
- [ ] A new hero after the prologue (level 3) clears Safehouse and reaches level 5 (new skill slot, the level-up toast) in about 8 minutes of mission; the MOBILITY tab offers rank 1 at 150 Credits right then.
- [ ] Renown toasts still show the base amount while the bar fills faster (the boost is applied on top): nate to say if the toast should show the boosted number.

## Studio list: retention (2026-11-20, list-combat)
- [ ] 10 and 5 minutes before a boss: the orange "RISES IN N MINUTES!" toast (and the bounty line on the week's bounty boss); none in the prologue, a mission or the creator.
- [ ] Beat the week's bounty boss: the result card shows WEEKLY BOUNTY! and the pay is doubled with 60 Dust; a second kill that week pays normally.
- [ ] Two friends on one server: one joins the boss, the other (out in the city) gets the friend toast once.
- [ ] Change the week (or edit stats.newsWeek in a test save): the NEW WEEK toast after the daily card; a brand-new save never sees it.
- [ ] Meet your nemesis a second and third time: the callback line names the meeting and 1 then 2 welded scars show on its chest.
- [ ] Journal > GOALS: the bars fill from your own save (level, HQ, mobility, Mastery, lore, boss MVPs); they look right on a phone.

## Studio list: two-hand grips, the guard shield, throwables (2026-11-20, list-combat)
1. Equip the Greatsword, Great Axe, Warhammer, Scythe, Spear and Bo Staff in turn: in the stance and every combo swing the left hand closes on the mesh's second grip (the hilt under the right hand, or ahead on the shaft), not a hand's width off it (HeroAnim.twoHandGap from Config.WeaponArt grip2).
2. Hold Block: an energy shield with a glowing eight-sided rim snaps up in front of the chest (not a flat disc), kicks and flashes when a hit lands on it, and your own shield's rim runs from white-blue to red as your guard drains and flickers near empty. Others' shields look the same at full rim. Let go: it is gone at once.
3. Walk past benches, bins, lamp posts and vending machines you can pick up: they look like the street kit's own props (no plain box shows); mailboxes are blue city mailboxes, signs are yellow diamonds on a post, dumpsters have a lid and wheels, slabs show rebar. Pick one up, carry and throw it: the look stays on it all the way and lands with it; clicks and hits still work as before.
4. Juggernaut: cast Girder Swing: a red steel I-beam appears in the right hand, swings with the arm through the arc, and shrinks away after about a second; others see it on your hero too.
5. Brawler: Lift and Throw with nothing to lift: the thrown crate is the street kit's wooden crate mesh in the air, and on landing it gives way to flying planks and wood dust.
6. Gunslinger: cast Bullet Time: a quick white flash, then your screen goes warm sepia with tinted edges for the field's 2 seconds and comes back; nobody else's screen changes; casting again inside it keeps it until the later one ends.
7. Colossus: cast Grow, then Titan Form: your hero gets a lit outline in its path colour and bursts out with dust and a thud; walking, every footfall puffs dust with a low thud and shakes nearby cameras (stronger close by); as a titan, benches, bins and lamps underfoot go flying. Others see the same on you. The outline goes when the form ends.
8. Mind: ride Psi Platform: a concrete slab under your feet with a wooden crate and a blue barrel riding two corners (the street kit's meshes); all of it fades when the ride ends, nothing is left floating or falling.
9. Wear a cape (each of the 9 styles in turn): standing it hangs and breathes a little; running it lifts behind you, sprinting, falling or flying it streams out nearly flat and ripples; strafing swings it to the side; it never pokes through the body when you stop. The yoke, clasps and collar stay on the shoulders. Watch another player's cape too (it sways on your screen as well). A look change mid-run still sways.
10. On a phone (landscape) beat or outlast a world boss: the result card sits under the POWERS / GEAR / MAP row, never over it, shrinks to fit when it has many lines (bounty, drops, style, MVPs), and still closes with a tap; on PC it shows at full size under the row.
11. Join: the loading card shows the drawn CAPELINE logo (gold block letters, navy 3D edge, red cape sweep) where the typed title was, tilted like before; on a slow connection the typed title shows until the image arrives. The main menu (returning players) shows the same logo at the top of its panel. (asset 138065040749947, approved; DRAFT look until nate OKs it.)
12. Open MENU: a HOW TO PLAY button is in the drawer. It opens a panel with MOVE, FIGHT, BLOCK, POWERS, GEAR and HERO PASS tabs down the left; each tab shows its title and 3-4 lines that match what the game does; X and the usual close keys close it; on a phone it fits the screen. (DRAFT wording, Config.Ui.help.)
13. Flip a car with a heavy hit: where it lands, textured dust, sparks and dark chips burst (no plain grey ball or neon sticks); when it resets after 30 s, a glass sparkle and a little dust.
14. PC: open SETTINGS: under KEYS (PC) each action (Leap, Fly, Boost, Down, Block, Skills 1-4, Super, Awaken, Hold power, Hold weapon, Gadget, Emotes) shows its key. Tap Leap, press E: Leap is on E (Space does nothing now) and the HUD's key hint says E. Bind Fly to Space: it swaps (Leap's old key goes to Fly's place as the note says). Try M: refused (MAP's key). Escape cancels. Rejoin on another PC: the keys follow the account. RESET KEYS puts every key back. Touch and gamepad players see no KEYS section.
15. Open MENU: an INVITE FRIENDS button is in the drawer; tap it: Roblox's own invite-friends window opens (PC, phone, console); pick a friend and they get the invite. Where invites are off it reads INVITES ARE OFF HERE for a moment, nothing breaks.
16. Weather: in Studio set the Workspace attribute WeatherForce to rain, then storm, fog, overcast and clear (clear it after to go back to the timed weather). Rain: thin streaks fall all around the camera (not a box edge in sight while walking, flying or boosting: the box leads you), rings splash on the street or the roof under you (medium and high presets), the sky hazes grey-blue and the clouds close in; storm: heavier rain, a jagged white bolt far off with the screen lit twice and the thunder rumbling a second or three later; fog: a thick pale bank you lose the far towers in; clear: back to the usual warm sky. Fades take about 25 s. Dive underwater, enter the HQ or a mission pocket: no rain there and the look relaxes. Check phones (low preset: lighter rain, no splashes) keep their frame rate (FrameProf "WeatherFx"). Two clients see the same weather and the same flashes.
17. Set pieces move: in the toy shop pocket the train runs round its ring and the music box dancer turns and bobs; at the Museum Gala the chandelier sways gently under the canopy and still falls when it breaks (no snapping back up); in the Pit the fans on the benches hop and cheer, out of step with each other; on "Cat on a crane" the cat swishes its tail and looks left and right, and rides your back normally once picked up. Nothing jitters when you walk away and come back.
18. Power looks (Art.Powers): Gadgeteer: Turret Drop stands a tripod turret with twin barrels and a lit strip in your path colour on the ground; Fix-It Drone is a white quad-rotor drone with a claw circling you; the Hoverboard is a dark deck with glow strips and rear jets under your feet that turns with you; the Mech Suit puts a chest plate with a glowing core and a thruster pack, arm sleeves and leg greaves on your body (sized to the bigger body, gone when it ends, back after a respawn). Spirit: Guardian Jab and the Ascended Guardian show a see-through spirit giant with a horn behind you; Spirit Ride is a see-through spirit wolf you stand on, head first. Earth: Rock Ride is a rough rock slab with glowing veins; Stone Pillar is a cluster of basalt columns rising under you; Overgrowth grows thorny vines with a flower at each tip. Tide: the Tsunami is a curling wave with a foam lip rolling down the line, as wide as the line. Ward Totem: a carved post with a glowing star; Shock Mines: six squat discs with lights. Repair drones over a broken building look like the Fix-It Drone. None stand upside down or backwards; none block a hit or a walk.
19. Collapse a building: its rubble is heaps of broken floor slabs and concrete lumps with rusty rebar sticking out (no plain tilted boxes), in the building's colour and concrete, the same size and place as before; you can walk and land on the heaps; the repair drones clear them as before.
20. Weapon Rack: for Bare Fists, Martial Arts, Power Gauntlets, Strikers, Baton, Bo Staff, Sai, Tonfa, Nunchucks, Tower Shield, Blaster Pistol, Twin Blasters, Blaster Rifle and Arm Cannon pick the 2nd and 3rd style: the weapon's shape changes on the hero (plates, studs, hooks, a stub or heavy barrel, a slim or flared cannon), nothing floats off the hand, and the Energy style still glows.
21. Nemesis armour (Art.Powers look Nemesis): DEV spawn a nemesis with the horned head, a cape and every trait (DEV > Nemesis, or meet one in the city). Its shoulders are riveted pads, the horns curved and pointing up and out, the cape hangs at its back with folds and a ragged hem in its main colour, the front plates are riveted diamond plate, the second cannon sits on its right shoulder pointing ahead (not sideways), the antenna stands on its head with a glowing tip. A bulky or spiked nemesis has wider shoulders, cape and plates. Nothing floats off the Brute Bot or sinks into it; the cloak still hides every piece; the scars and the weak point still show. Remove ReplicatedStorage.Art.Powers.Nemesis meshes: the old parts come back, no errors.
22. Weapon textures (Art.Weapons re-uploaded with baked maps): at the Weapon Rack try the Sword, Greatsword, Hammer, Bo Staff and a blaster in Steel and Gold: blades show fine brushed streaks and darker creases at the guard, grips show their wrap or grain, the colour you picked still paints every piece (nothing turns grey or white). Wood, Obsidian and Crystal blades look as before (their own material); the Energy style and the glow pieces still glow flat. Phone: no frame drop when 10 heroes hold weapons. With Config.Weapons.textures = false the weapons are flat colours again.
23. Power effects pass (CombatFx): Storm's Bolt Ride: a jagged white-hot bolt with two short forks, crackle along it, sparks where you leave and land and a glowing scorch on the ground, over the old streak (other Storm moves look as before). Flame Jets pours flame and embers down out of both jets and Light Rail sheds sparks off its back (no more balls dropping every frame). Singularity: sparks pulled into the hole, a swirl and a pull pulsing over it, a flash and shock ring as it ends. Heavenfall Beam: ki particles along the beam and a heavy hit where it lands. Sunfall: embers and flame shed by the sun as it forms and falls, a fire burst on landing. Check each on a phone: no frame drop beyond today's.
24. Billboard ads (art/city/ads.py, art/preview/city_ads.png; 8 approved images in Config.City.kit.play.billboard.ads): fly over Downtown and Uptown roofs: every rooftop billboard shows a printed comic ad (Zap! Cola, Crane News Network, Brightharbor Bank, Mega Donuts, Harbor Ferry, Radio Hill 99.1, Turbo Kicks, Cosmo Pizza) on the street side, the right way round (words readable, not mirrored), filling the panel inside the frame; the boards differ across the city. The rooftop row's billboard (east of the Hall) shows one too, and its AC units are the kit's metal units. Wreck a building with a billboard: the ad falls or hides with the board, never floats. If an ad shows mirrored or on the back, flip the face in CityBuilder adOn.
25. Vehicle lettering (Shared.VehicleKit.LETTERS, SetPieceMeshes): Downtown's taxis read TAXI on both front doors; the Docks' trucks HARBOR FREIGHT on both container sides; the escorts: the Halo Academy bus HALO ACADEMY under its windows, Scoops's truck SCOOPS, Nora's van LIVE NEWS 9, the ferry van GULL POINT FERRY, Ambrose's van AMBROSE THE ASTONISHING inside its bulb frame, Tess's truck TESS PARTS on the cab doors. Words sit flat on the side (not floating off it or sunk in), read the right way on both sides, and move, flip and reset with the vehicle (escorts driving, a flipped car).
26. Citizens chat (Shared.CitizenSim, Config.Destruction.citizens.chat): stand on a Downtown corner a couple of minutes: now and then a walker stops a few studs from someone standing, the two turn to face each other and gesture (talk, shrug, look round) for 4 to 9 s, then walk on; nobody stands inside another; a fight nearby still sends both running.
27. Photo Mode backdrops (art/ui/photo_backdrops.py, art/preview/photo_backdrops.png; Config.Ui.photoBackdrops): open Photo Mode (N), press LOOK through each: COMIC a printed panel border with halftone corners and a yellow burst behind your name caption; SPEED inked speed lines from the edges; SUNSET a warm glare top right with lens rings; NOIR black bars, rain streaks, grain and a dark vignette over the grey grade; NEON glowing pink and cyan edge bars and a grid at the bottom. The hero in the middle stays clear; on a phone the pictures fill the screen without stretching oddly; NONE removes everything.
28. Robot gear and foe props (Art.Foes): DEV spawn a Shield Bearer (curved riot plate with a rim and ridge, window and stripe still on it), a Captain (peaked cap with piping and a curved brim), a rifle robot (chunky blaster with a scope, the aim laser still from its tip), a grenadier (stitched strap with a buckle, three round grenades, a satchel), a medic (backpack with pockets and a handle, the green plus and light on its back), a jet robot (swept wings with ribs, glowing tips), a hook launcher robot (drum launcher, claw hub, curved fingers). In the side quests: the toys' brass key, the Salt Dogs' hooks, Captain Brine's anchor, the Tidewright's trident, Skylance's spear. Nothing floats off its hand, arm, back or head; the riot shield still knocks aside when the guard breaks; pieces break off the shell as before.
29. Bulwark's battle form (Art.Bosses re-uploaded, art/preview/boss_NullWarFrame.png): play Live (or DEV the War-Frame): the mech is Bulwark's blue with silver and electric yellow trim, a six-sided Hex Badge lamp on the chest, wide layered pauldrons under the cannons and a tall tower shield on its left forearm that swings with the arm in every pose (stomps, swipes, the land). The entrance nova and name card slash are electric yellow. At phase 3 the pauldron lames break off with the other plates. Check the shield does not cut through the left leg in any pose.
30. Collection finds (Art.Finds, art/preview/finds.png): walk Downtown's streets with the HQ Scanner until a Comic Cover sparkles: it is a red comic book standing open (star bursts on its covers) bobbing and turning slowly over a soft red halo, with the sparkles, light and chime. Check one of each world set: Lost Toys (a tin rocket with a key), Ship Bells (a brass bell in a yoke), Deep Relics (a barnacled diving helmet), Roof Pigeons (a pigeon figurine on a plinth), Star Charms in the outskirts (a glowing star on a loop). Picking one up still pops the juice.
31. Contest stage (Art.Stage, art/preview/contest_stage.png): go to the plaza corner stage: a rounded purple deck with a glowing gold lip and gold stars round its skirt, a silver truss arch at the back with a red marquee board lettered COSTUME CONTEST in comic letters ringed by glowing bulbs, two spotlight cans on top; 8 glowing star pads and gold, silver and bronze podium blocks with star plates. Walk on the deck, pads and podium (you stand on them as before; the arch does not block). Enter a contest: in your spotlight turn a soft beam reaches from the nearer can to you; it goes when the next contestant's turn starts.
32. Site props (Art.SiteProps, art/preview/siteprops.png): talk to any side quest giver: the marker over their head is a chunky gold comic "!" (round, it reads from every side). Find Patch's relay (The Scoop's giver): a steel post on a bolted foot, a rugged radio case with a handle, side grilles and two dials, its glowing screen, a coiled aerial with the blinking lamp. Trigger CatOnCrane (DEV incident): the cat is a ginger loaf with a round head, ears and a tail; carry it: every piece rides on your back.
33. Loading preload (Config.Ui.preload): join on a phone with an empty cache: after LOADING YOUR HERO the card may read LOADING THE CITY for up to 3 s; then the hero's body, the troopers, the street props, the prologue stage and the ability icons show at once with no pop-in. Join to control stays under 10 s (spec).
34. Client errors (Everything Left 480): in a published test server, break something on purpose on one device (a Dev command that errors, if any) and a day later check the Creator Dashboard's custom events: client_error with the script's name and device, and session_errors (clean yes/no) for every session.
35. Save tools (Services.SaveAdmin; Launch Checklist "Player data requests"): in a published test place with a spare account: play, then from the server command bar erase(<its id>): the account is kicked, rejoining starts a new hero, its board rows are gone. Play again, note the time, change something, leave, rollback(<id>, <time>): rejoining shows the hero as it was before.
36. PvP rings (Art.Rings, art/preview/rings.png): turn the PvP flag on as a Vigil hero: a glowing blue round band with inward chevrons grows in under your feet and slowly turns; as a Syndicate hero a red notched square, turned 45 degrees. While spawn protection lasts it is faint. Go Downed: a grey dashed ring with four plus marks pulses and turns under you.
37. Block looks (Art.Guard, art/preview/guard.png): hold BLOCK: a blue domed shield with a raised hexagon and an eight-sided rim snaps up in front of the chest, its rim reddens and flickers as Guard drains, and it kicks on each blocked hit. Let a BruteBot block: a faceted blue bubble turns slowly round it; when it drops a few shards fly off. Perfect-block a hit: a yellow starburst faces the camera and spins out; get shattered: the starburst plus eight crystal shards. Check the shield does not hide your hero's face from the default camera and reads on phones.
38. Juice (Art.Juice, Config.Ui.juiceArt; art/preview/juice.png, juice_ui.png): defeat a robot: six gold coins with a sparkle flip out and drop. Rescue a citizen: a green ring with outward ticks spreads and turns. Pick up a Legendary drop: a twisting column of light in the tier colour over a ring. Beat a boss: spiky bursts in the sky over rings, and paper confetti (curls, stars, dots, triangles) falls. Respawn: a turning geodesic bubble round you for 5 s. Get stunned in PvP: three gold stars circle your head. Clear an incident: the stamp has a worn ink double frame in the medal colour; level up: the gold medallion with ribbons spins in under the number. With Reduce flashing on nothing spins.
39. WANTED poster and heads (Art.NemParts, Config.Hq.wanted.paper): in your HQ with a nemesis (DEV > Nemesis 1) the poster is an aged, torn, folded bill with an inked border, the halftone band behind WANTED and a ruled frame round the bust; the wood or steel board shows past the torn edge and the bottom-right corner. Beat nemeses of each head kind (DEV) and check the trophy heads: glowing lens eyes, a glass dome with metal ribs, a cyclops eye in a metal hood, a ribbed neck on the bust, chevron chest plates, a shoulder cannon with cooling rings pointing forward, a green heal drone in a white ring, a segmented antenna. B (Read) still opens the WANTED panel.
40. Crater card (Config.NewsArt.cards.places): walk into the Crater for the first time this session: the name card THE CRATER / WHERE THE OLD HEROES FELL shows a picture on its left: the broken white lab wall with the Crane C, a cracked glowing tank, the gold helmet, the blue shield and the red cape over a teal sunburst, inked like the boss cards.
41. Throwables (Art.Throwables): on a Downtown sidewalk find a mailbox (blue, rounded top, white band, slot, feet), a warning sign (a yellow diamond with a black border and a bold "!" on a steel post), a dumpster (green, ribbed, a split dark lid, casters), a broken concrete slab (rebar out of its broken end, a crack) and a rubble heap. Each sits on the ground where its box was (no floating, no sinking), is picked up, carried and thrown with its look on, and comes back with it.
42. Shield Drone (Art.Gadget): use the Shield Drone gadget: a white rounded pod with motor pods and a glowing lens circles you facing you, a dark four-blade rotor spins over it (not on low), and a turning blue geodesic bubble surrounds you until the shield ends or breaks, then a pop of sparks.
43. Rope links (Art.Tether): swing the Chain Whip: the line is a chain of interlocking metal links, not plain bars, and lashes and reels as before; the Rope Dart's line is a twisted three-strand rope and the Yo-yo's a thin twisted string, each in its weapon's own colour. No gaps or kinks at the joints when the line is straight or curled.
44. Farm truck wheels (Art.SetPieces FarmTruckWheel*): start The Harvest King's escort step: as the red farm truck drives down the farm road, its four treaded tyres roll at the road speed and the front pair turns into the bends; they stop when it stops. Every other set piece looks as before (the SetPieces model was re-uploaded).
45. Auras and mentor looks (Config.Mentors look tex, Fx.MentorFx, Fx.AuraFx): finish Captain Meridian's quest line: the gold trail is a bright comet strip, not a flat ribbon; The Margrave's violet trail rolls as smoke; Old Beacon's lantern glow and The Velvet Paw's spark crackle are soft glow motes and spinning sparks, not grey squares; a finished badge group's accolade aura is glow motes; the reward moment showers the look's own motes. Awaken: gold (Anime: white) sparks rise off the hero with a gold ring now and then; Glacier Armor's shield is the blue geodesic bubble (Art.Guard), not a plain ball.
46. Charge glow (Fx.ChargeFx, Config.Combat.charge.motes): with a second player, have them hold a charge-up power: their outline glows in their colour as before, a quick implosion pulls in at the start, their path's own motes gather into them closer and closer together as it fills, and the full charge pops a glow and a flat shock ring. Yours: the implosion at the start and the pop when full, the motes orbiting your hand as before (AimPreview), never doubled.
47. Aim preview (Config.Ui.aimArt, Fx.AimPreview): hold a nova skill: a glowing drawn ring with ticks and four inward arrows lies flat on the ground round you in the power's colour (no neon disc); a field skill puts it at the aim point. Hold a bolt or dash: a strip of glowing chevrons runs from you along the aim and scrolls outward, pointing away from you (if the chevrons point back at you, set aimArt.turn = true; if they scroll inward, make aimArt.scroll negative). Hold a cone: five chevron rays fan out. All brighten as the charge fills and pulse white at full; nothing shows on other players' screens.
48. Prologue crowd (CitizenRenderer.dressRig, Config.Story.opening.plaza.crowd.seed): play the Ceremony prologue on a new hero: the crowd before the Hall wear Roblox shirts and pants in many colours, catalog faces and catalog hair, like the city's citizens (no flat-coloured blocky people); the same crowd on a second device. They still cheer and run off. The intro film's citizens look the same way. On the phone preset (14 people) the frame holds.
49. Side style crests (Art.SideGear, LookService sideCrest): with Vigil reputation tiers 1 to 5 buy and wear Vigil Patrol Visor, Lantern Vest, Watchman Wraps, Rooftop Boots, Safehouse Cape, Guardian Longcape, Vigil Buckle and City Sash: each is its slot's shape in its colour with a small gold shield and glowing star on the front (the visor's brow, the vest's left chest, the outer side of each hand and boot, the buckle, the sash) and a big one on the cape's back; on a Syndicate hero the Syndicate styles show a steel crowned diamond with two glowing fangs instead. No crest floats off or sinks in on the slim, broad and tall builds, and none shows on loot styles.
50. Rides (Art.Powers FlameJets, LightRail, PsiSlab, Wave): use each travel power: Flame Jets puts two steel thrusters with glowing throats under your feet with flame pouring out of them; Light Rail a glowing spine on faint hard-light ties running ahead of you; Psi Platform a torn street slab (asphalt, a yellow lane stripe, bent rebar, a faint ring under it) with the crate and barrel on its corners; Wave Surf a small breaking wave just behind and under you (if it looks backwards or buries you, its offset is in CombatFx.ride). Each turns with your heading and fades at the end. The other summons and rides look as before (the Powers model was re-uploaded).
51. Travel styles (Config.TravelStyles.art, Fx.TravelStyleFx): wear one style of each burst kind (Dev: grant styles): the sparks styles trail a sparkle strip and throw spinning star-sparks, puff styles a smoke roll and soft smoke clouds, ring styles a neon strip and a flat shock ring on take-off and landing, petals a ribbon and leaves, ink a dark streak and ink splats, prism a twin strip and stars in rainbow colours; none shows grey squares. A second player sees the same.
52. Rooftop row (Art.Roof): play the prologue's rooftop chase or fly along Downtown's rooftop row: the water tanks are wooden barrels with steel hoops, a ladder and a cone roof on a steel stand (standing on the stand, not sunk or floating); the hatches are concrete housings with a green door; the skylights are pitched glass with dark frames; the washing hangs on its line between two poles (a red shirt, a white sheet, a blue striped towel) and you pass through it. Landing spots in the middle of each roof are still clear; the tanks and hatches block as before.
81. Bomber burning patch (CombatFx.zone): let a Bomber's grenade land; the burning patch now has Ember Ring's ring of flame tongues round its edge, turning slowly, over the glowing disc with flames and embers; it fades with the patch.
80. Medic Unit repair beam (CombatFx.mend): fight a Medic Unit that mends a robot; the beam is a flowing green energy strip from the Medic to its patient (texture moving along, a slight pulse), with repair packets running along it; both ends follow as they move; stunning the Medic cuts it and nothing is left hanging.
79. Repair drone beam (BuildingFx droneJob): wreck a building and wait for the repair drones; each hovering drone shines a flowing cyan energy beam down to the roof (texture moving downward, wider at the roof) with small weld sparks where it lands, on only while it works; the beam follows the drone as it circles and goes when it leaves.
78. Shatter hits (AbilityFx HIT.shatter): hit with Glacier Armor; chipped glassy stones (the Blender rock in glass, path-tinted) burst out, tumble and fall, instead of little cubes.
77. Halo and spin hoops (CombatFx hoop, AbilityFx FLAIR.halo and spin): cast Thunder Drop or Sunfall (a halo over the head rises, widens and turns) and Overcharge or Glacier Armor (two tipped hoops round the body widen and swing opposite ways); each is a hollow crested ring, not a filled flat disc, in the path's colours.
76. Crater hits (AbilityFx HIT.crater): hit robots with Stone Pillar, Thunder Drop and Flashpoint on a street and on grass; under the hit a dark scorch mark lies flat on the ground (the scorch sheet, not a grey disc) with stones thrown out, and it fades within about 2 s.
75. Standing rings (CombatFx disc, Art.Powers ShockRing): land Martial hits (the ki ring faces the camera), Fire, Earth, Tide and Frost hits, a Wake trail ability and a crater hit; every ring that was a flat neon disc is now the crested shockwave ring, standing across its direction (toward the camera for the ki ring) or flat on the ground, growing and fading; none appears edge-on by mistake.
74. Robot shot streaks (CombatFx shotTrail, Config.Fx.shot trail/life): get shot by a Blaster, a Sniper and a famous fake; each shot leaves a short glowing energy-strip streak behind it (red for robots, the path colour for fakes) that reads from the side and from straight ahead, so you can see it coming and dodge; no streak hangs in the air after the shot ends; check frame time in a 10-robot fight on a phone.
73. Prologue run barrier (PrologueFx coverBarrier, Art.Rubble Barrier): in the Ceremony run's JUMP beat the barrier is a yellow-and-black striped sawhorse barricade with steel legs and an orange lamp, standing knocked askew across the street, feet on the ground (not sunk or floating); the hero vaults it; it reads at the run's camera distance.
72. Prologue tunnel art (PrologueFx coverChunk, coverGirder): play the Ceremony prologue to the subway; each falling roof chunk is a chipped concrete block (not a box) that drops with its tween and crumbles; the beam that pins the hero is the riveted red steel girder lying across the shoulders (its length across, not stuck upright), and on the heave it tumbles away down the tunnel and fades with its part.
71. Hero copies (CombatFx ghostPieces): cast Shadow Clones, Holo Decoy, Tide Mirror and Spirit Swap with a dressed hero (suit, cape, catalog hair, a weapon); the copies are your own hero in the path's colour, glowing and see-through, two clones either side facing your way and following you; a second player sees your copies, not theirs; check frame time with 2 clones on a phone (48 parts a copy at most).
70. Crater rims as broken blocks (GroundFx, Art.Rubble Chunk1-3): land a super on a road, a plaza and the park grass; the crater's rim is a ring of broken, chipped concrete blocks in the ground's colour and material (not clean boxes), none floating or turned on edge oddly, and they still sink back into the ground over the last minute.
69. Girder Swing's Blender girder (Art.Powers Girder, CombatFx.heldGirder): as a Juggernaut cast Girder Swing; the girder in the right hand is a riveted red I-beam with a torn, bent far end, hangs straight down from the fist (not floating beside it), swings with the arm and shrinks away; check it reads at camera distance and is not sideways or inside the body.
68. Comic hit star and spark spray (Config.Fx.lib hitstar, hitspark; art/fx/hitstar.py): a weighty hit sprays textured spark streaks out of the robot along the hit (twice as many, plus two long hot streaks, on heavy hits), not little white bars. In Comic style, every hit pops a jagged comic starburst (and a nova, burst or slam a big pale one over its dome) with a black ink rim in the hit colour (gold on a crit), bigger on your own hits, gone in a blink, over the flat ring; Anime style keeps its white flash and speed lines. Check the ink rim stays black in daylight and at night, and a big crowd fight on a phone stays smooth.
67. Travelling lines (CombatFx.line): Crescent Wave and the other blade lines send glowing crescents (the Swordsman's Blender crescent, tilted a little in Comic) racing down the line; Ground Spark and the other ground lines jut glowing crystal spikes up out of the ground one after another. Check the crescents bulge forward (not back toward you) and the spikes stand straight up on the ground.
66. Flying heads (Art.Powers Heads): Light bolts fly as a fluted spear of light with a diamond tip, Mind bolts as a face-on psi ring with three teeth turning as it flies, Frost bolts as faceted ice crystals, Fire as fireballs trailing flame tongues, Earth as chipped stones (Colossus as big ones), Shadow as kunai with a ring at the back, Tide as teardrop water globs; each points nose first along its flight, in the path colour (the kunai in Shadow's dark accent). Check none flies backward or sideways (the import turn), Martial's ki orb is still a round glowing ball, and the Fire puffs still swell round.
65. Broken chunks (Art.Rubble Chunk1-3, Config.Destruction.structure.bits): blast a Downtown building: knocked-out pillars and floor slabs tumble as broken concrete blocks with chipped corners in the building's colour and concrete (not clean boxes), and each knocked-out wall panel flies with its windows plus two small broken bits off its torn edges that spin faster. Check the chunks keep the building's texture, sit where the piece was the moment it breaks (no jump), and fade after 5 s; Shift+F5 with a big collapse: frame time like before.
64. Energy bolts and held beams (Config.Fx.energyBeam, art/fx/energy_beam.py): any path's bolt (Fire, Ice, Cosmic and the rest) streaks as a glowing rippling energy band with racing streaks in the path colour round a thin white core, gone in a blink; a held beam (a channel) is a wider swelling energy column whose ripples race out toward the target and follows it as you aim. Check both face the camera from the side, look right in Anime style, and leave nothing behind.
63. Storm lightning (Config.Fx.lightning, art/fx/lightning.py): Zap Bolt, Chain Lightning between targets and Bolt Ride each draw a jagged glowing lightning bolt with forks in the path colour (not a straight neon bar), flickering between its four drawings while it fades in a blink; Zap Bolt keeps a thin white core. Check it faces the camera from the side and from above, and nothing is left hanging after it fades.
62. Static Field (Storm, Art.Powers VoltRim): cast it on open ground: the field's edge is a thin glowing ring with jagged lightning arcs leaping up off it in the path colour, jumping to new spots and flickering a few times a second like live current (not a smooth spin); it fades with the field. Check the arcs stand on the ground at the field's edge.
61. Bench sitters (Config.Destruction.citizens.sit): stand on a Downtown or Old Town sidewalk a few minutes near a bench: now and then a walker steps over to it, sits (thighs on the seat, feet on the ground, hands in the lap, facing out of the bench, not into its back) for 8 to 20 s, then stands and walks on; two can share a bench, never one seat; a blast nearby makes them jump up and run. Also check the park's picnic pairs: legs out in front of them on the blanket (they bent backward before).
60. Tidal surge waves (Art.Powers Wave on Fx.EventFx): during a Tidal Surge event, stand on the Docks: each roller coming in off the water is a curling breaking wave with a white foam crest (the Tsunami's meshes), facing the shore, rising and falling as it rolls; none are flat blue cylinders. Check the crest leans toward land and the waves sit on the water, not under it.
59. Shock rings and cone blasts (Art.Powers Shock): any burst, nova, slam or landing rings out as a hollow glowing ring with a rounded crest (not a flat disc) that grows and fades on the ground; a cone power (Frost Breath, Flamethrower, Push Wave and the like, Comic style) throws a flared fan of light with white streaks out to its reach. Anime cones keep their white rays and speed lines. Check rings sit on the floor, not in it.
58. Window shopping (Config.Destruction.citizens.shop): stand on a Downtown or Old Town sidewalk a few minutes: now and then a walker stops side-on and faces the shop fronts for a few seconds; some then vanish into the shop and come back out at the same spot carrying a coloured paper shopping bag in the right hand (folded top, two rope handles) for half a minute. Check the bag hangs from the hand while walking (Roblox's walk clip near the camera and the code walk further out) and is gone on pooled bodies. A collapse nearby still sends lookers running.
57. Sword crescents (Art.Powers Slash): as a Swordsman (or with any sword weapon), each combo swing draws one glowing crescent in the path colour with a white edge that sweeps through the swing and fades in about a fifth of a second, tilted per combo step (Rising Blade stands it up); Heaven Splitter flies a giant upright crescent down the line. Anime style: a white blade with a coloured edge. Punches keep their fist flash.
56. Power looks (Art.Powers Sun, BlackHole, KiRing, EmberRim, HaloRim): Fire Sunfall: a glowing lumpy sun in a spiked flame corona forms high up and turns as it drops; Pulse Singularity: a dark core in a spinning spiral disc with a bright photon ring; Martial Heavenfall Beam: the rings along the beam are ki rings with flames trailing back; Ki Blast's halo is the same ki ring; Ember Ring's edge is a slowly turning ring of flame tongues and Halo Blades' edge a fast-turning circle of light blades. Check sizes read at phone and PC distance and that Anime style turns them white.
55. Legs overlays (Config.Creator.legsArt): in the creator's Legs tab try Jeans (gold stitching), Ripped Jeans (skin through the rips), Track Pants (accent stripes), Shorts (bare shins), Checkered Pants and Battle Skirt: each shows its drawn Pants over the leg colours, tinted by the hero's picks; the skirt, hakama, greaves and knee pads keep their 3D parts over it; with a suit on, the suit's own pants step aside. Check the Legs tab's tiles match.
54. Gear style pictures (Config.Ui.styleTiles): GEAR > STYLES, each slot tab: every style button shows its look as a small comic card at the left with its name beside it; locked styles' cards are dimmed; tapping still applies or explains as before. Check the pictures match the look shown on your hero.
53. Gunslinger bullet (Art.Powers Bullet): pick Gunslinger and fire at a robot: each shot is a small copper bullet with a glowing tracer streak behind it in the path's accent colour (white in Anime), not a glowing bar, flying straight and fading at the hit; High Noon's fan of shots too. The other paths' shots look as before (Powers was re-uploaded again as 119146000962620).
